There is no blocker-ordering step (Foundations removed damage assignment order): after blocks the active player simply gets priority. `legalDefenders(attacker)` returns every non-eliminated opponent plus their planeswalkers. `advance()`/`advanceUntil(pred)` run `tick()`. `emit()` appends an event and runs `detectTriggers` (matches against every battlefield permanent's `triggered` list — and a command-zone card's Eminence abilities, and a graveyard card's `fromGraveyard` ones, a suspended card's `whileSuspended` ones (702.62b), rule 113.6k, found through the per-name `graveyardTriggerNames` cache — honouring intervening-ifs via `interveningIfMet`). `prepareForPriority(player)` is the pre-priority fixpoint loop: run SBAs (not while a resolution is suspended), drain pending mass-effect/sacrifice queues, resume a suspended resolution, place pending triggers (APNAP order) — repeat until stable, then hand off priority (to `awaiting.player` if a decision got raised mid-loop). A sacrifice *cost* of several permanents (an ability’s `{filter, count}` or `{each}`, a spell’s `additionalCost.sacrificeCount` or `flashback.sacrifice`) is paid after the rest of its cost, the spell or ability already on the stack (rules 601.2h, 602.2b — an `abilityCostModification` reaching an ability is `abilityCostModificationReaches`: its kind — equip, exhaust, cycling from the hand, loyalty — `byYou` and source filter; a `targetingSource` one is settled by the chosen targets, the best single target assumed while offered and `targetingSourceOptions` narrowing the offer to affordable targets; `loyaltyCostIncrease` folds an "additional [+1]" into the loyalty cost, 606.5, for the 606.6 check, the −X ceiling, the payment and the offer's `loyalty`): `beginCostSacrifice` records `GameState.pendingCostSacrifice` and asks each part with the ordinary `sacrifice` decision (`continueCostSacrifice` — only what leaves the later parts payable, nothing asked where exactly enough is there), `applySacrifice` routes the answer there rather than to the edict queue, and `finishCostSacrifice` sacrifices every pick as one leave batch. The mana is planned with what could pay the sacrifice tried last, and a Treasure the plan sacrifices for mana doesn’t count towards it (`costSacrificeRemaining`, `spellSacrificeParts`). Mana is **auto-paid**: `manaSources` reports each permanent's `{T}: Add` abilities (plus an untapped once-a-turn one — Vivi Ornitier's `{0}: Add` — and an untapped one with a mana cost, once per payment: Llanowar Envoy's `{1}{G}`) as `ManaOption` alternatives, a mana cost of generic, coloured, `{C}` or two-colour hybrid pips included (a converter, however much it makes for its cost — Prismatic Lens, Ceta Disciple; one sacrificing itself only when it makes more) — a land's intrinsic ones too (rule 305.6): `effectiveActivated` lists printed abilities, then one for each basic land type the land has that no printed stand-in covers (`extraIntrinsicManaColors` — a type an effect gave it), then granted ones, and a typed land's printed `{T}: Add` stand-in lapses with its type (`inactiveStandIn`, rule 305.7), sizing a live `amount` (a power, a turn stat) against the board as it stands and compressing "X in any combination of" into one `anyColorOf` option rather than X+1 splits, resolving a list read off the board (`manaOneOf` — rule 106.7's "could produce" is `couldProduce`, which asks through other such lands, never back to one already asking), an option per colour for an amount read for "that color" (Nykthos), a land's mana another type instead (`landManaInsteadFor`, a `manaProduction` rule, static or player effect — Infernal Darkness, Deep Water: `landManaInsteadOptions` as many units of it, the same in `standaloneManaVariants` and in `resolveManaAbility`'s `addMana`; an entry's `one` is one unit whatever the amount (Contamination), `colorsOnly` leaves {C} alone (Hall of Gemstone), `produces: "any-color"` is the tapper's pick, one colour for all the land makes (Pulse of Llanowar, Harvest Mage), a player effect's entry reaches every player's lands (Pale Moon); a land its own cost sacrificed is read as it last was, and `couldProduce` reads all of it, rule 106.7), a `manaProduction` rule's `times` (`tapManaMultiplier`) and each triggered mana ability's extra on top (`withManaExtra` — the extras are `ManaOption.extras`, which the planner keeps apart so `useManaSource` stamps none of the source's restriction or rider on them) → `Game.payMana` hands a `ManaPlanningView` to `mana-payment.ts`'s `planPayment` (= `resolveHybridCost` → `planManaPayment`, both pure) → `executePayment` (taps/sacrifices sources, spends the pool, pays life); `castingCostOf` folds in commander tax and `costModification` statics (a `commanderTaxAsLife` card's tax is life instead, paid beside the mana and reserved out of the planning view's `life`). `payMana`'s last two arguments are the difference between a preference and a rule: `avoid` puts a source last (don't casually tap a permanent for its own ability's mana cost), while `exclude` removes it outright — an ability whose cost includes `{T}` taps its source to pay that, so it can't be tapped for mana as well (rule 602.2a). Without the latter, Mind Stone's `{1}, {T}, Sacrifice` funded its own `{1}` off an otherwise empty board. Ability objects are minted onto the stack by `mintAbilityObject` and deleted from `state.objects` on resolution. An ability that was *granted* (a `grantsActivated`/`grantsTriggered` static, or a modifier's `grantsTriggered`/`grantsActivated` — a one-shot grant, or a copy exception's "and it has …") carries a `GrantedAbilityRef` from the moment it's activated or triggers, and `stackAbilityOf` resolves from that rather than by index into its source's current abilities — the grant is often gone by then (an Aura that fell off with the creature it enchanted), and rule 113.7a says the ability doesn't care. The same rule is why a permanent that has lost all its abilities still *lists* its printed triggered abilities (`TriggeredEntry.lost` — they don't trigger, but an index into the list still names the one that triggered before the loss), and has whatever was granted it since the loss, triggered, activated and mana abilities alike (rule 613.7). All P/T reads go through `computeCharacteristics`. Combat damage runs through `combatDamageStep` → `runCombatDamageSubPass` (first-strike sub-pass, then regular, each followed by SBAs + a priority window when relevant; who deals damage in the regular one is fixed by who had first or double strike as the first began — `CombatDamageState.firstStepStrikers`, rule 510.4 — plus whoever has double strike by then) → `dealCombatDamage`, which takes a player-chosen `assign-combat-damage` split when one exists, else auto-assigns the standard split (`standardAssignment`); `dealDamage` runs deathtouch/lifelink, prevention shields, and Fog uniformly (for combat and burn alike) and returns the amount actually dealt. Its replacement and prevention effects are `DamageStep`s (`Game.damageSteps`: multipliers, pluses, at-least raises, halves, the computed preventions — N, all but N, half, B250 — the first prevent-all, static redirections to a host, this permanent or the recipient's controller (rule 614.9; `DamageApplied` keys the ones already applied so a redirected event isn't redirected back, 614.5), and the covering shields — a shield now also covers a recipient set, a source filter, a lasting duration and carries prevention's `then`, run by `applyPreventionFollowUp`; `damage-rule` makes them), applied by `runDamageSteps` in the order `Game.bestDamageOrder` picks (in a combat damage pass, one event, a `perEvent` prevent-all's `then` — "if damage would be dealt to this creature", not "if a creature would deal damage to it" — is owed once per replacement and recipient, `preventedThisEvent`, its "that many" the total, and applied after the pass, as a shield counter goes once — `shieldCountersSpent`) — every order of up to six, the least damage dealt, then the fewest shield points spent (rule 616.1, chosen for the affected player rather than asked). From a source with infect (rule 702.90) or wither (702.80) — `sourceHasDamageKeyword`, any zone, or its last-known information — damage to a creature is that many -1/-1 counters put by the source's controller (`addCounter`, so counter replacements and "you put" triggers see it) rather than marked damage (120.3d), and infect's to a player is poison counters rather than life lost (120.3b); the deathtouch state-based action reads `markedByDeathtouch` alone, since infect damage marks nothing (704.5h). Damage dealt all at once (a combat damage sub-pass, `damage-all`, "each opponent", the steps of a `simultaneous` sequence — `ctx.simultaneously`) runs inside `withDamageBatch`, which settles it as one event: lifelink gains once per *source* (rule 119.9 — so "whenever you gain life" counts sources, not recipients), a `dealt-damage` (enrage) trigger fires once per permanent for the total, and so does a `counters-removed` trigger for the counters the damage took (a planeswalker's loyalty — damage and a minus loyalty cost each emit `counter-removed` for it, rules 306.8 and 606.4; `emitLoyaltyRemovedByDamage`); a `deals-damage` trigger (the source's side) is not batched — it fires once per recipient, as rule 603.2 reads "deals damage to a permanent or player". `createTokens`/`addCounter`/`entersBattlefieldReplacement` all honour replacement multipliers (Doubling Season); `moveCounters` (rule 122.5) removes from one battlefield permanent — one token split off a stack — and puts through `addCounter`, or does neither, and the resolution context's `countersHere`/`counterKindsOf`/`singleOut` serve `move-counters` and `put-counters-of` (the latter reading last-known counters); `beginChoosePermanents` takes an `except` list of ids never offered. `moveObject` is the single zone-transition function: it applies enters-battlefield replacements — the entering permanent's own, then every other permanent's `others-enter-battlefield` (Giada's counters, Thalia's "enter tapped", The Wandering Minstrel's "enter untapped", which beats every enters-tapped), with the moving effect's own "tapped", "under your control", "it's an enchantment" and "it's a Phyrexian in addition" (`setTypes`, `addSubtypes` — the Enduring cycle, Portal to Phyrexia; and the rest of an `EnterTypes` — `addTypes`, `addColors`/`setColors`, `basePt`, `keywords`, through `enterTypesEntry` — as one modifier pushed once its face is settled, gated by `ifCreature` on its types right then: Rise from the Grave, Relive the Past, Excava, Lim-Dûl) passed in as `EnterOptions` rather than applied after the move, so a replacement can see and override them (and the permanent is its entering controller's for the whole entry, so the triggers its counters set off see it as theirs; the control effect that keeps it there is still the caller's to create) — defers a commander's move to a hand or library behind a `commander-replacement` decision (903.9b), from the battlefield, the stack, a graveyard or exile (a spell Remand counters into its owner's hand, Noxious Revival's "on top of its owner's library"; `EnterOptions.libraryPlacement` carries where in the library, `placeInLibrary` putting it there if its owner declines), and records one put into a graveyard or exile (from anywhere) for the next state-based check to offer the command zone (903.9a) — and **returns whether it moved** — `false` for that deferral, and for a card that can't enter the battlefield, which stays where it was (an instant or sorcery, rule 400.4a; an Aura with nothing to enchant, 303.4g; a card told to enter transformed that isn't a double-faced card, 712.14a); a caller that only announces a completed move reads that, never `awaiting`, which may be someone else's decision — redirects a graveyard-bound card to exile when a replacement or `castVia` says to, resets face/copy/modifier state on any move to a hidden zone, and emits `permanent-left-battlefield`/`permanent-destroyed` as appropriate. `runStateBasedActions` recomputes control (layer 2, every control effect in timestamp order), puts an Aura attached to nothing, or to something it can't enchant, into its owner's graveyard and unattaches an Equipment from anything it can't equip (704.5m–n), keeps only the newest of one player's Roles on a permanent (704.5z — a Role token is created attached by `create-token`'s `attachedTo`, which `createTokens` checks with `canCreateAttached`), deletes tokens that have left the battlefield (only those `moveObject` recorded leaving, `GameState.tokensLeftBattlefield` — it used to walk every object in the game, library cards included, each pass), removes a copy of a card still outside the stack and the battlefield (704.5e — one a `cast-now`'s `copies` made and nobody cast, `removeStrayCardCopies`), removes +1/+1 and -1/-1 counters in pairs (704.5q — not from a permanent the same check kills, which leaves with both), enforces commander-damage loss and the legend rule — over the permanents `filter.ts`'s `supertypesOf` still calls legendary (a copy "except it isn't legendary", Aeve as a token) and no "the legend rule doesn't apply" reaches (`characteristics.ts`'s `exemptFromLegendRule`) — the player's choice of which to keep, asked (`legend-rule`, one group at a time, APNAP) before anything moves, the answers waiting in `legendRuleKeeps` so every move the check found still happens at once — offers a commander that's arrived in a graveyard or exile the command zone (903.9a — so it has died, and every "dies" trigger saw it, before its owner is asked), and sacrifices 0-loyalty planeswalkers / completed Sagas — every graveyard-bound SBA is collected first (`stateBasedGraveyardMoves`) and performed at once (rule 704.3), so a creature that dies only because its lord did waits for the next check. `concede(player)` (rule 104.3a; `whyCannotConcede` refuses before the game begins and while they owe a decision, which the server answers first) makes them lose the same way and passes on priority they held. **Winning and losing by effect** (rules 104.2b, 104.3e): `winGame` ends the game at once with the winner (104.1 — `endGame`, which also takes priority away; a multiplayer win ends it for everyone, nobody else "losing") unless `playerCantWinGame`; `loseGame` makes every player it names who can lose (`playerCantLoseGame`) lose at once (`eliminate`, the same path a state-based loss takes) and then ends the game if one player or none is left (`endIfOnePlayerLeft`, 104.2a/104.4a — only after the whole SBA sweep for state-based losses, so two at 0 life draw). Every state-based loss skips a player who can't lose. Once the game is over `applyEffectSpec` does nothing more (`ResolutionContext.gameOver`), so a resolution stops where it won. `drawCard` applies a `would-draw` `"win-game"` replacement (Laboratory Maniac — `drawWinSourceFor`, through `drawWonInstead`) before any redirect when the library is empty, winning or (unable to win) simply not drawing — and again to each draw a replacement turns it into (rule 616.2): the Notion Thief controller's draw an opponent's becomes, each of a "draw N instead". The redirects themselves are a chain (`drawRedirectFor`, `eventualDrawer` for the effect loop's empty-library stop): each Notion Thief applies to a draw once, the draw handed on to whoever it reaches next, sparing the first card a player draws in their own draw step (`exceptFirstInDrawStep`, read off `drewInDrawStepThisTurn`). `affordableTargetOptions` drops a target whose spell would cost life (Terror of the Peaks) the caster can't pay (`canPayLife`), so a cast that would be refused is never offered. `canPayLife` is the one life-payment check (rule 119.4, and 119.8's "can't lose life", which `changeLife` also honours for losses), used by every life cost; `lifeLossFromDamage` applies a `damageLifeFloor` (Angel's Grace) to damage dealt to a player. `maxHandSizeOf` folds `noMaxHandSize` and every `maxHandSize` in timestamp order (rule 613.11). `recordCastName` keeps `PlayerState.spellNamesCastThisGame`. `devotionTo` counts each token in a stack and a copiable mana-cost override (prototype, embalm). A player who loses **leaves the game at once** (`leaveGame`, rule 800.4a) and never receives priority again (`grantPriority` passes it on); during their own turn the turn goes on without an active player (800.4j: `activeHasLeft` skips their draw, attack declaration and cleanup discard): control effects they held end and what they own leaves with them — it stays where it is to be seen but is out of the game (`inGame`, which state-based actions skip; `matchesFilter`, which finds nothing a departed player owns; `moveObject`, which won't move it) — their spells and abilities leave the stack, what they control but don't own is exiled (a spell cast from someone else's cards, a permanent put onto the battlefield under their control — `ControlEffect.entered`, default control rather than an effect), their pending and delayed triggers are dropped, the monarch passes on (725.4), and what their permanents exiled "until" they leave comes back (610.3 — `returnExiledCards`, which parks an `exiled-return` `PendingEntry` while a returning card makes its "as this enters" choice). **Simultaneous leaves are one event** (rule 603.10a): the SBA sweep, a wrath (`drainPendingDestruction`), an edict's victims (every player chooses first, then all are sacrificed) and a return-all each run inside `withLeaveBatch`, and `detectTriggers` shows each leave event to the batch's already-moved permanents too (`lookBackSources`, leaves-the-battlefield triggers only) — so every one of them sees every other one leave, whatever order they moved in. A commander whose move to a hand or library the batch deferred for 903.9b carries the list as `leftWith` and completes its move inside the same batch. **Cards leaving graveyards are batched the same way** (rule 603.10a again): `moveObject` notes each card that leaves a graveyard, with a snapshot of it as it was there, and `withGraveyardLeaveBatch` — around a whole graveyard exiled, a return-all, the moves a `choose-from-zone` answer makes, an escape cost, a `simultaneous` sequence (`ctx.simultaneously`) — announces them as one `cards-left-graveyard` event once they have all moved; outside a batch each card is its own event. A batched `leaves-graveyard` trigger ("whenever one or more cards leave your graveyard") fires once per such event, matching its filter against those snapshots. **Simultaneous entries are one event too** (rule 614.12): a token batch, a return-all, a flicker's return, a tutor's finds and an O-Ring's exiles coming back each run inside `withEnterBatch`, whose members neither apply their `others-enter-battlefield` replacements to one another nor count as "already" on the battlefield for one another — a check land's `tappedUnless` included (`ConditionOptions.notYetHere`). The journal's `entered` snapshots of a batch of two or more are taken together there, in one cache region before the first is announced (`enterBatchSnapshots`, read by `recordJournal` while the permanent is in that stint): one each inside `emit` rescanned the board per permanent. **`makeResolutionContext`'s literal takes no spread**: one anywhere in it makes the runtime define each of its ~490 properties singly (2 ms a context against 5 microseconds), so a field only some resolutions have goes in `sometimes`, assigned after the literal. **"As this enters" choices are asked before the move** (rule 614.12 — `askEnterChoice`: a Clone's copy, a chosen creature type or word): a permanent spell resolving (`enterPermanentSpell`), a land played, a reanimation, a blink or its delayed return, an O-Ring's return and a tutor to the battlefield each ask first and wait — a spell or land play (or a `choose-from-zone` answer) parks a `PendingEntry` in `suspendedResolutions`, an effect step parks itself and runs again — and `moveObject` applies the answer (`GameObject.enterChoice`) as the permanent enters, before its replacements and triggers look at it. **A permanent that left is read as it last existed there** (rules 603.10a, 608.2h): `moveObject` snapshots it (`takeLastKnown` → `GameObject.lastKnown`) before resetting it, and a batch's callers snapshot all of their victims up front (`snapshotLeaving`, kept on the batch and used by `moveObject` in place of its own), so the second victim of a wrath is read before its lord left; a commander whose 903.9b move was deferred keeps the snapshot of the moment it was asked. `detectTriggers` matches a departed subject or look-back source through it — `matchesWho`/`FilterContext.lastKnown` (controller, types, subtypes, counters, keywords, power), no triggered abilities if it had lost them, and the abilities it had then (`departedTriggeredEntries`: a copied card's printed ones plus its granted ones by `GrantedAbilityRef`), recording `LastKnownRefs` on the trigger. A resolving spell or ability reads a departed source, triggering object, sacrificed permanent (by stint — `lastKnownOfStint`) or target (by `targetZones` — `lastKnownOfTarget`) through the snapshot in `makeResolutionContext`: `powerOf`/`toughnessOf`/`manaValueOf`/`manaSpentOf` (the `SpentMana` units, for `spentManaCount`)/`controllerOf`/`cardTypesOf`/`subtypesOf`, the `target`/`trigger-object`/`sacrificed`/`source` conditions, "trigger-controller" scopes, and the damage a departed source deals (`dealDamage`'s `sourceLastKnown`: its colours for protection, lifelink, deathtouch and controller). `choose-modes` and `unless` continuations carry the refs and target zones along, and a `choose-modes` carries `sourceLost` too, so the "you may" of an ability whose source has become a new object finds no "~" (rule 400.7). `castSpell`/`activateAbility`/`whyCannot*` thread through `via` (alt cast zone) — a card with no mana cost can't be cast for it (rule 118.6), only for an alternative cost or free — `face` (multi-face cards), `xValue`, `kicked`, `sacrifice` (additional/ability costs), and evaluate `costModification`/protection/`StaticAbility.condition` gates, and the prohibitions (`whyProhibitedFromCasting` — split second, a `prohibit` effect's `turnProhibitions`, a `prohibits` static (with `castLimit`, only once the player has cast that many matching spells this turn, read from `spellsCastThisTurnAs` — Rule of Law, Ethersworn Canonist), a `player-effect`'s `castCaps` (Irencrag Feat; `costModificationFor`, beside the statics, also takes an unspent `next` grant's `reduceGeneric` and `affinityFor` off a spell's cost — Kaza, Don & Raph), a card's own `castOnlyIf` — and, for land plays, `whyLandPlayProhibited` (a `prohibit` effect's `lands`, a `cantCast` player effect's `lands`, a static's `prohibits.lands`); a static's `from`/`notFrom` and a `cantCast`'s `from` bind only a card in (or outside) those zones as the cast or play begins (`prohibitionReachesZone`, rule 601.2a — Drannith Magistrate, Experimental Frenzy); an activated ability's per-turn limits — `oncePerTurn`, boast and `timesEachTurn` (a number or a count, rule 602.5b) — count the journal's `activated` entries under its `usageKey` (object, stint and which ability), one an activation (`timesUsedThisTurn`), and one whose effect asks `activated-this-turn` (Dragon Whelp) is counted in the journal's `activated` entries by `abilityTurnKey` as it's activated — and `abilitiesProhibited`, which `manaSources` honours too, so a barred mana ability can't pay for anything). **Ward** (rule 702.21) is a real triggered ability (`cards/helpers.ts`'s `ward(cost)`: a `becomes-target` trigger with a `{ kind: "ward", cost: WardCost }` effect), so it goes on the stack above the targeting spell or ability. Every spell, activated ability, copy *and triggered ability* announces the objects and players it targets as it goes on the stack (`announceTargeted` → `object-targeted`, its `firstThisTurn` the first by anyone this turn, and `player-targeted` once per player — a `becomes-target` trigger's `player`/`playerOnly`/`abilityOnly` read them (and the journal's `targeted` entries the timing wrapper), whose `stackObject` is the spell or ability object; a trigger announces from `mintTriggerAbility`, skipping event-filled `autoTargetSlots`), and a `becomes-target` trigger records that as `PendingTrigger`/`GameObject.targetedBy`. `beginWard` asks *that* player to pay the one compound cost as a `choose-modes` decision carrying `ward` (mana on the decision's `cost`, life/sacrifice/discard as the mode's effect, a `counter` of the targeting object as `onDecline`), offered only when all of it is affordable; `counterObject` removes a countered *ability* from the stack. Cascade's and suspend's free casts are whole casts offered as a `cast-now` (`raiseCastNow` with `freeCastOf`): modes, kicker, a gift, targets and costs chosen as any cast's are, or declined; `castSpell` records `castVia` (and the `spell-cast` event's `via`) as `"cascade"`/`"suspend"` and gives suspend's spell `hastyUntilItLeaves`, cost increases still apply (Thalia — rule 601.2f) and a card that can't be paid for isn't offered. Cascade's `cascade` effect reveals (`Game.cascade`), then runs as a `sequence` — `offer` (the `cast-now`, its spell filter "mana value less than the cascade spell's", rule 702.85a) and `finish` (`finishCascade`: what's still exiled goes to the bottom in a random order); a suspended card's offer comes from suspend's own triggers on the stack (702.62a — `suspendTrigger`: the upkeep's "remove a time counter", a `remove-counter` on the source, then "when the last is removed", a `cast-now { suspend: true }`), a declined or unplayable card left exiled, no longer suspended. A triggered ability needing a real target choice parks in `pendingTargetedTrigger` and raises a `choose-targets` decision rather than picking synchronously. A copy of a spell whose effect lets its controller choose new targets (`copy-spell`'s `newTargets`, rule 707.10c) parks in `pendingCopyTargets` and raises the same decision with `current` — each slot's target now, kept even if it's no longer legal, and offered first; the copy sits on the stack meanwhile and its targets are announced once answered (`retargetCopy`). A copy of an activated or triggered ability (`copyStackAbility`, from `copy-ability`) asks the same way, its slots the ability's own specs judged from its source for the copy's controller (never offering a copy itself, rule 115.5); an ability countered while an `activates-ability` trigger waits on it is kept as it last was in `GameState.departedAbilities` (emptied as each turn begins). Copies made while another is being asked (storm's several — `stormCopy` copies from `lastOnStack` too, so a countered spell still storms) wait in `copyTargetsQueue`, asked one at a time in the `prepareForPriority` fixpoint (`promptNextCopyTargets`). A `discard` with `unlessOne` (Compulsive Research's "unless they discard a land card") raises the `discard` decision with `orOneOf`, any one of which is a whole answer (offered to the bots on its own, and named for the prompt by `orOneKind` when the filter is one card type); the `choose-from-zone` legal action carries its `destination` (and `split` for Cultivate), so a client can say where the cards go — `library-top` is the order picked. An attack tax (`attackTax` statics — Ghostly Prison — and `attackTax` player effects — Forbidding Spirit, War Tax) is a cost to attack in mana and in life, covering the player, their planeswalkers or both, locked in before the attackers tap and paid after (rule 508.1f–j): `attackTaxOn`/`attackTaxTotal`/`attackTaxPayment` (attackers that tap withheld from the payer), validated in the `attackers` decision through `DecisionReadCtx.whyCannotPayAttackTax`, and offered as `AttackOffer.attackTax` (`perCreature`, `lifePerCreature` and `phyrexianPerCreature` — Norn's Annex's {W/P} pips, paid as the declaration's `phyrexianLife` says (rule 107.4f), counted as 2 life each by `withinAttackTax` — by defender, a sure-to-pay `budget` from `attackTaxBudget`, a `lifeBudget`) that `combat/attacking.ts`'s `withinAttackTax` trims a driver's declaration to; a creature that could attack only a taxing player isn't in `mustAttack` (508.1d). **Split cards** (rule 709, `CardDefinition.split`): `faces` is `[whole, left, right]`, face 0 the combined card off the stack (709.4) and never cast — `castableFaces` offers each half alone (709.3), and leaving the stack resets the face to the whole card. **Divided damage** (rule 601.2d): a card's `divided: { total, slot }` makes the cast carry a `division` (validated by `spellDivision`, an even split when absent), kept on the spell (`GameObject.division`, in `SpellSnapshot` so a copy keeps it) and dealt by `damage-divided`; an `any-number` group's `max` caps its members (`slotOptions`, `invalidTargetReason`). The cast offer carries `divide` for a client. A `divided.total` that is an `EffectAmount` (Volcanic Wind's creature count, Monstrous Onslaught's greatest power) is read when the division is announced and fixed from then on (rule 601.2d) by `dividedNow` (a spell's or activated ability's, for its source and X) or `triggerDivided` (a trigger's or reflexive trigger's, with its last-known refs, 603.3d/603.12); the offer carries the number and `specsCappedByDivision` caps the group at one target per point. A spell's total read from X beyond plain X (Meteor Shower's "X plus 1", Shatterskull Smashing's "twice X if X is 6 or more") is offered at every X (`dividedTotalsByX` -> `divide.totalByX`, the client's division step reading it at the X chosen), and `xGroupOffer` takes it to bound the number of targets each X allows (`minXByTargetCount`) as "up to X" does. **Copying a spell** (`copySpellFrom`) starts from a `SpellSnapshot` — its characteristics and every cast choice: modes, targets, X, kicker/overload/evoke paid — taken off the live spell, or off `GameObject.lastOnStack`, which `moveObject` and `forgetSpellTargets` record as a spell leaves the stack, so a copy trigger still copies a spell countered in response (Shiko and Narset, Unified). A copy of a permanent spell becomes a token as it resolves (608.3f), not "created". A copy aimed by its effect (`retargetedSnapshot`, rules 707.10d–e) is the snapshot with every non-automatic target replaced, built only if that one is legal for each slot as the copy's controller would choose it (`castTargetSpecs` reads a snapshot's specs as `spellTargetSpecs` does a live spell's); `copySpellForEachItCouldTarget` makes one per permanent it could target (Zada), splitting token stacks, and with two or more raises a `choose-permanents` whose order is the stack order. **Leaving the stack after resolving** (`leaveStackAfterResolving`, 608.2n) honours `GameObject.exileAsItResolves` (Feather): exiled instead of put into its owner's graveyard, then a `next-end-step` delayed trigger returns it (`exileAsItResolved`); beside flashback, an exile-afterwards permission, a graveyard-to-exile static or an Adventure it asks the owner which applies first (rule 616.1) as a `choose-modes` raised there — the spell waits on the stack, and `resumeSuspendedResolution` holds the resolution open around it. Cipher (702.99a — `CardDefinition.cipher`) comes before all of these, as the spell's own last instruction: a `choose-permanents` with `cipher` set (up to one creature its controller controls, the spell waiting on the stack), answered by `finishCipher` — exiled with the creature's `encodedCards` recording it, or on through `leaveStackAfterResolving(id, true)` as usual; `effectiveTriggeredEntries` gives a creature one `cipherTrigger` (a `cast-now` with `encoded`, one free copy) per card still exiled in the stint it was encoded in, and `moveObject` clears `encodedCards` (702.99c). Rebound (702.88a — `rebounds`, a card with `CardDefinition.rebound` cast from its owner's hand, never a copy) is another: `reboundAsItResolves` exiles it and makes a `your-next-upkeep` delayed trigger whose `cast-now` offers it free from exile (`rebound.test.ts`). Saddle (702.171) is crew's cost on a sorcery-speed ability marked `saddle`: paying it records each tapped creature as having saddled the source (`recordSaddlers`, 702.171c), and its effect makes the source saddled (`becomeSaddled`, emitting `became-saddled`); cleanup clears both. Which keyword a total-power cost is goes to `tapCostOffer` (`tapCostKeyword` — an ability marked `crew` or `saddle`), whose `power` for each choice is `tapCostPower`: the creature's power, or under a `crewsAs` static of its own (a Pilot's "crews Vehicles [and saddles Mounts] as though its power were 2 greater" / "using its toughness rather than its power", rules 702.122a and 702.171a) the modified value — saddle only under `saddles`, teamwork never, and none once it has lost its abilities (`crew-power-mods.test.ts`). A `look-and-choose`'s `then` keeps the source's stint (`thenSourceStint`), so it reads a source that has left as it last was (Caustic Bronco). Teamwork (702.194) is a kicker whose cost taps creatures: `spellTapCost` gives a kicked teamwork spell crew's total-power tap cost beside an alternative cost's (Sephara), offered as the kicked variant's `tapCost`, and `castModalRange` makes `castModal.kickedModes` the exact mode count when kicked (`teamwork.test.ts`). A `counter-added` event carries `after`, how many of that kind the object has once they're on, so a `counters-put` trigger's `nth` ("when the Nth counter is put on this" — the Plans) fires only on the placement crossing N (`nth-counter.test.ts`). A `put-into-graveyard` trigger's `firstTimeEachTurn` (`reachedGraveyardEarlier`) reads the owner's `descended` turn history less this move's own entries, so a card that reached the graveyard before the permanent was there still counts as the turn's first (Crawling Sensation). Sneak (702.190) is an alternative cost cast `via: "sneak"`: `sneakReturns` lists the caster's unblocked attacking creatures in their own declare blockers step, `legalActions` offers one cast per creature (`sneakReturn`, carried into `castSpell` as `sneakReturnChoice`), paying returns it to hand and stamps `GameObject.sneakAttacking`, and `enterPermanentSpell` puts the permanent onto the battlefield tapped and into the attack against that (`putIntoAttack`) — `sneak.test.ts`. Web-slinging (702.188a) is the same shape under normal timing, cast `via: "web-slinging"`: `webSlingingReturns` lists the caster's tapped creatures, `legalActions` offers one cast per creature next to blitz (`webSlingingReturn`, carried as `webSlingingReturnChoice`), and paying it — after the mana, so a creature tapped for mana first may go — splits one token off a stack, stamps that creature's last-known mana value on the spell (`GameObject.webSlingingManaValue`, kept onto the battlefield like `xValue` and read by an enters replacement's `"web-slinging-mana-value"`), and returns it to hand; never alongside another alternative cost — `web-slinging.test.ts`. Emerge (702.119a) is cast `via: "emerge"` from the same places: `emergeSacrifices` lists what it could sacrifice (`emerge.from` for "emerge from [quality]"), `legalActions` offers one cast per creature (`emergeSacrifice`, its `sacrifice` choices just that one), each priced inside `withEmergeSacrifice`, whose `emergeManaValue` `castingCostOf` takes off after increases, as harmonize's power is; `castRouteSacrifice` (Exploration Broodship's graveyard-permission sacrifice too) hands the creature to the additional-cost sacrifice path, which taps it for mana first where it can and sacrifices it as the cost is paid — `emerge.test.ts`. A `trigger-object` condition's `manaSpent` reads `lastStackManaSpent` for a spell gone from the stack (opus — `opus.test.ts`). **Cast and play permissions from exile and the library top** (rule 601.3): `playOptions` collects the `mayPlay` statics a card in exile answers to (Grolnok's croak counters, Haldan's fetch counters with `exiledByYou`, Tinybones' stash counters during your turn), `mayPlayFromExile` combines them with the card's own `impulse` for a land play or a `via: "impulse"` cast (`playLifeMode`: a static's `payLifeManaValue` — Valgavoth, Terror Eater — is that cast's only alternative cost when no other permission reaches the card, one more otherwise), and `libraryTopCastPermissions` the `mayPlay` statics the top card answers to as the spell it would be, at the X announced (`libraryTopMinX` gives an X spell's offer its `xCost.minX`, and floors every count's least X when its targets are tied to X — `xGroupOffer`, whose counts are of the group's members alone, `targetCount.from` its slot); a `gainsHaste` one puts a `castRider` haste modifier on the spell, which `moveObject` keeps onto the battlefield like a prototype's. A `payLifeManaValue` one (Bolas's Citadel) casts it only for life equal to its mana value: `libraryTopPaysLifeOnly` (no other permission reaches it at any X, `which: "paid"`) empties the paid variants and makes `alternativeCostsOf` return that one entry, as an impulse's does; beside Future Sight it's one more entry. A forced pay-life entry sits at index 1 when the card has an alternative cost of its own, since `ownAltCost` reads index 0 as that one. `spendAsFor` is each payment's `SpendAs` (see `mana-payment.ts`). Toxic's poison counters are given in `dealDamage`, for combat damage to a player (`toxicOf`, rule 120.3g). Casualty (rule 702.153): `queueCasualty` lists a spell's instances — printed `casualty`, granted `grantsToSpells.casualty` — once it's on the stack, from `castSpell` and `commitFreeCast` alike; `promptNextCasualty` (in the `prepareForPriority` fixpoint) asks each as a `choose-permanents` of up to one creature with power N or greater, the awaiting's `casualty`; `applyCasualty` sacrifices the one chosen, takes back the cast triggers that creature fired (`dropCastTriggersOf` — it left before the spell became cast, rule 601.2i; a token off a stack takes only its share), and queues the copy as a self-contained trigger record (a `copy-spell` of `trigger-spell`), and priority then goes to the caster. Conspire (rule 702.78a) runs beside it: `queueConspire` counts the instances (printed `conspire`, granted `grantsToSpells.conspire`) into `pendingConspire`; `promptNextConspire` (in the same fixpoint, after casualty) asks whether to pay as a `choose-modes` of one mode or none (`conspireAsk`), skipped without two untapped creatures sharing a colour with the spell (`conspireCandidates`); `applyConspireAsk` then asks which two as a `choose-permanents` of exactly two (`conspire`), and `applyConspire` taps them and queues the same copy record (`queuePaidCostCopy`, shared with casualty). `additionalCost.copyForEachSacrificed` (Plumb the Forbidden's "you may sacrifice one or more creatures. When you do, copy this spell for each creature sacrificed this way") queues that record too as the cast finishes, once with X copies when X (the sacrifice's count) is 1 or more, keeping the original's targets (`newTargets: false`, rule 707.10c). Gift (rule 702.174a) is the kicked variant of a `kicker.keyword: "gift"` card: `castSpell` queues `queueGift` (its opponents still in the game, in turn order from the caster), and `promptNextGift` (in the same fixpoint, before casualty) promises it to the only one without asking, or raises one `choose-modes` with a "Promise the gift to …" mode per candidate (each an `about-player`, so its `modePlayers` name them and a client can pick them on their panels), the awaiting's `giftAsk` — `applyGiftChoice` promises it to the chosen mode's candidate (`promiseGift` sets `giftTo` and emits `gift-promised`). The `giveGift` resolution hook reads `giftTo` / `enteredGiftTo` (as the source last existed, if it has left) and gives the `GiftKind`. Cascade's and suspend's free casts offer kicker and a gift as any cast does. `endStep` takes `phasesAfterThisCombat` front first, at the end of a combat phase or of an added upkeep step, which then goes on to the postcombat main phase, and at the end of the postcombat main phase when one was made there (Grim Reaper's Sprint), and at the end of the precombat main phase, where it comes before the turn's own combat, which goes behind it as an entry marked `regular` (Moraug, Fury of Akoum's rulings; rule 500.8). An `additional-combat`'s `atBeginning` is a delayed trigger on `{ thatCombat: mark }`, the mark on the `AddedPhase` or in `GameState.extraCombatMarks` beside `extraCombats` (newest first), in `TurnState.addedCombat` as that phase begins; `GameObject.attacksThisTurn` counts declarations. Riot is one more `nextEnterChoice` kind, applied in `enteringReplacementOf` (the counter) or as a permanent haste modifier (`gainRiotHaste`). Exert: `applyAttackerDeclarations` queues `pendingExerts`, which `prepareForPriority` asks (`promptNextExert`) before placing any trigger; enlist (rule 702.154) comes first: with an attacker that has it, `applyAttackerDeclarations` stops after the attackers tap and the attack tax is paid, `promptNextEnlist` asks each one's `choose-permanents` of up to one creature, `applyEnlist` taps the pick, marks `enlistedIn`, emits `creature-tapped-for` and queues the reflexive +X/+0 (`powerOf: "tapped"`), and `announceAttackers` emits the declaration's events once the last is answered; a crew cost's creatures are recorded by `recordCrewers` (as `recordSaddlers` does saddle's) and every crew or saddle creature announced as a `creature-tapped-for`, and `tapCostOffer` leaves a `cant-crew` creature out of a crew cost; `untapStep` skips and then clears the active player's `exertedBy`. An ability's divided amount is split at activation (`activateAbility`'s `division`) or as a trigger goes on the stack (`applyChooseTargets`, or evenly when nothing is asked), stored as the ability object's `division`, and passed to its resolution. `targetLegality` rechecks the relations among targets at resolution: two "controlled by different players" targets one player controls are both illegal, and a group over its `maxTotalPower` is wholly illegal. An Omen spell (one with an `omen` card's Omen face as its characteristics — the card cast as its Omen, or a copy of one) leaves the stack for its owner's library, shuffled, a copy ceasing to exist as its owner shuffles (`leaveStackAfterResolving`); a graveyard permission's `filter` and the flash check judge the face being cast (`withFace`). **Library ordering** (rule 401.4): `beginLibraryOrder` asks the order of cards going onto the top or bottom of a library together as a `choose-from-zone` with `order: true` (every card picked, the first nearest the top), skipped when there's nothing to choose between (one card, or copies of one); `applyScry` asks it for a scry's or surveil's kept cards and then a scry's bottomed ones (`thenOrder` — rules 701.22a, 701.25a), parking its `then`; a `look-and-choose`'s `"bottom-any-order"` (or `"top-any-order"`) leftover asks it once the chosen cards have moved, before `then` (`moveChosenFromZone` returns a `LibraryOrder`: the cards and which end); a chosen card's `"library-bottom"` destination is placed in pick order, and cards it takes from a hand are announced as `cards-put-on-bottom`. A chosen card that is its owner's commander, bound from their hand for their library (Brainstorm, Valakut Awakening, Teferi's Puzzle Box), is offered the command zone first (rule 903.9b): `finishZoneChoice` parks the choice (`PendingEntry`'s `commanderAnswers`) behind a `commander-replacement` decision whose `deferredCommanderMove` is `heldByZoneChoice`, so `applyCommanderChoice` only records the answer, and the choice then moves it with the rest, to the command zone or to its picked place. An activated ability's `reveal` cost ("Reveal X white cards from your hand") is `beginBehold` over a `fromHand` spec once the ability is on the stack, its X capped by `revealCostCandidates`; a `fromHand` spec without `exile` is asked with the `"reveal"` destination and reveals (rule 701.20a), and a kicker's `orControls` kicks a cast whose caster controls a match once its costs are paid, marked `kickedByControl` so `copySpellFrom` leaves it unkicked. An activated ability's `exileFromGraveyard` cost is `payGraveyardExileCost`: a `choose-from-zone` (`destination: "exile"`, the matching cards eligible) raised as the ability goes on the stack, whose `priorityTo` hands priority back to the activator (rule 117.3c) — as a cost's `discard` decision's does too (`costDiscardPriorityTo`, a spell's additional discard included); no more matching cards than it takes are exiled at once. `flicker`'s `tapped` returns the card tapped (`completeFlickerReturn`). A `becomes-untapped` trigger matches `permanent-untapped` but not a shock land's `asItEntered` one, its trigger object is the untapped permanent, and a token stack untapping fires it once per token; so does a `counters-put` trigger for counters put on a stack (`counter-added`'s optional `count` names how many tokens of it got them, for a batch that entered with counters and folded into a stack, and then the stack's own "counters put on this" fires for those tokens only — `mintTokenBatch` announces it, and gives a fresh stack its `stackCount` before its entering counters are announced). Tokens split off a stack fold back mid-turn once identical (`refoldSplitTokens`, see "Token stacking" above). A modal spell's chosen modes resolve in printed order as a `sequence`'s steps do: a mode that stops to ask something (a search, a discard, a choice) parks the modes after it in `suspendedResolutions`, each with its own target slice, so they wait for the answer (rules 608.2c, 700.2 — Insatiable Avarice's draw finds the card its search put on top). `exchangeLifeToughness` is rule 701.12g's exchange (neither half when a can't-gain or can't-lose effect stops the life half, 701.12a); `putCostCounters` pays an `AbilityCost.addCounter` — straight on, no counter replacement — by hand and through `useManaSource`, where `manaSources` admits such an untapped once-a-turn mana ability as a last-resort source (a plan step's `counterCost`); `add-mana`'s `first` (what a mana ability does before its mana — Empowered Autogenerator's charge counter) is a plan step's `first`, applied by `useManaSource` before the mana goes in, and `liveManaAmount` (with `sizesManaFirst`) reads a live amount as it will be after a `first` of a fixed number of counters on the source (`counterTotal`, `countersPrevented`), for `manaSources`, `standaloneManaVariants`, `fixedManaOutput` and `couldProduce` alike — any other `first` keeps the ability out of `manaSources` (2026-11-07); a cast records its `castFrom` as soon as it's on the stack, before its costs are paid, so `manaUnitCanPay` can answer a `castFrom` or `notCastFrom` restriction. `manaUnitCanPay` reads `asSpellBeingCast` for a `spell` filter's `hasAbilities` (rule 601.2a; a `grantsToSpells` read through `spellGrantAmount`), and first of all the spell's `spendOnlyManaFrom`, which `manaPlanningView` also applies to the sources, as it does an ability cost's `spendOnlyChosenColor` (`spendOnlyManaTypeFor`). Cycling (`cycleCard`) is an activated ability from the hand (rule 702.29a) — so `whyCannotCycle` refuses it under a split-second spell (702.61a) or a can't-activate prohibition, as for any non-mana ability: cost paid and card discarded as it's activated, then an ability object sourced from the cycled card — its `grantedAbility` a `cycling` ref, resolved through `cyclingAbilityOf` (the draw, or landcycling's revealed search) — goes on the stack, so it can be responded to, and a `this-cycled` trigger (the card the event's subject, wherever it went, contributing only those abilities) goes on above it and resolves first (rule 702.29c). `graveyardGrantsFor` offers a card's own `mayPlaySelf` permission (Gravecrawler) as it does a one-shot one, spending nothing. `beginZoneChoice`'s `"command"` zone is the chooser's own commanders in the command zone (Command Beacon, Hellkite Courser), and `putInCommandZone` returns a commander still on the battlefield there. A card entering a graveyard gets a timestamp (rule 613.7d), which orders a `fromGraveyard` static (Anger's ruling). `expireUntilEndOfCombat` runs as each non-combat step begins. `createTokens`/`createTokenCopy` make a creature token as a `creatureTokensInstead` replacement's token instead (`creatureTokenSubstitute` — Divine Visitation), and amass then finds no Army in it. `becomeCopy` is the `become-copy` effect — `copiableValuesOf` reads what the object copied gives (a permanent's copiable values now or as it last existed, a spell's, or a card's printed front face), `setCopiableValues` applies them for good or as a temporary copy, and `endTemporaryCopies` (cleanup, the end step's start, a turn's start) undoes ended ones back to the latest left or `copyBase`; `copyExceptionModifier` takes the registry to look up a `thisAbility`; `payReturnToHandCost` pays an `AbilityCost.returnToHand` after the ability is on the stack, asking with `choose-permanents` only when there's a choice. An `attacker-declared` carries `firstThisTurn`; `attackConditionsStillHold` asks `"most-still-attacking"` again of what the attacker attacks then. `attackRandomOpponent` picks with the seeded RNG. `sacrificeAll` sacrifices every match at once; `putExiledThisWayOntoBattlefield` returns the cards a resolution exiled from non-battlefield zones; `withConvoker` binds a convoker as target 0 when it's still the same object and only as a read (pinned as the context's `tapped`) when not. `graveyardExileReplacements` returns every replacement found, each with its permanent's controller, and `exileCountersChosen` decides whether `moveObject` puts a `withCounters` (Dauthi Voidwalker's void counter) on the card as it arrives in exile: beside another replacement that exiles it with none (a finality counter, flashback, a disturb back face's own `self` replacement — read off the face's text, so a Clone of it has it and one that lost its abilities doesn't, rule 707.2 —, a Kess-style permission, Rest in Peace), only when the affected object's controller — its owner off the battlefield and stack — controls a replacement putting it (rule 616.1, the one choice that serves them). `chooseExiledToPlay` grants a this-turn play permission to one chosen exiled card; `putArrivedOntoBattlefield` puts a batched graveyard trigger's cards onto the battlefield; `beginZoneChoice` takes a `picker` who chooses from another player's zone (Tasigur). `payExileFromGraveyardCost` asks with a `choose-from-zone` (`destination: "exile"`, `priorityTo` the activator); an activated ability's "X target …" group is offered once per X and checked at the X activated with (`specsAtX`). `votesFor` counts this resolution's `modes-chosen` answers for its source; `revealUntilCount` and `putOntoBattlefieldTogether` serve `reveal-until-count`. `activatedGrantSources` also reads, memoized per cache region, the `grantsActivatedOf` statics ("has all activated abilities of ...", rule 613.1f), whose zones of cards go through `cardsAGrantReads` (a graveyard, the library's top card, exile, the cards exiled with this stint, the last `choose-exiled-card` choice), each ability with a `card-activated` ref (an `onceEach` grant's naming its card too, its limit under its `usageKey` through `usedThisTurn`/`markUsedThisTurn`); `triggeredGrantSources` reads their `withTriggered` the same way, with `card-triggered` refs; all through `copiedActivatedGrants`, which applies them one at a time on top of the other grants in rule 613.8's order (one whose result another would change waits for it, a loop by timestamp), each reading graveyard cards, the library's top card or other permanents with everything they have (`copiedActivatedEntries`, the refs passed through). `spendAsFor` reads `spendManaAs.activating` against an ability purpose's `source` and `ability`. `endWhileSourceEffects(ids)` ends every modifier and control effect that lasted while one of `ids` remained on the battlefield; `moveObject` calls it as the source leaves — or, inside `withLeaveBatch`, once the whole batch has left (`leaveBatch.endsWhileSource`), so the event's look back (rule 603.10a) still sees the effects. It ends what lasted for the stint that left even if the source is back by then (a blink within one event — a new object, rule 400.7). `lastingHere(duration)` (in the resolution context) turns a `PermanentDuration` into the stored `Expiry` with the resolving source's stint for `"while-source"`; `gainControlByEffect`'s `opts.whileSource` drops only the same player's effects that end no later than the new one. `untapUnlessStunned(id)` is every untap — the untap step's, `untapAll`'s, `setTapped`'s — so a stun counter is removed instead (rule 122.1d). The resolution context's `damageSource(from)` also takes a target slot (`DamageFrom`), and `returnFromGraveyardByEffect` every graveyard (`opts.allGraveyards`, entering under the effect's controller, `enterAs`). `beginZoneChoice` takes `{ cards }`, a look-and-choose over the resolving ability's own targets, and asks nothing when there's nothing to look at (an empty library, X of 0): the resolution goes straight on (Thassa's Oracle's win check). Populate and amass ask through `beginChoosePermanents` when the candidates differ (`interchangeableKinds` — alike but for timestamps is one choice), amass re-entering with `onto` the Army chosen; `beginChoosePermanents` takes `exceptSource`, and `optionModes` an unless/each-player-may sacrifice option's `exceptSource`, and turns each `UnlessOption` kind into the mode its chooser takes (a permanent's return, tap or counter through `choose-permanents`, `sacrificeThat` through `sacrifice-target`, `payEnergy` through `pay-energy`, `pay`'s `andLife` and `ifPaid` beside the decision's own cost). A `transform` effect leaves a daybound or nightbound permanent alone (rules 702.145b/e). `beginBlight` (rule 701.68) is the one place a player blights — an ability's `AbilityCost.blight`, a spell's `blight` cost option or `keyword: "blight"` kicker (after the spell is on the stack, 601.2h), ward's blight and the `blight` effect: auto-chosen with one creature (a stack singled out first), else a `choose-permanents` with `blight` set, whose answer singles the creature out of its stack before `then`; `canBlight` gates every offer (701.68b) — counting only the creatures a -1/-1 counter can go on (`blightable`: no `preventsCounters` static protects them), the only ones a blight paid as a cost may choose. `beginBehold` (rule 701.4a) is the one place a player beholds — a spell's `behold` cost option, `keyword: "behold"` kicker, mandatory `additionalCost.behold` or flashback's `behold` (after the spell is on the stack, 601.2h, under `withDecisionSource`), the `behold` effect, and a land's `tappedUnlessBehold` as it enters (`askEnterChoice`'s `"behold"` choice, recorded in `enterChoice.reveal`): `beholdCandidates` is the player's matching hand cards and permanents together (never the spell being cast or the entering land, nor a permanent entering beside it); with exactly as many as it takes and nothing optional they're beheld unasked, else a `choose-from-zone` with `behold` set (a `count` over 1 splits stacks so each token is a candidate). `applyChooseFromZone` hands that answer to `applyBehold`/`carryOutBehold`: hand cards revealed (`revealCards`, so `revealedThisTurn` shows them to every seat), then with `exile` each exiled — a permanent singled out of its stack, `permanent-exiled`, linked through `exileLink` (607.2q: the spell's id and its stack stint + 1, the battlefield stint it becomes) unless a token — then `then` with the first beheld object as target 0. `canBehold` gates every offer; a kicked cast with `kicker.flash` skips the sorcery-timing check (601.3c). Bargain (rule 702.166a) is a `keyword: "bargain"` kicker whose kicked variant adds one part (`BARGAIN_FILTER`: an artifact, enchantment or token) to `spellSacrificeParts`, so it rides the sacrifice-of-several path: offered only while one is left once the mana is paid (the candidates tried last for mana, 601.2g), chosen with the `sacrifice` decision once the spell is on the stack (`beginCostSacrifice`, 601.2h). A kicked variant's `kicker.reduceGeneric` ("costs {1} less if it's bargained") is priced through the `kickedCostReduction` probe — set per variant in `castSpellActions` and by `whyCannotCastSpell` / `castSpell` themselves (`kickedReductionStale`), as `harmonizePower` is — which `castingCostOf` subtracts for that card after increases (601.2f). Airbend (rule 701.65a): `exileObject`'s `airbend` and `exileAllByEffect`'s (with its `except`) set `airbended` on each card they exile (a token is gone); `legalActions` offers each of a player's own airbended cards (each castable face, never a land) through `castSpellActions` with `via: "airbend"` and `Game.AIRBEND_COST`, which `castCostString` pays in place of the mana cost — kicker and other additional costs concatenate onto it, X is 0 (no X in the cost), and the variant loop drops overload, free casts and other alternative costs as it does for warp (`alternativeCostOf` and `evokeCostsOf` return none for it, `whyCannotCastSpell` refuses them, 118.9a). `whyProhibitedFromCasting` refuses a cast from outside the caster's hand while a `castOnlyFromHand` player effect names them. Waterbend (rule 701.67): `waterbendTaps` is what pays a waterbend cost's generic — the driver's `waterbend` (held in `waterbendChoice` for the card or source being dispatched or checked, as `sneakReturnChoice` is, so the long positional `castSpell`/`whyCannotCastSpell` signatures don't grow) checked by `whyWaterbendIsWrong`, or the fewest from the front of `waterbendCandidates` (untapped artifacts and creatures, noncreature artifacts first, an ability's own source after the rest, mana makers last) that leave the rest payable (`waterbendProof`, halving from the most that may tap) — never more than the waterbend part's generic or the total's (701.67b: `spellWaterbend` / `abilityWaterbend`). The taps are withheld from the mana plan and tapped with the payment (`tapForWaterbend`, a stack peeled token by token). An omitted `waterbend` is how every offer is judged, so a spell or ability mana alone can't pay is offered when taps make up the difference; `castCostString` adds an additional waterbend's `{N}`/`{X}` to the total like escalate, `maxXByWaterbend` (beside `maxXByDelve` in `xPlanFor`) and `maxWaterbendAbilityX` raise X as far as taps reach, and `waterbendOffer` builds the offer (`byX` for an `{X}`). **Phasing:** `phaseOut(ids, until?)` takes the attached along indirectly, snapshots each as `lastKnown`, removes it from combat (506.4), moves it to `GameState.phasedOut`, emits `permanents-phased-out` and ends "for as long as" effects tracking it (`endWhileSourceEffects`, 702.26f); `phaseIn(ids, tapped?)` brings the indirect ones with each host, re-attaches or detaches a directly phased-out Aura/Equipment (702.26i), recomputes control (a control effect that ended meanwhile gives it to nobody) and emits `permanents-phased-in`; `phasingStep` is the untap step's turn-based action before untapping (502.1 — the `phasing` keyword out, what phased out under the active player in); `phaseInUntilLeft` phases in what waited on a permanent leaving, right after the leave event (`leaveBatch.phaseInAfter`). `moveObjectUncached` refuses a phased-out object. The untap step's turn resets, `beginTurn`'s expiries and cleanup's damage and "until end of turn" removal (514.2) reach phased-out permanents too. Protection from everything prevents damage to a player (`dealDamage`) and stops an Aura enchanting them (`canEnchantPlayer`); `changeLife` refuses a gain when a player's life can't change. A static's `loyaltyAtInstantSpeed` skips the loyalty ability's sorcery timing (Teferi, Master of Time), and a permanent's loyalty activations are the journal's `activated` entries for its stint, so each turn starts afresh. A `cast-spell` trigger's `offTurn` is "if it isn't that player's turn". A loop of mandatory actions is a draw (rule 104.4b): `noteMandatoryResolution` counts the resolutions since a player last did anything but pass (`dispatchOne` resets it — not game state, so a restored game counts afresh) and, past `LOOP_RESOLUTIONS`, ends the game in a draw when the latest of them repeat one short cycle (`repeatsOneCycle`); a loop that asks someone a choice each round still meets `runUntil`'s budget. A keyword's non-mana cost (`KickerAlsoCost`: a kicker's or squad's `also`, `alsoTimes` over, a spliced card's `splice.also`) comes through `castAlsoCosts`: checked by `whyCannotPayAlso`, multikicker's offers bounded by `alsoCostCap`, what's tapped in `spellTapCost`, what's sacrificed in `spellSacrificeParts`, life paid at once, and the rest queued by `queueAlsoCosts` in `GameState.pendingAlsoCosts` (`AlsoCostAsk`), paid one part at a time by `payNextAlsoCost` before anyone gets priority; "an opponent gains N life" asks as a gift does (`GiftAsk.gainLife`). Splice (rule 702.47): `activeSplice` holds the cards spliced onto the spell being priced or cast (`withSplice`/`enterSplice`, set per variant in `castSpellActions` and around the `cast-spell` dispatch), and while it's set `castCostString` adds their splice costs and `effectiveTargetSpecs` their targets after the spell's own; `castSpell` stores their names on the stack object (`spliced`, copied to a copy, cleared with the stack, read by `castTargetSpecs`), and `resolveTopObject` follows the spell's own effect, chosen modes or fused halves and then each spliced card's as parts, each with its slice of the targets. `spliceFits`/`spliceCandidates`/`whyCannotSplice` say what may be spliced onto what. **Flip cards** (rule 710): `flipPermanent` (the `flip` effect; `EnterOptions.flipped` for "return it flipped") sets the one-way status, `moveObject` clears it, and every copy records a flip card by its normal half (`unflippedName`) so the copier's own status picks the half (707.3); a flip card is never double-faced, never transforms, and its alternative half is never cast. **Sideboards** (2026-10-30): `setup` makes `DeckList.sideboard`'s cards `zone: "outside"` objects (for a deck with commanders only its companions — 903.5e, 702.139d — and `sideboardOf` gives that player no sideboard to count or search; **companion**, 2026-11-01: `beginCompanionReveals` asks each player with an eligible card, starting player first, before the hands, as a `choose-from-zone` flagged `companion` (103.2b), `GameState.companions` holds what's revealed, and `take-companion` is the {3} special action, 116.2g); `beginLibrarySearch` adds them for a search's `outside` (`"only"`: nothing else, no shuffle, nothing asked when nothing fits) and the owner's face-up exiled cards for `faceUpExile`; a revealed find from outside is revealed `"sideboard"` (`cards-revealed`'s `from`), a face-up exiled one not at all. **Assist** (rule 702.132a, 2026-10-31): `castSpellActions` wraps `castSpellActionsAlone`, adding a spell with assist's offers again per other player whose mana could help (`assistCapacity`), judged under an `AssistChoice` (`withAssistChoice` — `"max"` while only asked, a number once answered; `whyCannotCastSpell`'s `judgeAssist` takes that share off the total cost's generic before delve, convoke and mana, and `maxXByAssist` reaches the X it allows). A cast with `assist` goes to `requestAssist`, which raises the helper's `choose-number` with an `AssistAsk` (the cast, and the least the caster leaves) and emits `assist-requested`; `applyNumberChoice` hands the answer to `resumeAssistedCast`, which gives the caster priority back, re-checks the cast at that amount and either dispatches it (`castSpell` paying the helper's share first, `executePayment`'s `adding` merging what the spell records of mana spent) or emits `cast-reversed` (rule 733.1). Not for a `cast-now` cast. **Another player's choices as it's cast** (rules 601.2c, 700.2e — `otherChoice`, `requestOtherChoice`, `resumeOtherChoice`): a card with `opponentChoosesTargets` or `castModal.chosenBy`, or an activated ability with `opponentChoosesTargets`, is offered once per opponent (`otherChooser`, judged under `withOtherChoice` so a `controlledBy: "chooser"` filter reads them, their slots stripped from the offer); a cast or activation naming one parks as an `OtherChoiceAsk` on that opponent's `choose-targets` (`targetsFor` the caster) or `choose-modes`, and their answer re-dispatches it whole (`expandOtherChoice`) or reverses it (733.1) — a `cast-now` cast goes on under its offer (`carryOutCastNow`). The spell or ability keeps `otherChooser` for the 608.2b recheck and, for modes, as "that player" (an `about-player` around each mode). `meldByEffect` (2026-10-31): the `meld` effect — `meldPartners`' test, `choose-permanents` among several counterparts, both exiled in one leave batch, then the pair's main card (the commander's, 903.3b, else `meldOf[0]`) moved onto the battlefield with `EnterOptions.melded` and the other card's object deleted, an entry announced (with the effect's `tapped` / `attacking`, a `MeldEntry`, it enters tapped and goes through `putIntoAttack` first, rule 508.4 — Mishra, Claimed by Gix, 2026-11-11); tokens, copies and cards of another pair stay in exile (701.42c). Leaving, `scatterMeldedCards` (beside `scatterMergedComponents`, through `placeComponentCard`) makes the other card, recorded in `leftWith`, which `placeInLibrary`, `flickerByEffect`, `exileByEffect`'s `exiledBy` and the exile's `exiledUntil`/`exiledWith` follow. `beginSearchInstead` (2026-10-31): a search's second choice, over its finds, of which go to `instead`'s destination (raised by `finishZoneChoice` once the first is done; carried out at once when it is no choice), the rest moved in the same batch by `moveChosenFromZone` (`searchRest`) and the library shuffled after both. Graveyard cast permissions (2026-10-31): `graveyardGrantsFor` reaches another player's graveyard for a one-shot grant on the card or a resolved `player-effect` whose `graveyards` include its owner (the priority offers then walk those graveyards too), spends a `once` one in `state.playerEffects` (`GraveyardGrantOption.playerEffect`), carries its `spendAs` (read by `spendAsFor` through `castGrantScope`, set around `castSpell` and `castSpellActions` so the cast's own named grant decides), and offers a card's own `mayPlaySelf` with its `PlayTerms` the same way as a permanent's `mayPlay` — `playCostsPayable` gates both (601.2h), `graveyardExileChoices` narrows `exileOthers` by `exileFilter`, `graveyardPermissionCost` prices the alternative `mana` and the `costIncreasePerOther` in `castCostString`, the `discard` is paid after the mana as retrace's; `playOptions` lets a `fromExile` card's owner cast it from exile (Squee, the Immortal). Granted casting abilities (2026-10-31): `tapPayKinds` reads convoke and improvise printed or from a `grantsToSpells` static (`castGrantReaches`), and `tapsToPay`, `improvisesOnly`, `convokeCandidates`, `maxConvokeFor`, `resolveConvoke` and `whyCannotConvoke` take the card and caster, a spell with both letting creatures convoke and artifacts improvise (`convokesForColor`, the offer's `alsoImprovise`); `alternativeCostOf` offers a granted prowl (`grantedCastCost`'s `"prowl"`) under the printed one's condition and returns its `keyword`; `castingCostOf` takes an impulse permission's `undaunted` off. Attach hosts: `readyToEnterAttached` takes an `EnterAttachHost` (a player — `enchantPlayer`; `among` — left to the Aura's enchant choice, filtered, or the Equipment's `attach-host` enter choice) and runs before an entry's "as this enters" choices in every path (put, return-from-graveyard, zone choice); `attachableHosts` backs `attach`'s `target: { among }`; `canEquip`/`canEquipEntering` read the static `attachOnlyTo` (rule 301.5c). `spendNextSpellGrants` hands each unused `next` grant the spell it matches as that spell becomes cast (rule 601.2i — before the spell-cast event, so a granted cascade triggers on it), putting its `entersWithCounters` on the spell; `tapPayKinds` reads a turn grant's `convoke`; an `entersWithCounters` entry of kind `"sunburst"` becomes +1/+1 or charge counters by the copiable types as the spell resolves (Solar Array) (2026-10-31). A kicker's non-mana `also` takes `returnToHand` (paid with `payReturnToHandCost`, as an alternative cost's) and `tapUntapped` (`spellTapCost`, the kicked variant's `tapCost`, as teamwork's); `additionalCost.payLifePerTarget` makes `costDependsOnTargets` true and is checked against `targetCount` in `whyCannotCastSpell` (2026-10-31). `alternativeCostOf` gates an alternative cost's `discard` on the hand (besides the spell), and the cast pays it with `discardByEffect` (the filtered card, then `others`) and a `revealHand` with `revealCards`; an `exileFromHand.manaValueX` variant is offered once per mana value in hand (`perX`), counts as an X spell (`hasX`), and its behold filter takes `manaValue` = X (2026-10-31). Every cost's discard (an ability's `cost.discard`, a spell's additional, option, kicker, alternative, jump-start, retrace, permission and `byDiscarding` discards) is begun through `payCostDiscard(stackObject, decisionSource, pay)`, which sets `costDiscardFor` so a discard made at once is recorded on that spell or ability by `discardCards` (`recordCostDiscard` → `LastKnownRefs.discarded`), and one asked or queued carries `forCost` on its `discard` decision or `pendingDiscards` entry until `applyDiscard` / `promptNextDiscard` make it; `makeResolutionContext` hands them on as `ctx.discarded`, read by the `"discarded"` `AmountRef` and the `discarded` condition (2026-11-01). A cost's reveal from the hand is remembered the same way: `beginBehold`'s `forCost` (the spell, or the ability's stack object) rides on the behold record, and `carryOutBehold` → `recordCostReveal` puts each revealed card on it (`LastKnownRefs.revealed`, with its hand stint and its power, toughness and mana value there); `makeResolutionContext` hands them on as `ctx.revealed` for the `"revealed"` `AmountRef`, its `powerOf` / `toughnessOf` / `manaValueOf` reading the recorded numbers once the card has left the hand (`revealedGone`). `beholdCandidates` with `BeholdSpec.shareColor` offers only cards with a colour `count` of them have, and `beginBehold` asks them with the `together` rule `"share"` over their colours (Illuminated Folio). A static's granted ability names its granter (rule 201.5a): `grantedActivatedEntries` and `effectiveTriggeredEntries` put `granter`/`granterStint` on the `"static"` `GrantedAbilityRef` (`grantKey` leaves them out of the copies-of-one-ability keys), the activation and `mintTriggerAbility`'s placement copy it to `LastKnownRefs.granter` (`granterOfRef`), and `makeResolutionContext` hands it on as `ctx.granter`/`granterLost` for the `"granter"` `ObjectRef`, a damage's `from: "granter"` (`damageSource`, as it last existed) and `stintOf`'s reads; a `sacrifice: "granter"` cost is paid by `sacrificeCandidates`' granter branch (`granterNow`: still that permanent, the activator's) (2026-11-01). A cost keyed to a spell's targets (`targetedBySpellsCost`' life and mana, printed or granted, on the permanent named or read through `targeting`) is `targetingCost`, behind the `targetedCostsInPlay` gate; `castingCostOf` adds its mana for the chosen targets, or `targetTaxProbe` while `targetTaxOffer` finds what an offer's caster can afford (`targetTaxes` on the offer, `xCost.targetTaxes`; `target-count.ts`' `targetsWithinTaxes` and `maxXForTargets` are the drivers' read); an activation's is `abilityCostModification.targeting` (`abilityTaxReaches`: its target options judged at each target's price, an X ability offered once per X) (2026-11-01). A discard's cards move through `moveDiscardedCards`, one batch whether held or not: a `discardedByOpponentEnters` card whose discard's `cause` was an opponent enters by `enterHeldEntries` (which now carries `withCounters`, as its suspended `held-entries` does), before the `cards-discarded` event that still names it (2026-11-01). Casting from other zones (2026-11-02): `handsOpenTo`/`inHandOpenTo` read `PlayerEffect.playFromHandsOf` for a `via: "other-hand"` cast or a land play (rule 601.3; Sen Triplets); `playOptions` reads a permanent's a linked `mayPlay` over its linked exile (rule 607.2a; Idol of Endurance), one grant spent per cast, beside `playOptions`; `playOptions` also answers a card's `mayPlaySelf`; `returnUntilLeft` honours `exiledUntil.returnTo: "graveyard"`; the `exileFromHand` context method is `exile`'s `fromHand` (Planeswalker's Mischief). `eachDealsDamageByEffect` takes `to: "host"`, each dealer to its own attachment (Aura Barbs); the `conditionMet` closure answers `host-now`, the source's host now or as it last was (Consuming Ferocity). `turnExiledFaceDown` is `exileFromLibrary`'s and `exileGraveyard`'s face-down step. `delayedTriggerFires` reads a `your-…` timing against `DelayedTrigger.turnOf ?? controller`; `beginTurn` marks `your-next-turn-end-step`'s `turnBegan` as that turn starts and drops it as the next does, so it fires on that turn's end step or never. A token copy (`createTokenCopy`) is planned first (`PlannedTokenCopies`), and `oneTokenEvent` holds one instruction's plans (`withTokenCopyEvent`). If any has something to settle as it enters (`tokenCopySettlesAsItEnters`: `nextEnterChoice`, a land's life or damage), all of them are minted off the battlefield (`mintEnteringToken`, `zone: "outside"` in no list), asked one at a time (`PendingEntry` `token-copies`, `enterTokenCopies`) and moved on together through `moveObject`, which keeps what an `enteringToken` was made as; a Clone's copy replaces a token's copiable values (rule 707.3). One that can't enter was never created (303.4g). Otherwise they're minted as before, stacks and all. A copy effect's `withCounters` (Littjara Mirrorlake, Ochre Jelly, 2026-11-11) rides both paths: minted, it's one of the entering replacement's counters (one object a token); entering through `moveObject`, it's put on before the entry is announced. `askTurnBegin`, first thing in `beginTurn`: reads through the turns that would begin, without changing anything, and stops to ask (`choose-creature-type`'s `turnSkip`) a `would-begin-turn` replacement's "you may" or whether a skip owed goes on a turn skipped anyway (rule 616.1); `answerTurnBegin` runs `beginTurn` again, and `skipsTurn` carries it out (`turnBeginAnswers`, `untapAsNextTurnBegins` for rule 614.10b). `CardDefinition.entersUnderChosenPlayer` (rule 614.1d) overrides `enter.under` in `moveObjectUncached` from the as-enters `chosenPlayer` and makes the default-control effect itself at the end of the branch, and `gainControlByEffect` ignores a later `entered` from the effect that put it there. `emptySeatOptional` makes a triggered ability's `whose: { seat }` slot optional only past the table or for a player who has left (Turf War), so a seated player with nothing to target still removes it (603.3d). `beginLibrarySearch` is the one way into a search, so it applies `cantSearchLibraries`: no library cards eligible, the shuffle kept only where the card says "search your library, then shuffle", not "if you search your library this way". `ignoreStaticEffect` is the `ignore-static-effect` special action. `land-played` names its land as the trigger object and its player as a trigger's `"trigger-player"` (an intervening if on the land played, Cemetery Protector; Horn of Greed's "that player"). `beginZoneChoice` asks nothing of a graveyard where nothing may be chosen and nothing else follows. `heldChoices.entries` and the `held-entries` entry carry `enterAs` (Ghouls' Night Out's cards enter together as black Zombies) and `tapped`; a library search's find is held too, still in its library, and `heldChoices.shuffles` shuffles after the entries (an `each-player-may` over a `search-library` asks everyone, then searches from `EachPlayerMayProgress.searching`, rule 101.4; an effect in `ASKS_NOTHING` — a draw, a discarded hand, life, a token, counters — is done for all of them once the last has answered, `EachPlayerMayProgress.doing` holding who's left if one's replacement stops to ask); `GameState.searchShufflesOwed` is what `search-library`'s `shufflesLater` leaves for a `shuffle-library` of `searchers`; `askVote` narrows a permanent question to its `about` player and names them in its prompt. `putFaceDownAs`, one card manifested or cloaked from any zone (`manifestTop` and a `look-and-choose`'s `"manifest"` or `"cloak"` destination go through it, the latter out of an enter batch: one at a time, rules 701.40e, 701.58e); `cloakExiledPile`; `exileIntoPiles`; `completeFlickerReturn`'s `faceDown`. `addTimeCounterInExile` and `removeTimeCounter` over a suspended card or one with `GameObject.timeCast` (Alaundo), whose last counter fires `timeCastTrigger`; `removeTimeCountersInExile`, `timeCountersOnSuspended`. `refreshStaticNames` at each state-based check: `GameObject.staticName`, the name a static gives (`setName`); `become`'s `setName` and `addSupertypes` go on a modifier that isn't copiable. Shared plays: `newPermissionGroup`, `permissionGroupOpen` and `spendPermissionGroup` over `GameState.permissionGroups` — a graveyard card's own permission (`graveyardGrantsFor` offers a grouped one after the resolved ones, `spendGraveyardGrant` spends it) and an impulse (`playOptions` refuses at none left; a cast or a land played under it spends one) — and `prunePermissionGroups` as the untap step begins. The cleanup step leaves the damage on a phased-in permanent with a `keepsDamageInCleanup` static (`StaticAbility`, rule 514.2 — Ancient Adamantoise).
 **A spell gaining an ability**: `grantKeyword` and `grantTriggered` take a permanent spell, their modifier a `castRider` the permanent keeps (rule 400.7a); `rebounds` reads a `"rebound"` keyword as well as the card's `rebound`; `spell.entersWithCounters` entries may carry a `condition` asked as it enters. **Riot granted** (`enteringKeywordInstances` in `characteristics.ts`): what a card about to enter will have that its own text doesn't give it (rule 614.12), asked under the player it will enter under (`TargetView.enteringUnder`, `FilterContext.enteringUnder`), one `enterChoice.riot` pick per instance (702.136b). **An alternative cost's other parts**: `castAlsoCosts` adds its own `alternativeCost.also` (a `KickerAlsoCost`) to a keyword's and a splice's, so `whyCannotPayAlso`, `queueAlsoCosts`, `spellSacrificeParts` and `spellTapCost` pay it with no second shape; the cast records which of its own costs it was (`GameObject.alternativeCostPaid`, `"printed"` or `"madness"`, kept by a copy), for the `self-alternative-cost-paid` condition. **Granted alternative costs** (`alternativeCostsOf`, rule 118.9): `alternativeCostGrantsReaching` is the caster's `alternativeCostForSpells` statics and any other player's `anyPlayer` ones (Aluren); `fromHand` and `fromZones` limit where from; the `filter` is judged through `withFace` as the spell it would be, and against the permanent (`filterAmounts`), so As Foretold's counters read. **Cycling that asks for more** (rule 702.29a, `CyclingAbility.also`, an `{X}` in `cost`): the plain `cycle` action names only the card, so such a cost is listed by `handGrantedActivated` and activated as any ability is; `noteCycled` counts it (`cardsCycledThisTurn`) and emits `card-cycled` with its X, a `this-cycled` trigger's `triggerValue`. **Plot** (rule 702.170): the `plot` special action (`whyCannotPlot`/`plotCard`, sorcery timing) exiles a card from hand and `makePlotted` marks it (`GameObject.plottedOnTurn`, cleared by `moveObject`, a `card-plotted` event that fires its own `this-plotted` triggers from exile);
