`sameNameAsSource: boolean` (Marvin, Murderous Mimic's "creatures that don't have the same name as this creature") compares with `ctx.source` as it is now, a nameless object sharing no name (a static's filter: an effect's matching has no `source`); `sameNameWith: ShareReference` ("the same name as" any object the reference names, itself included — Cylian Sunsinger's `"source"`, Ixalan's Binding's and Exclusion Ritual's `"exiled-with-source"` — linked by `exiledWith` or exiled until it leaves, `exiledUntil` — Cornered Market's `{ permanents }`, Pattern Matcher's `{ controlled }`, Pyromancer Ascension's `{ graveyard }`, with `itsController` for Dragonlord Kolaghan's "their graveyard") is bound in an effect to `sameNameAsAny` by `bindDynamicCompares` and read live elsewhere by `liveReferenceObjects` (source, exiled-with-source, controlled, permanents, graveyard; the rest match nothing unbound), failing closed in the layer fold; `sameNameAsAnother: CardFilter` is Eye of Singularity's "the same name as another permanent", read live against the battlefield, a token stack its own namesake; a bound name matches either half of a split card (`namesOf`), and `nameForMatching` names a permanent gone since as it last existed; `ResolutionContext.permanentsMatching` reads with the source, and `cardsExiledWithSource(orUntil)` can take the "until" exiles too; `sameNameAsTriggerObject: true` (the Odyssey Shrines' "cards in all graveyards with the same name as that spell") is bound the same way, to the trigger object and its name; `sameNameWith: { thisWay: kind }` (Haunting Echoes' "for each card exiled this way … with the same name as that card") is bound to `sameNameAsAny`, the names of the set's members; `sharesColorWith: n` (radiance's "target X and each other X that shares a color with it", rule 105.2) likewise, to `sharesColorWith: { object, colors }` — the target itself and its colors (`ResolutionContext.colorsOf`). `attachedToObject: n` (2026-11-01 — "all Auras attached to it", "all Equipment attached to that creature") likewise, to `attachedToRef` (the slot's `TargetRef`, `null` for an illegal or empty slot), matched on the battlefield against `attachedTo` / `attachedToPlayer`; unbound it matches nothing. `wasAttachedTo: "source" | "trigger-object"` (2026-11-07 — Zack Fair, Rhuk) is bound to `wasAttachedToAny`, the `AttachmentRecord`s from the context's `attachmentsOf` (`LastKnownInfo.attachments` once it has left, what's attached now while it's there), matched by object and stint (400.7). `attachedToPlayer: "you" | "opponent"` is an Aura on a player (rule 303.4 — Witchbane Orb's Curses). Clauses about a card's own properties: `basePower`/`baseToughness` (after layer 7b — `Characteristics.basePower`), `hasManaAbility` and `hasAbilities` (`characteristics.ts`'s `hasManaAbility`/`hasAnyAbility`, granted abilities included), `xInManaCost`/`coloredManaSymbols` (the up face's own mana cost — a transformed back face has none), `cardTypeCount` and `nameDiffersFromEach` (an inner filter over the battlefield, rule 201.2c). `hasKicker` (a kicker or multikicker ability, read off its rules text in any zone) and `kicked` (a spell on the stack whose kicker was paid, rule 702.33d — set as its cost is paid, so a cost reduction or a spend restriction can't ask it). Subtype clauses ask `subtypes.ts`'s `hasSubtype`, so a changeling matches every creature type, and `ofChosenType` reads the chosen creature type of the `FilterContext.source` (static scopes, trigger filters and a `look-and-choose`'s filter pass one — Herald's Horn); a source that has left the battlefield is read through `lastKnown.chosenCreatureType` (rule 608.2h), since the move cleared its choice. The shared "which objects" vocabulary for mass effects, tutors, and filtered triggers — `ZoneChoiceFilter` is an alias. A numeric clause's `n` may be a `DynamicOperand`: `{ own }` (the matched object's own power/toughness/mana value) or `{ amount: EffectAmount }`, answered by `FilterContext.amount` — `Game.filterAmounts` supplies it to target filters (via `TargetSource.amount`, rechecked on resolution) and trigger filters, and `applyEffectSpec` binds an effect's filters to plain numbers as that effect applies (`bindDynamicCompares`); with nothing to answer it, the comparison fails closed. Answers type/subtype/colour clauses lazily via `effectiveTypes`/`effectiveSubtypes` (layer 4 alone — `characteristics.ts`'s `layerFour`) and `effectiveColors`; only a `power`/`toughness`/`keyword` clause needs the full characteristics fold (kept cheap deliberately — a `StaticAbility.condition` re-runs `matchesFilter` over the whole battlefield on every read). `FilterContext.layered` is how a static's `filter` scope is matched from *inside* the fold computing its target: types/subtypes/keywords as folded so far, and a power/toughness clause fails closed. Also the shared **weighted** scan behind "other" and aggregates: `weightedMatches` weighs each permanent by its `stackCount` and drops one permanent per excluded id (so "other" beside a stacked source leaves the rest of the stack counted), and `AggregateSpec`/`aggregateOver` sum or max power/toughness/mana value over it, or with `aggregate: "distinct"` count the different values of a `DistinctOf` (mana value, mana cost, power, name by `distinctNameOf`, colour pair, permanent type, subtypes of a type — a changeling every creature type), or with `aggregate: "count"` count them, `of: "counters"` summing every counter of every kind; its set comes from one `aggregateMatches` (2026-10-31): `in` the battlefield, graveyards (`graveyardMatches`), exile (a face-down card only for an owner-only filter, rule 406.3a) or an `ObjectSetRef` (`object-sets.ts`: the members a resolution hands it through `AggregateReads.members`, each read live or by its `lastKnown` snapshot carried on the `WeightedMatch`; a static's own sets through `AggregateReads.source`), a list of zones, and `plus` sets with filters of their own, each object once; `AggregateReads` also carries the excluded ids, cards still counting as in a graveyard (an entering replacement, rule 614.12) and the layer fold's own battlefield matcher; an effect amount's `forTarget` reads it from a target player's side. The `EffectAmount`, the `aggregate`/`source-greatest`/`controls-greatest` `StaticCondition`s, the `CostReductionAmount`, a CDA's `CountSpec` and `grantPtPerCount.aggregate` all read through it (2026-10-30). `isSource` is "is the filter's own source" — inside `attachedTo`, "Equipment attached to it". `greatestAmongItsController: { of, among }` is "with the greatest power among creatures that player controls" — a per-controller maximum, ties all matching, never inside the fold (Crackling Doom, Soul Shatter, Will of the Abzan). `controllerIn` (engine-set, never authored) is "controlled by one of these players" — a `grant-protection` filter's "your opponents" fixed as it resolves. `not` is a whole-filter negation, matched like `anyOf` ("can't be blocked except by creatures with flying or reach", B253). `damagedThisTurnBy.source` (`"this"` / `"host"`) names one source by its battlefield stint (`damagingStint`: on the battlefield now, or as it last was there), matched against `DamageSourceRecord.sourceZoneChangeCount` — Sengir Vampire (2026-10-29). `controlledBy: "chooser"` reads `FilterContext.chooser` — the player choosing a target for another (rule 601.2c), passed from `TargetSource.chooser`. `printedManaCost` of a melded permanent is its cards' front faces' costs joined (202.3c, 712.8g), a copy of one its combined face's none (2026-10-31). `ofChosenColor` (2026-10-31): the colour chosen for `FilterContext.source` as it entered (`chosenOnEnter`), its `lastKnown` once it has left (608.2h), beside `ofChosenType`; `fixChosenColor` turns it into a fixed colour for a filter kept past its resolution (a prevention shield's "source of your choice of the chosen color"). `blockingRelation` (2026-10-31): a creature blocking the source or its host (`of: "host"`), blocked by it, or either (509.1g-h) — `inBlockingRelation` over `blockedAttackersOf` (now in `state.ts`, re-exported by `combat/banding.ts`, so `filter.ts` reads it without an import cycle); a partner that has left is read off its `lastKnown.blockedAttackers`/`blockers` (608.2b, 608.2h), a creature removed from combat is in none (506.4); `bindDynamicCompares` binds the partner as `with` at resolution. `inSet: ObjectSetRef` (2026-11-08; `triggerBatch` before it, 2026-11-02): one of a set's members — `"trigger-batch"` is "those creatures", the objects the resolving trigger fired on — bound to `amongObjects` from `ResolutionContext.objectSet`, each with its stint (400.7), so a `delayed-trigger`'s `at` keeps them; unbound it matches nothing.
- **`player-control.ts`** — who answers for whom (rule 723; "you choose which creatures attack / block"; 2026-11-01). Since 2026-11-05 a `PlayerControl` can be `during: "search"` (Opposition Agent, `Game.beginSearchControl`): a turn's machinery ignores it, and it ends as the search's `choose-from-zone` is answered. `Game` asks a *player* for every decision and the action names that player, so nothing in the rules reads this file: the spell cast for them is theirs, the mana and the discard too (723.3, 723.5a). `askedPlayer` (the player a decision is put to) and `answeringPlayer` (who makes it: a `combatChoices` chooser for the declarations it names, which takes precedence, else `decidingPlayer`, following a chain of `controlOf`s, the last created working, 723.1a; one whose controller left ended, 800.4a). `GameState.playerControl` holds `PlayerControl`s oldest first: they wait for the controlled player's next turn or combat phase (`began`, set by `Game.beginPlayerControl` as it actually starts, so a skipped turn waits, 723.1b) and go as the next turn begins or that combat ends; `extraTurnAfter` comes due whichever worked. Read by `Game`'s controller loop (`controller.standInFor`: the answering controller's stand-in, `StandInController` by default, which passes and blocks only what it must, or `EvalStandIn`, each decision the best one step ahead for the bot), by the server, and by `viewFor`. **`viewFor`** merges the controlled player's own view into the controller's (`withControlledSight`, 723.4: not their sideboard, so a wish made for them finds nothing there, its ruling) and adds `playerControl`, `perspective` (`perspectiveOf`, the seat the client draws from) and `answering`. Attackers under a chooser go in two steps (`AwaitingDecision.attackers.group`, the 2008 ruling): the chooser names the group, one defender a creature so nothing asks; the active player sends it when that leaves them anything to say (`Game.sendingAttackersIsAChoice`: several defenders, a cost, a band), and one they won't pay for goes back to the chooser without it (`attackChoiceRefused`). No new decision kind.
- **`goad.ts`** — `goadersOf`/`isGoaded`: every player who has goaded a creature (rule 701.15), whichever way — a one-shot goad (`GameObject.goadedBy`, until the goader's next turn), one for the rest of the game (`goadedForGameBy`), or a static one (`StaticAbility.goads` — Baeloth Barrityl, an Aura's "enchanted creature is goaded" — read live, its source's controller the goader). The attack checks, the `goaded` filter clause and last-known information (`LastKnownInfo.goaders`) all read through it, so a goad is never asked about half-way. Goaded is a designation, not an ability: nothing here is in the layer fold.
- **Soulbond** (rule 702.95, no module of its own): `GameObject.pairedWith` on both creatures, each naming the other, cleared by `moveObject`; `characteristics.ts`'s `pairedPartner` is the only reader (both on the battlefield, mutual, one controller), behind the `paired` filter clause, the `{ scope: "paired" }` static scope and the view's `pairedWith`; the `pair` effect (`ResolutionContext.pairWithSource`, 702.95c-d) pairs, `Game.unpairBrokenPairs` in the state-based loop ends a pairing for good once 702.95e breaks it (left, changed control, stopped being a creature), and a paired token never folds. `cards/helpers.ts`'s `soulbond()` is the keyword's two triggers.
- **`ring.ts`** — the Ring tempts you (rule 701.54), the pure half: `isRingBearer`/`ringBearerOf`/`hasRingBearerDesignation` over the `GameObject.ringBearer` designation (cleared on a zone change and whenever another player gains control of it — `Game.emit`'s `control-changed` hook — and when another creature becomes that player's Ring-bearer), `ringEmblemText(count)`, and `RING_EMBLEM_TRIGGERS`, the Ring emblem's three later abilities, each gated by a `ring-tempted` trigger condition (the count only grows, so asking it as the event happens equals the ability having arrived). The first ability is read where it's asked: `supertypesOf` makes the Ring-bearer legendary, `combat/eligibility.ts` keeps creatures with greater power from blocking it. `Game`'s `ringTemptBegin`/`becomeRingBearer`/`ringTemptFinish` apply the `the-ring-tempts-you` effect: the emblem (an `EmblemState` with `ring: true`, its text rewritten as the count rises), a `choose-permanents` of one creature, then the `ring-tempted` event (`chosen`, `count`) that `ring-tempts` triggers read; `PlayerState.ringTemptations` holds the count.
- **`subtypes.ts`** — `LANDWALK` (each landwalk keyword and the land type it walks, read by the fold and by combat), subtype lists (creature types fit a creature or a kindred — rule 205.3m, `subtypeFitsTypes`; the `kindred` `CardType`, 2026-10-11) and their two markers, `EVERY_CREATURE_TYPE` (changeling, rule 702.73a) and `EVERY_LAND_TYPE`: one entry standing for every type of its kind, so a changeling's list is `["Shapeshifter", EVERY_CREATURE_TYPE]` rather than ~300 names in every snapshot, mana unit and type line. **Every subtype question goes through `hasSubtype(list, subtype)`, never `includes`** — filters, lords, amass, mana provenance. Asked *for* a marker it means "any type of that kind", which is how "shares a creature type with" a changeling works (Path of Ancestry). `withoutTypeMarkers` is what a type line shows (`viewFor` strips them; the changeling keyword icon says it instead). Changeling adds its marker as the first step of layer 4, in every zone (`characteristics.ts`'s `withChangeling`); an effect adds either marker as an ordinary `addSubtypes` entry. `isBasicLandType` and `BASIC_LAND_TYPE_CHOICES` ("choose a basic land type", B253).
- **`target-polarity.ts`** — which side of the table a target belongs on, for the bots. `slotPolarities(effect, count)` reads each target slot of an effect tree as `harm` (bad for whoever controls the target), `help`, `take` (yours whoever's it is — reanimation, a copied spell) or `either`, through a rule per effect kind that is **total over `EffectSpec["kind"]`**, so a new kind fails the build until someone says which side it belongs on; effects that rebind target 0 (`look-and-choose`, `reveal-top`, `reveal-until`, `choose-permanents`) or choose their own (`reflexive-trigger`) aren't descended into. The strongest effect on a slot decides: removal and theft outrank the consolation they give their victim (Swords to Plowshares' life, Generous Gift's Elephant), a major effect a minor one (Sign in Blood's cards outrank its life). Every query takes an optional `PolarityBias` (a deck's own reading of some effect kinds, from `deck-bias.ts`), which overrides the rule for each slot those kinds touch. `castPolarities`/`abilityPolarities` (memoized per definition, one memo per bias), `auraPolarity` (an Aura's `attached` statics, `controlEnchanted`, Curses; an Aura that goads harms, whatever else it grants — Psychic Impetus; with nothing in its statics, its own `who: "attached"` triggers — Bestial Fury's pump, False Demise's return, Corrupted Roots' life loss for the enchanted land's controller — and any trigger that destroys what it enchants, Yoke of the Damned), `offerPolarities` for a `legalActions` cast or activation, `offerDamage` for the damage each of its slots is dealt (a fixed amount, or X at the offer's most), which `rankTargets` takes so a harmful slot's damage is aimed first at an opponent it kills (rule 704.5a; their whole board goes with them, 800.4a — not one who can't lose life or the game), then at what it kills and at players, then at what it only scratches (gone at cleanup, rule 514.2), `pendingTargetPolarities` for a `choose-targets` decision (reflexive, chapter and announced-modal triggers included), `specSide` (a spec that names the side is the card choosing, not something to second-guess), and `targetValue`/`rankTargets`/`onlyWrongSide` — a cheap static value that orders a slot's options best-first (a targeted `lose-game` harms its target). About 97% of the pool's targeted slots classify (a ratchet in its test). No `bot/` import, so decision modules may use it. See `docs/plans/bot-effect-knowledge.md`. A `look-and-choose` over `"targets"` and a `cast-now` from them take every slot; a `damage`'s or `damage-all`'s `from` target is the caster's (help). `behold` touches no slot (its `then` rebinds target 0); a `blight` whose `who` is `{ target: n }` harms that player. `become-saddled` helps. `increase-speed` touches no slot. `move-counters` harms its `from` slot a little and helps its `to` slot (the other way round for a harmful named kind); `put-counters-of` helps its target. `phase-out` is a minor `harm` (a blocker gone for a turn), on a `controlledByTarget` player too.
- **`effect-worth.ts`** — the other half of `target-polarity.ts`: once targets are chosen (or where there are none), what doing an effect is worth to one player, as a coarse signed number (a card about 2, a point of life a quarter of one above 15 and three quarters below, removal 4). A linked exile of one of your own cards ("exile it *with this*", Colfenor's Urn, Bag of Holding) is worth half a card, so v1 takes that "may". `effectWorth(effect, {state, me, controller, targets, source?, triggerObject?, triggerPlayer?})` reads the targeted slots through `slotStrengths` against the targets as chosen, and the untargeted parts off the effect's player scope for the kinds nearly every optional clause is made of (draws — a draw the library can't cover reads as losing —, life, discards, sacrifices, tokens and token copies of our own, tutors, recursion, a sacrifice-source); anything else is 0, unknown rather than bad. Something done to an opponent is split across the opponents. `costWorth` prices a choice's mana, life and energy. What v1 answers a "you may", a punisher's "unless", a villainous or modal choice, and which activated ability to use with; it only has to get the sign and the order between a cost and what it buys right, since v2 searches each of those and keeps v1's answer only when nothing beats it. Targeted slots read with `me`'s deck bias. No runtime `bot/` import. `win-game` and an untargeted `lose-game` outweigh everything (±100, as decking does), so a pact's upkeep payment is paid whenever it can be. `behold` is worth only its `then` (revealing or choosing costs nothing), and a `blight` aimed at a player slot is read off `targets`. `increase-speed` is worth a quarter of a card a step to its controller, and the step to max speed (rule 702.179e) a whole card; nothing past it. An untargeted `move-counters` end (graft's "from this onto it") is worth a point to whoever gains the counter and half that lost to whoever gives it up, so a graft onto your own creature is taken and onto an opponent's declined; `put-counters-of` onto an untargeted end is a point to its controller. A `grant-protection` with a fixed quality is worth, beyond its slot's help, an opposing spell or ability on the stack aimed at the permanent that the quality would stop (removal's worth) and a little per opposing creature it matches (`protectionWorth`, which needs `WorthContext.cards`): what v1 picks "the color of your choice" by. Life lost or damage of `{ manaValueOf: slot }` reads the card in that slot (`sizeHere`), so a Sin Prodder yes that would be lethal reads as it is.
- **`card-flow.ts`** — `cardFlowGain`: how many cards up a spell's effect leaves its caster, if it only draws and filters their own cards (draws, scries, surveils, looks that keep or put back, a discard after a draw, a tutor into hand, a Treasure beside the cards, an extra land drop), else `null`; `isCardFlow` is the yes/no. Moved out of `controller.ts` (2026-11-10) so `bot/hand-worth.ts` can price a card by it without importing the controller. Pure over a definition.
- **`bot/hand-worth.ts`** — what a card in the bot's own hand is worth beside an average card (the audit's Q2, 2026-11-10): `handQuality` is the evaluation's feature of that name (the sum of each card's quality less 1, at `hand`'s weight) and `handCardQuality` one card's, which v1's `chooseFromZone` ranks a search into hand by. Quality is 1 for an average card, 0.5 to 1.5: card flow 0.4 a card netted (under any card it could draw, because rollouts read the real library and a cantrip priced at an average card was held on a poor top card), less a discard cost; a tutor 0.85 of the best library cards its filter matches (`matchesFilter`; a tutor found by a tutor reads as 1, so no recursion); another untargeted instant or sorcery 0.85 of `effectWorth` over 2; a creature a fifth of its board terms, never under 1 with an ability; a land 1.25 as the first in hand below `landsWanted`, 1 behind it, 0.5 at or past it; anything with targets or an additional cost, and a noncreature permanent, 1. Then reach: 15% off per mana past lands + 1, to half, by the cheapest face. No cache: it reads the state (lands, library, `effectWorth`'s life), and a cache keyed on first sight would break replay.
- **`bot/interaction.ts`** — which bot actions are interaction, and whom each is aimed at (the audit's Q4, 2026-11-10): `aimedAt` reads a cast or activation with a target an opponent controls (a permanent or a spell on the stack) as aimed at that opponent, and one whose effect tree has a mass removal kind (`destroy-all`, `exile-all`, `damage-all`, `return-to-hand-all`, `sacrifice-all`, `sacrifice-all-but`) as aimed at `"all"`; `aimedByLine` does it for a planned line's steps, each on the board it was made from. `evaluate.ts`'s `aimedOutcome` marks such an outcome with how the players aimed at stood in the decision's pass (`EvalOutcome.hit`, beside `players`), and `scoreOutcome` then counts what the hardest-hit of them lost at `opponent` in full rather than by standings. Read by `eval-bot.ts`'s `score`, `scoreAlone` and the planner's `value` hook (`TurnPlanHooks.value(state, line?)`); `scenario-fit.ts` gets the outcome as scored through the trace's third argument.
- **`scry-pick.ts`** — what a bot scries or surveils away (`scryAway`), judged top card first, each knowing what was kept above it: a land stays while the bot has fewer than seven among its battlefield, its hand and the lands kept above, or when it makes a colour the bot can't make (lands in hand counted as made); a spell goes when its mana value is more than three beyond the bot's lands and mana rocks (two fewer for a colour it can't make); surveil also bins a card castable from the graveyard. v1's answer, and v2's in a tie — which is nearly always, since a scry changes the next draw and a rollout to the end of the turn rarely reaches it. No `bot/` import.
- **`land-colors.ts`** — which colours a bot needs (its commander's identity, or with none the coloured pips in its hand) and has no permanent to make (`missingColors`, from printed mana abilities and basic land types), and `newColorsFirst`, which orders a choice among lands alone so those making a missing colour come first: v1's `chooseFromZone` and v2's capped candidate order (`byManaValue`) for a fetch, a Cultivate, a Farseek; and `colorToName`, the colour v1 names for "as this enters, choose a colour" (Valgavoth's Lair, the Thriving lands — asked as a creature-type choice over colours, `allColors`): within the commander's identity, the one the hand's pips want most, ten times over when nothing in play makes it. A permanent that taps for its chosen colour reads as making that colour once it has one. No `bot/` import.
- **`deck-bias.ts`** — what a deck wants that the bots' generic card knowledge gets backwards: `COMMANDER_BIASES`, keyed by commander name, each entry a `why`, a `polarity` (`PolarityBias`: effect kinds whose target slots the deck aims the other way — Teval's `mill: "help"`) and `ownWeights` (evaluation weights for the deck's *own* features only; opponents stay on the shared ones). `deckBias(state, player)` reads the commanders the player owns off the `GameState`, merges a pair's entries, and caches the answer per `state.objects` table (once per game, once per search clone), so v1, v2, every seat of v2's rollouts and a person's own Teval deck all get it without being told; `polarityBias` is the polarity half. Each entry is pinned by a gate scenario that fails without it. Only a type import from `bot/`. See `docs/plans/deck-biases.md`.
- **`text-change.ts`** — text-changing effects (rule 612, layer 3, 2026-10-31): a `TextChange` is one colour word or basic land type for another (`PtModifier.textChange`, written by the `change-text` effect's `Game.changeText` — Sleight of Mind, Magical Hack, Mind Bend, Crystal Spray; both words asked as it resolves through `choose-value`'s `"text-word"`, the `choose-creature-type` menu). `state.ts`'s `textChangesOf` lists an object's in timestamp order and `rulesTextName`/`textNameOf` then return `textChangedKey(base, changes)` (prefix `\u0002text\u0002`, wrapping its own, an exchanged or a merged name), which `CardRegistry.get` builds with `changeDefinitionText` and caches: colours in the colour-word fields (`COLOR_WORD_KEYS` — filters, protection, `setColors`/`addColors`, devotion, unspent mana; never `mana`/`oneOf`/`all`/`xColor`, a choice's options or a chosen value), every string that is a basic land type and every landwalk/landcycling keyword, the one colour shorthand target (`"nonblack-creature"`, rewritten as its filter), and the display strings (`text`, `prompt`, `label`) with the card's name masked (612.2); a face's printed colours/subtypes (an object with a `manaCost`) and the definition's top-level characteristics stay. `changeSubtypes` gives its type line's land types to `characteristics.ts`'s `printedSubtypes` before layer 4 (a Forest made an Island taps for {U} — `printedLandTypesHold` is false under a text change). A spell resolves and re-checks its targets by `rulesTextName` (`resolveTopObject`, `spellTargetSpecs`), and a permanent spell's change is a `castRider` (400.7a). `test/text-change.test.ts` audits the pool: colours only in known fields, the shorthand list, no `resolve` hatch naming a word. A token a changed ability creates takes the changed colour words (`create-token`'s `tokenTextChanges`, `tokenColorsAfter`, a copiable colour on the token: 111.3, 612.2). Not yet: Swirl the Mists' all-objects static, Sarpadian Empires' option words.
- **`this-way.ts`** — The `phased-out` kind (2026-11-11), off `permanents-phased-out`: each permanent, direct or indirect, by who controlled it as it went, read through its last-known snapshot while it's still out (Out of Time). The `countered` kind, off `spell-countered` (which now names its controller: a copy or ability is gone by then), each copy of an ability object one. The `amassed` kind: the Army an `amass` chose, off the `amassed` event (rule 701.47a), behind the `"amassed"` ref (`AttachRef`, `AmountRef`, `DamageFrom`).`damagePreventedBy(state, madeIn)`: what the shields a resolution made have prevented, off the `damage-prevented` events that name it (`PreventionShield.madeIn`, `shieldMadeIn`); a delayed ability reads its creator's (`DelayedTrigger.madeIn`, carried to `LastKnownRefs.madeIn` as it fires) — the `damagePreventedThisWay` amount, Sacred Boon. `keptOnTopSince`: the cards scries and surveils left on top, a count off the `scried` events, so which they are stays hidden (`keptOnTopThisWay`, Starving Revenant). `thisWayEntries(state, what, since?)` also reads `"gained-control"` (what really changed hands, off `control-changed`) and `"control-effect"` (what a control effect applied to, off the permanents' `ControlEffect.madeAtSeq`, since one on a permanent its player already controls logs nothing — Mob Rule's ruling), and marks a `"revealed"` entry so an aggregate over the set (`SetMember.shown`) can read it in a hidden zone: what the resolution under way (`GameState.resolutionSince`) has discarded, drawn, milled, sacrificed, destroyed (a destroy effect, not a death), destroyed and put into a graveyard (`"died"`), exiled, returned to a hand, put into a graveyard, put onto the battlefield from a zone or chosen in a `vote` (`"chosen"`, as each is chosen: Divine Reckoning's "destroy the rest" is `notThisWay`, Fortunate Few's "that hasn't been chosen" too, 2026-11-01), read off its own events — each object with whose it counts as, whether it left the battlefield doing so (so it's read as it last existed there) and how many tokens a stack that went whole stood for. Pure over `GameState`, so `filter.ts` can use it too: behind the `thisWay` amount, the `this-way` condition and the `thisWay` / `notThisWay` filter clauses ("a creature card milled this way"; Martial Coup's "all other creatures" after its Soldiers). `"put-on-bottom"` reads `cards-put-on-bottom`: the cards a choice put from a hand on the bottom of a library (Valakut Awakening's "draw that many"). `"tapped"` reads `permanent-tapped` events, a stack tapped whole as each of its tokens. `"counters-put"`: permanents a counter went on (`counter-added`) — Ripples of Potential's "had a counter put on them this way". `"counters-removed"`: each removal (`counter-removed`, a suspended card's `time-counter-removed`), counted by the counters it took (times its `tokens`, a stack's mass removal) — Garnet's "for each lore counter removed this way", Mister Hyde's "If you do". `"exiled-from-hand"`: cards exiled from a hand (`cards-put-into-exile`'s `from`), by their owner — Lost Legacy's "draws a card for each card exiled from their hand this way". `"revealed"`: cards revealed from a library (`cards-revealed`), by who revealed them — parley's "for each nonland card revealed this way" after a `reveal-top` with `who`. `"put-into-hand"` (2026-10-31) is read off the hands, not the log — no event announces a card arriving in a hand: every card in one whose `GameObject.handArrivalSeq` (stamped by `moveObject` on a move into a hand from another zone, cleared by `drawCardRaw` for a draw and by any later move) is at or after `since`, by its owner — Adventure Awaits' "if you didn't put a card into your hand this way". Beside it, read off the same events: `lifeLostSince` (the `lifeLostThisWay` amount), `excessDamageSince` (the excess damage past lethal or loyalty, rule 120.4a — the `excessDamageThisWay` amount and `excess-damage-this-way` condition) and `damageDealtSince` (the damage actually dealt, after prevention, to one player or object or to players only — the `damageDealtThisWay` amount and `damage-dealt-this-way` condition). A `this-way` condition's `acrossPasses` reads from `GameState.repeatSince`, where the `repeat` under way began (`ResolutionContext.scopeRepeat`). A `repeat` begins each pass's "this way" by moving `resolutionSince` to the pass (`ResolutionContext.scopeThisWay`) and moves it back once the last pass is done, so a condition after a pass reads that pass alone and what follows reads them all. `"chosen"` (2026-10-31): the cards picked in a `look-and-choose` (`cards-chosen-from-zone`), wherever they went — a processor's "If you do" under Rest in Peace. `playerCountersRemovedSince` (2026-10-31): the counters players lost (`player-counters-changed`, a negative delta), which the `thisWay: "counters-removed"` amount adds when it has no `filter` (rule 122.1 — Leeches).
- **`token-replacements.ts`** — `planTokenEvent`, pure: what one token-creation event comes to under a player's `would-create-token` replacements (rules 614.5, 616.1). The event is a list of kinds of token; each replacement that can apply changes it for the next, each once; the order searched is the one making the most tokens, with doublers multiplying the answer last, since last is never worse (a multiplier with `ofTokens`, Ojer Taq's, is in the search: it depends on what the event has become). `Game.tokenEventPlan` reads the replacements off the battlefield (a token stack's tokens one each) and `createTokens` / `createTokenCopy` mint the plan: `null` is the tokens the effect named, anything else a fresh token with only what the effect does to the tokens. `Game.withTokenEvent` (inside `simultaneously`, and `oneTokenEvent` around a copy-of-each loop) keeps a `plusOneToken` to one opportunity across an instruction's several kinds.
- **`token-fold.ts`** — the pure half of `Game.refoldSplitTokens`, folding tokens split off a stack back together mid-turn (docs/plans/token-stack-choices.md, "Folding back"). `exactTokenShape(o, state)`: a token object as one key-sorted JSON string minus `id`, `stackCount`, `splitFromStack` and `splitFrom` (the stack a token was split off, which its `permanent-left-battlefield` names as `stack` for the client while that stack stands; not walked by `referencedObjectIds` either) — stricter than `state.ts`'s `tokenFoldKey`, which the end-of-turn fold compares, because mid-turn what happened to a token this turn (attacked, damage dealt to it, abilities used) still tells it apart. `referencedObjectIds(state)`: every object id the rest of the state names — other objects' fields, delayed triggers, shields, anything waiting, and this turn's journal entries by object (not `damage`, `counters-put` or `tapped`, whose per-token records `exactTokenShape` compares instead, `PER_OBJECT_KINDS`)) — leaving out the zone lists, the event log, each object's own `id`, cards in a library, the snapshots of things that left a zone (`lastOnStack`, `lastKnown`, `earlierLastKnown`, `ceasedTokens`, `departedAbilities`: read only by something already waiting, and the fold runs once nothing is) and the history kept only to count (`damageDealt`). A named token is never folded away, only into; and a token that attacked this turn isn't folded mid-turn at all, since the journal's `attackers` counts each creature once by object and a second combat would count a token split off a folded stack again (Windbrisk Heights). Tested by `token-stack-refold.test.ts`.
- **`replacements.ts`** — `EntersBattlefieldReplacement.notes` (2026-11-11): "As this enters, note your life total" (Sigarda's Splendor) — `Game.enteringReplacementOf` reads the amount as it enters (signed) into `EnteringReplacement.noted`, and both entry paths put it on `GameObject.chosenOnEnter`, where the `note` effect (`ResolutionContext.noteNumberOnSource`, the source's own stint only) writes a later one and `{ chosenNumber }` reads it. `LifeLossReplacement.from: "radiation"` and an `{ effect }` instead (2026-11-11): `Game.changeLife` takes the loss's cause (`loseLifeTallied` names `"radiation"` when the resolving source is the rad-counter ability object, rule 728.1a), `lifeLossReplaced` skips a `from` that isn't the cause, and `lifeLossInstead` hands an `{ effect }` to `runReplacedBy` with `"x"` the loss after any doubling, the loss never happening (rule 614.6 — Strong, the Brutish Thespian); not read on damage's loss. `EntersBattlefieldReplacement.mayEnterTapped: { then? }` ("you may have this land enter tapped. If you do, …", Mariposa Military Base): `Game.nextEnterChoice`'s `may-tapped`, asked as a `choose-creature-type` (`enterTapped`, options "Enter untapped" / "Enter tapped", the ability's text as prompt), kept on `enterChoice.enterTapped`, read by `enteringReplacementOf` as an "enters tapped" of the land's own (so `enterTapOrderAsked` orders it against an "enters untapped") with `then` applied in `moveObject` as it enters (`EnteringReplacement.choseTappedThen`). A `would-deal-damage` `prevent`'s `then` reads the recipient as `"trigger-recipient"`, for that stint (rule 400.7), once per recipient over a simultaneous event (rule 614.5; Vigor). `DiscardToLibraryReplacement` (`would-discard`, Library of Leng, Nephalia Academy: `Game.discardCards` holds the cards in `GameState.pendingDiscardsToLibrary` for a `choose-from-zone` with `discardToLibrary`, then `finishDiscardToLibrary`); `GraveyardExileReplacement`'s `instead: "library-top"` (`topsItselfInstead`) and `notCycled` (`Game.cyclingDiscard`); `CounterExileReplacement` (`would-counter-spell`, Guile: a `counter` effect by this permanent's controller exiles the spell, never countering it, and offers it, `Game.counterSpellByEffect`); `GraveyardExileReplacement.then` (what an exile does beside it: Kalitas's Zombie, a `reflexive-trigger` for Head of the Hunt's "when you do"; given up like a void counter when the object's controller picks another exile, `Game.exileReplacementChosen`), `GraveyardExileReplacement.linked` (the card exiled *with* the permanent, rule 607.2b — `exiledWith` set once it's in exile, from `Game.exiledLinkedTo`, in the stint the permanent has then or last had; given up the same way: The Darkness Crystal, Gisa, Glorious Resurrector), and `self` with `from: "battlefield"` for "if this creature would die" (Wildwood Escort).`would-draw` also takes `instead: { effect }` (`DrawReplacedByEffect` — Underrealm Lich's look at three: the draw becomes the effect, applied on an empty library and each draw of several answered before the next, rule 614.11/614.11a, `Game.drawEffectReplacementFor`; with `may`, Abundance's "you may instead", asked of the drawing player draw by draw as a `choose-modes` with `nextDrawFor` — "Draw a card" or each optional one, `Game.optionalDrawReplacementAsked`, rule 616.1), and `exceptFirstInDrawStep` / `firstEachTurn` on `draws` and `effect` replacements (Teferi's Ageless Insight, Reed Richards — read from `PlayerState.wouldDrawThisTurn`, counted by `Game.noteWouldDraw`). `would-deal-damage` also has `plusOf` (a `plus` read as the damage would be dealt) and `fromOther`; `player-effect`'s `damageReplacement` is the same for a duration, read in `Game.damageSteps`. `ReplacementSpec` (rule 614/616), a discriminated union on `StaticAbility.replacement`: `enters-battlefield { tapped?, tappedUnless?, counters?, sunburst?, transformed?, instead? }` (a self-replacement; `sunburst` is rule 702.44a's counter per colour spent to cast it, +1/+1 by its copiable creature type or charge otherwise; `instead` is an `EnterInstead` (`exileUnless` is Primeval Spawn's exile, nothing asked), "sacrifice a Plains instead … if you don't, put it into its owner's graveyard" or Mox Diamond's discard, asked by `askEnterChoice` before it moves, and on `insteadDone: false` `moveObject` sends it to the graveyard and reports no move), `others-enter-battlefield { filter, tapped?, untapped?, counters? }` (how *other* permanents enter — never its own source, never one entering alongside it; `counters.amount` is an `EffectAmount` that never counts the entering permanent or its batch), `would-create-token { multiplier }`, (a `manaProduction` rule's `times` since theme 13: Mana Reflection — rule 106.12b: a `{T}` mana ability its controller activates makes that many times its own mana, never a triggered mana ability's extra; applied by the hand path in `Game.activateAbility` and to the auto-payer's options by `mana-payment.ts`'s `multipliedManaOption`, an "any colour" unit becoming that many of one colour), `would-add-counter { multiplier?, plus?, halved?, counterKind?, filter?, other?, byEffectOnly?, whoPuts?, onPlayers? }` (`Game.counterTotal`, every `addCounter`, the counters a permanent enters with and a cost's counters (`putCostCounters`, a loyalty plus cost) unless `byEffectOnly` — Doubling Season's "if an effect would put": each `plus` before the doublers, after them for -1/-1, a negative `plus` (Vizier of Remedies' "minus one") the other way round and never below none — the order best for the controller, rule 616.1, searched by `counterTotalInBestOrder` when `halved` rounds; `whoPuts` reads `addCounter`'s `by` and any controller's permanent, `onPlayers` reaches `Game.playerCounterTotal` from `changePlayerCounters` and `changeEnergy`, whose `by` is the effect's controller or, for infect and toxic, the source's), `would-be-put-into-graveyard { instead: "exile", filter?, from?: "battlefield", self? }` (`from` is the dies-only form; `self` a disturb back face's own "exile it instead", applied by `moveObject` to the object it's on and skipped by the external scan), `would-draw { who: "opponent", instead: "you-draw", exceptFirstInDrawStep? }` (Notion Thief — sparing the first card a player draws in their own draw step; `Game.drawCard` lets the draw it makes be handed on by a Thief of one of *their* opponents, each effect once, rule 614.5) / `{ who: "you", instead: { draws } }` (Vnwxt's "draw two instead", gated on max speed; several multiply — each doubles the others' draws, the ruling — in `Game.drawCountFor`), `would-mill { who, multiplier?, plus? }` (multipliers then additions), `would-gain-life { who, plus?, multiplier?, prevent?, becomesLoss? }` (applied in `changeLife`, so lifelink too; additions then multipliers), `would-deal-damage { multiplier?, plus?, atLeast?, combat?, prevent?, then?, source?, to? }` (doubling, "plus 2", raising it to this permanent's power, or prevention with a follow-up, scoped by combat or noncombat, a source filter and the recipient — applied in `dealDamage`, multipliers then additions then the power floor then prevention). Two exile-instead replacements belong to the moving permanent rather than to a static, so `moveObject` applies them itself: a finality counter (graveyard-bound from the battlefield ⇒ exile) and `GameObject.exileIfItWouldLeave` (Whip of Erebos — any destination ⇒ exile; the ability form, "it gains …", is a modifier's `grantsExileIfLeaves`, read through `hasExileIfLeavesAbility` so a later loss of abilities removes it, rule 613.1f). A card's own "if this would be put into a graveyard from anywhere, … shuffle it into its owner's library instead" (`CardDefinition.shufflesIntoLibraryInstead` — Blightsteel Colossus) is applied there too, from every zone and ahead of Rest in Peace (`Game.shufflesItselfInstead`). Counter *prohibitions* aren't replacements: a static's `preventsCounters` (Solemnity, Melira) is asked by `Game.countersPrevented` at every placement. `would-draw` also takes `instead: "win-game"` with `whileLibraryEmpty` — Laboratory Maniac's "if you would draw a card while your library has no cards in it, you win the game instead" (rule 614.11), applied in `Game.drawCard` before a redirect, and to the draw a redirect or "draw N instead" makes (rule 616.2). Not modeled: multi-replacement ordering in general (Feather's exile beside another exiling replacement is the one case asked, by `leaveStackAfterResolving`; Laboratory Maniac against an opponent's Notion Thief applies the win first; Thieves of two different opponents go in turn order from the drawing player, who would choose), damage redirection to a third object. `enters-battlefield`'s `revealOrCondition: true` makes a reveal land's `tappedUnlessRevealFromHand` and its `tappedUnless` alternatives — either spares it — where by default each one failed taps it (Temple of the Dragon Queen). `would-create-token`'s `creatureTokensInstead` names the token made in place of creature tokens (Divine Visitation); its `multiplier` is optional; `plusTokens`, `plusOneToken`, `tokensInstead`, `oneOfEach` and the `ofTokens` that limits them are planned as one event by `token-replacements.ts`. A graveyard-exile replacement's `withCounters` (Dauthi Voidwalker). `tappedUnlessBehold: BeholdSpec` is a behold land (Theorist's Sanctum, rule 701.4a): asked as a `choose-from-zone` before it moves, none chosen entering tapped; entering unasked, it beholds whenever it could. A `would-deal-damage`'s `toFilter` narrows the recipient to a permanent matching a filter (Iroas, God of Victory's "attacking creatures you control" — `Game.damageReplacementReaches`). A `would-deal-damage` `prevent` + `then` with `thenCountsUnprevented` runs `then` with the damage's amount even when it can't be prevented (rule 615.12 — Jared Carthalion's ruling; the `prevent-all` step's `thenAmount`), and on a resolved `replacement-effect` it is the record's `then.countsUnprevented`, the `shield` step's `thenAmount` (Gatta and Luzzu). `enters-battlefield`'s `removeAllCountersFrom: CardFilter` (2026-10-31) takes every counter off each other permanent matching it as the permanent enters, never one entering beside it, and `counters.amount: "removed-as-enters"` is how many (rule 614.1c — Thief of Blood; `Game.removeCountersFrom` now returns the count). `rollAsEnters: { sides, count }` (2026-11-11) has its controller roll dice as it enters, through `Game.rollDice` (the roll every `roll-dice` effect makes), and `counters.amount: "rolled-as-enters"` is the total (Neverwinter Hydra); neither happens in `enterTapOrderAsked`'s look before the move (`Game.probingEnter`). `enters-battlefield`'s `asEnters` (`AsEntersAction`) is something done as the permanent is about to enter: `exile` (from its controller's graveyard or `anyGraveyard`, `count` a number, `"x"` or `"any"`, `upTo`, `orToGraveyard`, `counterForEach`), `returnPermanent`, `discard` (any number of matching cards from hand, `keywordCounters` one counter of each listed keyword among them — Indominus Rex, Alpha) or `putFromExile` (a count of opponents' cards from exile to their graveyards, all or none: Ulamog's Despoiler), `removeCounters: { filter }` (2026-11-13: a `choose-permanents` with `asEntersRemoveCounters` over anyone's matching permanents with a counter, each chosen losing every counter then, the count of counters its `asEntersDone` — Sin, Unending Cataclysm) or `turnFaceDown: CardFilter` (2026-11-13: every other match turned face down through `Game.turnFaceDown`, token by token, nothing asked — Ixidron), asked by `Game.askAsEnters` on any entry, the count on `enterChoice.asEntersDone` for `counters`' `"done-as-enters"` (times `eachDone`), the exiled cards linked to it as it enters (`linkExiledToSource`), and `CountSpec`'s aggregate `in: "exiled-with-source"` reading their total mana value, power or toughness (`staticSetMembers`). **`asCopyOf`** on `others-enter-battlefield` (2026-11-12) is "enter as a copy of" (`"source"`, `"enchanted"`, a resolved replacement's `{ target }`): `Game.enterAsCopyAsked` applies it first in `askEnterChoice` (control, then copy, then the rest), asking a `replacement-order` when several differ; a "next time" shield is spent by the whole enter batch (`spendEnterShields`), and a token minted straight onto the battlefield becomes the copy in `mintFreshTokenObject`, unasked. **`exileInstead`** on `others-enter-battlefield` (2026-11-13, Containment Priest) exiles the entering permanent in `moveObjectUncached` (rule 614.6 — already in exile, it stays): `Game.enterExilers` judges the filter as the permanent would exist on the battlefield (614.12), putting it there for the look — on the battlefield list, under the entering player, face down, its chosen copy, the effect's `asEntered`, the `EntryRecord` its move would write (so `cast` is a cast spell resolving), its mana cost unseen by devotion — and restoring everything after; `Game.exileInsteadOrderAsked`, in `askEnterChoice` after the copy, asks a `replacement-order` against its own `as-enters`, `devour` or `instead` actions (616.1e) and otherwise applies the exile first (`enterChoice.exileInsteadFirst`), so nothing else is asked.
- **`cards/define.ts`** — `costModification.minimumMana`: a floor on a spell's total cost (rule 601.2f's "effects that directly affect the total cost", Trinisphere), the largest reaching one returned by `Game.costModificationFor` and applied at the end of `castingCostOf`, after `reduceManaCost`, as generic mana up to that many by `cheapestManaAmount`; delve, convoke, assist and waterbend pay what it leaves.
- **`effects.ts`** — `PlayerScope`'s `"attacked-players"` / `"you-and-attacked-players"`: the players a trigger's counted attackers (`sets.batch`) were declared attacking, read off `LastKnownRefs.attackers` in `makeResolutionContext`'s `scoped` (a resolution's alone: `Game.scopedPlayers` answers nobody), in turn order from the active player; a planeswalker's or battle's controller isn't one (rules 506.2, 508.1b). Zurzoth, Chaos Rider.
- **`effects.ts`** — `collect-evidence`'s `amount: EffectAmount` and `required` (2026-11-11): "you may collect evidence X. When you do, …" (Incinerator of the Guilty, rule 701.59a) is a `may` around a `choose-value` number, capped at the graveyard's total mana value (701.59b), whose `then` collects `"x"` and makes the reflexive ability. `required` (the may already answered) can't be declined and counts evidence 0 with nothing exiled (`collectEvidenceRequired` on the `choose-from-zone` decision; `Game.beginCollectEvidence` fires `evidence-collected` at once over an empty graveyard).
- **`effects.ts`** — `roll-dice`'s `sides: "planar"` (2026-11-11): "Roll the planar die" (Fractured Powerstone). Planechase isn't modelled, and only a Planechase game has the die (rule 901.3), so `applyEffectSpec` returns at once (rule 101.3, the card's ruling): no `rollDice`, no event, no random draw. If Planechase is ever built, this is where its roll goes.
- **`effects.ts`** — `WardCost.payGeneric` / `payLifeAmount` (with `text`): a ward cost part read as the ward ability resolves (rules 702.21a, 608.2h), fixed into `mana` / `payLife` by `bindWardCost` in the `ward` effect before `Game.beginWard` sees it, so the payment check and the prompt work on numbers; `wardCostText` prints the card's wording until then. Minthara, Merciless Soul; Raubahn, Bull of Ala Mhigo.
- **`effects.ts`** — `DelayedNextSpell.orNextAbility`'s `{ loyalty: true }` (2026-11-11): "when you next cast an instant spell, cast a sorcery spell, **or activate a loyalty ability** this turn" (Repeated Reverberation, rules 603.7, 606.1), beside `{ xInCost: true }`: `Game.fireActivationWatchers` fires each waiting watch whose kind the activated ability is (a loyalty cost, or {X} in its cost), the ability its trigger object. Jaya's Phoenix's "copy the next loyalty ability you activate this turn" needed nothing new: a `whenever` watch on `activates-ability` with `loyalty`, `once` (`copy-next-loyalty-ability.test.ts`).
- **`abilities.ts`** — a `deals-damage` trigger's `to: "spell-caster"` and `spellCaster` (2026-11-11): "the player who cast [the granting spell]", written into the granted ability by `Game.boundToSpellCaster` as `grant-triggered` / `grant-triggered-all` resolve (rules 112.2, 707.10); absent, it never triggers. Hellish Rebuke (`granted-spell-caster.test.ts`).
- **`play-permission.ts`** — `PlayTerms.sacrificeCount` (2026-11-13): a permission's `sacrifice` of several — Scourge of Nel Toth's "by paying {B}{B} and sacrificing two creatures". Not named on the cast (`castRouteSacrifice` gives nothing then); `Game.spellSacrificeParts` (now taking the caster and face, to read the permission through `castPermission`) adds it as a part, chosen once the spell is on the stack as `additionalCost.sacrificeCount`'s is.
- **`abilities.ts`** — `ActivatedAbility.maxX` (2026-11-13): the most X may be, beside `minX` — "Sacrifice **up to three** permanents" (Baba Lysaga, Night Witch) is a sacrifice of `count: "x"` with `maxX: 3`. One more cap on the offer's `xCost.maxX` in `pushActivations`, and `whyCannotActivateAbility` refuses above it. Not an {X} in the cost (Magus Lucea Kane's `xInCost` reads the mana cost only).
- **`effects.ts` / `game.ts` / `view.ts`** — `create-emblem`'s `activated` (2026-11-13): an emblem's own activated abilities (rule 114.4 — Karn, Living Legacy; Ob Nixilis of the Black Oath), on the emblem object's modifier beside its triggers as `grantsActivated`, each marked `zone: "command"` so `whyCannotActivateAbility` takes its zone path (owner only). `grantedActivatedEntries` reads an emblem-kind object's own modifiers; `pushActivations` walks `GameState.emblems` (the object is in no zone list). `PlayerView.emblems[].object` names the object of one with activated abilities, and a stack entry from one reads its own text (`modifier-activated`).
- **`effects.ts`** — the `{ cardTypesAmong: ObjectSetRef }` amount (2026-11-13): card types among a set's members, each once, a member gone from the stint the set found it in read as it last existed (608.2h) through `ctx.cardTypesAmong` — Baba Lysaga's "among the sacrificed permanents" (`{ paid: "sacrificed" }`).
- **`abilities.ts`** — `ActivatedAbility.xIs` (2026-11-11): the cost's X defined by the ability's own text (rule 107.3c), `{ manaValueOf: ObjectRef }` — Prototype Portal's "X is the mana value of that card" is `{ manaValueOf: "exiled-with-source" }`. `Game.definedAbilityX` reads it as the ability would be activated now (`null` while the ref reads nothing: X undefined, not activatable); `legalActions` offers the one X (`xCost.minX === maxX`) when the other cost caps reach it, and `whyCannotActivateAbility` refuses any other (`ability-defined-x.test.ts`).
- **`abilities.ts`** — `evolves` (2026-11-11): "whenever this creature evolves" (rule 702.100b), beside `trains`: off a `counter-added` event marked `evolved`, which `Game.addCounter` sets when the `add-counter` that put the counters says `evolve: true` (what `evolve()` writes) and at least one was put. The effect context's `addCounter` takes the mark as `keywordAct: "training" | "evolve"`. Watchful Radstag (`test/evolves-trigger.test.ts`).
- **`abilities.ts`** — `destroyed`: a permanent destroyed (rule 701.8b: a "destroy" effect, lethal damage, deathtouch damage), read off `permanent-destroyed` by its reason, since the legend rule and the other state-based moves to a graveyard share that event; a look-back trigger (`LOOK_BACK_TRIGGERS`, 603.10a). Jaws, Relentless Predator.
- **`abilities.ts` / `game.ts`** — `mills`' `perCard` (2026-11-13): "whenever a player mills **a** nonland card" (Glowing One), once per matching card milled, each its trigger object, value 1 (`detectTriggersUncached`, beside `discards`' `perCard`). `withMillBatch` now holds each player's `cards-milled` (`millAnnouncements`) until every player of one "each player mills" has milled, then announces them in order, so a card milled alongside an opponent's sees theirs from the graveyard (Infesting Radroach's ruling); a lone mill is announced at once as before.
- **`game.ts` / `events.ts`** — `Game.payLife` (2026-11-13): every payment of life (rule 119.4) — an ability's or a spell's cost, Phyrexian mana, a mana ability's, a `may`'s `costLife`, a shock land's, an attack or block tax, the commander tax in life — goes through it, which loses the life (`changeLife`) and announces `life-paid` (`player`, `amount`; never for 0, never in a dry run). An "unless … pays N life" option, run as a `lose-life`, carries the engine-set `paid` and announces it through the context's `lifePaid`. The `pays-life` row reads it (Font of Agonies); the client log keeps it to the detailed mode.
- **`effects.ts` / `journal.ts`** — `create-token`'s `investigate` (2026-11-13): an investigation (rule 701.16a), set by `helpers.ts`'s `investigate()` and by hand on every pool card that investigates. `applyEffectSpec` reads each creator's count first, makes the Clues as a plain `create-token`, then announces `investigated` once per investigation through the context's `investigated`; the journal keeps an `investigated` entry each, which the `investigates` row names so the timing wrapper counts it (Erdwal Illuminator's "for the first time each turn").
- **`game.ts`** — `mana-ability-resolved` (2026-11-13): an activated mana ability resolving (rule 605.3b), announced by the by-hand activation (after its own mana and a tap doubler's, before any triggered mana ability's) and by `useManaSource` for each source a payment uses, with `amount` the mana the ability itself made. Every mana ability, unguarded — one more event per land tapped. The `mana-ability-resolves` row reads it (Tyvar the Bellicose).
- **`game.ts` / `events.ts`** — `permanent-untapped`'s `untapStepOf` (2026-11-13): `untapUnlessStunned` takes the player whose untap step is untapping it, and `finishUntapStep` passes it for the active player's permanents and for others' that untap then (Seedborn Muse). The `untaps-during-untap-step` row reads it, `by` that player (The Ninth Doctor).
- **`game.ts` / `events.ts`** — `chapter-triggered` (2026-11-13): `triggerChapters` announces each chapter ability as it queues it (rule 714.2b), `final` for the final chapter number (714.2d-e), so a trigger on it waits beside the chapter ability for its controller to order. The `final-chapter-triggers` row reads it (Historian's Boon).
- **`game.ts`** — phasing out looks back (2026-11-13, rule 603.10b): `lookBackSources` returns a `permanents-phased-out`'s phased-out permanents and `detectTriggersUncached` counts it as a leaving event, so each contributes, read through its `lastKnown`, its `phases-out` triggers alone (Teferi's Imp, Ertai's Familiar; The War Doctor phasing out with others). `withPhaseOutBatch`, in `simultaneously`, holds `phaseOut`'s announcements (`phaseOutAnnouncements`) and makes one event of an instruction that phases several out one at a time (`for-each-target`'s `simultaneous`: Clever Concealment), for "one or more".
- **`trigger-events.ts`** — rows of 2026-11-13 (the triggers-journal lane): `pays-life`, `investigates` (journaled), `mana-ability-resolves`, `untaps-during-untap-step` (`by` whose step), `final-chapter-triggers`, `phases-out` (looks back, rule 603.10b) and `scry-bottoms` (a scry that bottomed any, value how many).
- **`abilities.ts`** — `searches-library`: a player searching their own library, off `library-searched` (`beginLibrarySearch`, as it begins, never a search forbidden; `"trigger-player"` who searched).
- **`filter.ts`** — `attackingOpponent: "or-their-planeswalkers"` (2026-11-11): "creatures attacking your opponents and/or planeswalkers they control" (Roar of Resistance) — beside the player, an attacked permanent with no `protector` (a planeswalker, rule 506.2) that the filtering player doesn't control; a battle never matches. `true` stays the player alone.
- **`abilities.ts`** — A one-permanent sacrifice cost whose filter reads X (2026-11-11, `sacrificeCostFilterReadsX`; Sidisi, Regent of the Mire's "a creature you control with mana value X"): `costAnnouncesX` counts it, `Game.sacrificeCandidates` takes the X (with none, each permanent is judged at its own mana value), and `legalActions` offers the ability once per X with something to sacrifice and legal targets, that offer's `sacrifice.choices` the ones at its X; `dispatch` and `activateAbility` check the named permanent at the action's X.
- **`abilities.ts`** — `AbilityCost.xSpendOnly` (2026-11-11): "Spend only black mana on X" (Crypt Rats). `Game.activatedAbilityManaCost` — the one place an ability's mana cost is priced — moves what is left of the X in the generic part, after increases and reductions (601.2f, 602.2b; other generic mana is reduced first), into coloured symbols of that colour, so `maxX`, activation and payment agree. A colour word in `text-change.ts`'s `COLOR_WORD_KEYS`. One colour, abilities only: a spell's X (Consume Spirit) isn't built.
- **`abilities.ts`** — Combat triggers' defender and count clauses: `attacks`' `attackedPlayerOrPlaneswalker` and `attackedMonarch`, `attacks-alone`'s `defender`, `attack-with`'s `eachOf` and `attackingYouDirectly`, `becomes-blocked`'s `blockers` and `attackedPlayer`, `blocks`' `blocking`, the new `become-blocked-batch` (`events.ts`'s `attackers-blocked`, from `Game.announceUnblockedAttackers`); an `attackers-declared` trigger's "that player" is the attacker; the condition `those-attackers-at-you` (the firing declaration's `LastKnownRefs.attackers`, a resolving ability's alone).
- **`abilities.ts`** — `enters-battlefield`'s `firstCreationEachTurn` ("whenever you create one or more tokens for the first time each turn": `Game.firstTokenCreators`, filled by `noteTokensCreated` for the span of one entry). No creator is recorded: a token enters under the player who creates it (rule 111.2), so "you create" is a token entering under you. `activates-ability`'s `exhaust` and `powerUp`, read off the ability as activated (`stackAbilityOf`), a granted one included. `onlyOnce` isn't `exhaust`.
- **`abilities.ts`** — `counters-put`'s `perCounter` (`eventMultiplier`) and `batched`: `Game.withCounterBatch`, entered by `withEnterBatch`, `withDamageBatch`, `simultaneously`, `addCounterAll` and a proliferate, and in `effects.ts` by a `together` `sequence`'s consecutive `add-counter`s (the card says it's one instruction; Common Bond's two sentences don't) and `counters-divided`. Counters a batch enters with are announced with `enterAnnouncements`, so permanents entering together see each other's (rule 603.6a).
- **`abilities.ts`** — `dies`' `batchValue` (`Game.triggerValueOf`: a batched trigger's value is their total power as they last existed, fixed as it triggers). `dealt-damage`'s `atLeast`, `excess` and `batched`, and `deals-damage`'s `atLeast`: damage triggers that read the amount. `atLeast` on a trigger settled with its damage batch (`dealt-damage`, a `deals-damage` naming no recipient) is asked as `withDamageBatch` finishes, of the event's total; on one naming a recipient, of each source's share as the event is matched. `excess` makes `triggerValue` the event's `excess`, so sources at once sum to what they dealt past lethal. `DamageSourceRecord.spell` backs `damagedThisTurnBy.spell`.
- **`game.ts`** — `reachingCostModifications` reads `costModification.exceptOnCastersTurn` (Defense Grid) against the casting player and the active player.
- **`game.ts` / `actions.ts`** — an optional life cost a permanent gives spells (`costModification.optionalLife`, the Defilers, rule 601.2b): `optionalLifeSources` lists the permanents asking it of a card (each once), the cast enumeration offers every set of them as its own variant (up to `MAX_OPTIONAL_LIFE_OFFERED`; `LegalAction`'s `optionalLife` and `optionalLifeText`, echoed as `Action`'s `optionalLife`), and `optionalLifePick` (`withOptionalLifePick`, set by `dispatch`/`canDispatch` like `altCostPick`) is the set the cast being judged or paid pays: `costModificationFor` skips an `optionalLife` reduction outside it, `whyCannotCastSpell` checks it (`optionalLifeOwed`: a source that reaches the card, once each, the life payable with the spell's own `payLife`), and `castSpell` pays it beside the other additional costs.
- **`characteristics.ts` / `filter.ts`** — a `filter` scope reading `hasToxic` waits with the keyword-scoped statics (`filterReadsKeywords`), and the fold hands it `TargetView.toxic` (`FilterContext.layered.toxic`): the target's total toxic so far — printed less ability loss, plus the first pass's grants and its modifiers' — so "creatures you control with toxic have lifelink" (Skrelv's Hive) sees granted toxic (rule 613.8).
- **`abilities.ts`** — `AbilityCost.untapOthers` (rules 701.26b, 601.2h): untap tapped permanents as a cost, paid in `game.ts`'s `payUntapOthersCost` with `choose-permanents` once the ability is on the stack and its mana paid, so one tapped for that mana can be chosen; `untapCostCandidates` leaves out a stunned permanent (rule 122.1d), as `{Q}` does. Activation gates on what's tapped then, its own `{T}` counted and its own `{Q}` not.
- **`abilities.ts`** — `AbilityCost.putFromExile` is a `PutFromExileCost` (2026-11-11): a number (cards an opponent owns, the processors) or `{ count, linked: true, filter? }`, the cards exiled with the source in its current stint (rule 607.2a — Shelob, Dread Weaver). One reader, `Game.putFromExileCandidates`, feeds both the gate (rule 118.3) and `payPutFromExileCost`'s `choose-from-zone`.
- **`effects.ts`** — `cast-now`'s `from: { zones }` takes `"exile"` (2026-11-11): the cards the controller owns in exile, a face-down one only if they may look at it (rule 406.3b), through `ResolutionContext.exiledOwnedSeen` (Kaya the Inexorable's emblem).
- **`effects.ts`** — `return-from-graveyard`'s `from: "command-zone-and-graveyard"` (2026-11-11): the controller's graveyard plus the commanders they own in the command zone (`returnFromGraveyardByEffect`'s `commandToo`), for "put all commanders you own from the command zone and from your graveyard into your hand" (Campfire); rule 903.9b's choice is `moveObject`'s as ever.
- **`game.ts`** — rule 903.9b from the command zone (2026-11-11): `moveObject` defers a commander's move to a hand or library from the command zone too (`CommanderMoveOrigin`'s `"command"`, the awaiting decision and its offer flagged `fromCommandZone`), and `applyCommanderChoice` leaves one kept there where it is, with no zone change. The seats' answer for this origin is to let it go (`AutomaticController.commanderReplacement`, `ScriptedController`'s default, the fuzzer's `randomAnswer` 85% of the time): the effect moving it out was asked for (Command Beacon, Road of Return, Netherborn Altar, Campfire).
- **`filter.ts` / `journal.ts`** — `CardFilter.controllerTurnHistory` (2026-11-11): the permanent's controller has `atLeast` things matching `filter` in one of their journal lists (`journalCount`, asked of that one player), for "controlled by a player who was dealt combat damage by three or more Pirates this turn" (Admiral Beckett Brass). `JournalView.oncePerList` (set on `combat-damaged-by`) makes `journalCount` take each object once per stint and list among the entries its filter passes: a double striker is one source.
- **`replacements.ts`** (explore) — `ExploreReplacement` (`would-explore`, `filter`, `instead: { modify: { times } }`; 2026-11-11), read by `Game.exploreTimes` (the `explore` effect's `ctx.exploreTimes`) as one instruction for one permanent is reached: it becomes `times` explorations in a `sequence`, each stamped `replaced` so it isn't replaced again; several statics multiply (rule 614.5). Topography Tracker.
- **`filter.ts`** — `CardKeyword` gains `"devoid"` (2026-11-11): `hasKeyword: "devoid"` reads the card's printed "Devoid" line (`hasKeywordLine`, which now also takes a keyword alone on its line), for "a spell with devoid" (Corrupted Crossroads's `spendOnly`).
- **`abilities.ts`** — `AbilityLifeCost` is a read, always through `abilityLifeCost(state, player, cost, x, source)`: a number, `"commander-identity-colors"`, `"half-rounded-up"`, `"x"` or `{ each, per }`. `TriggerSpec` has three about a change of control (rule 603.10d): `loses-control` and `gains-control` on the permanent itself, and `control-taken` for "an opponent gains control of a permanent from you". An `ActivatedAbility`'s `costReduction` takes a `condition` and a `reduceColored` (`Game.activatedAbilityManaCost`). `ActivatedAbility` (`cost: {mana, tap, sacrifice?, payLife?, removeCounter?, payEnergy?, discard?}` — `payLife` is an `AbilityLifeCost`, a number or `"commander-identity-colors"` (War Room), always read through `abilityLifeCost(state, player, cost)`, which says `null` for a player with no commander; `discard: {count, filter?}` is paid with the ordinary `discard` decision as the ability goes on the stack, narrowed to the matching cards by the decision's `eligible`, which `viewFor` shows only to the player paying, `sacrifice: "self" | "creature-you-control" | {filter: CardFilter, count?: number | "x", differentNames?: true} | {each: CardFilter[]} | {parts: {filter, count: number | "x"}[]}` (`parts` a number for each kind, Glass-Cast Heart's "this artifact and thirteen Blood tokens"; `differentNames` asked one at a time; on a mana ability the mana waits in `pendingCostSacrifice.manaAbility` for `Game.resolveManaAbility`, never on the stack) — every filter matched against the ability's source, so `isSource` ("this creature and another green creature", an `each` part; "another creature or an artifact", `isSource: false` on one `anyOf` branch), `hostOfSource` ("Sacrifice enchanted creature") and `ofChosenType` read it (2026-10-31); a `count` or an `each` is a sacrifice of several, chosen with the ordinary `sacrifice` decision once the ability is on the stack and its mana paid (`sacrificeCostParts`, `sacrificePartsFillable`; `"x"` is an X the mana cost needn’t have, `ActivatedAbility.minX` its floor — `Game.pendingCostSacrifice` in the `game.ts` entry), optional `sorcerySpeed`, optional `loyaltyCost` (a number, or `"-x"` for a "−X" cost — X announced on the activation's `xValue`, offered as `xCost` up to the source's loyalty, rules 107.3a, 606.6; `loyaltyDelta(ability, x)` resolves either to the counters it adds), optional `zone: "hand" | "graveyard" | "command"` (activatable only from that zone, by the card's owner, never a mana ability — Channel discards the source as an implicit cost unless `staysInZone` — ninjutsu (`ninjutsu: true`, the `ninjutsu()` helper, rule 702.49; a cycling ability is marked `cycling: true` as `Game.cyclingAbilityOf` builds it, and `isEquipAbility` reads an equip ability off its text — what `abilityCostModification.abilityKind` names) keeps it in the hand, revealed; its `returnToHand` cost records what the returned attacker was attacking on the ability, `LastKnownRefs.returnedAttacking`, either as it's paid or from the `choose-permanents` answer (`costOf`), and its `ninjutsu` effect puts the card onto the battlefield tapped and attacking that (`putOntoBattlefieldByEffect`'s `attacking`, set before the entered event) — a graveyard ability exiles it unless `staysInZone`, a command-zone one (Derevi, commander ninjutsu) moves nothing; a source that stays put is checked to be the same object on resolution via `GameObject.zoneChangeCount`, rule 400.7) and `costReduction` (a live-count discount printed on the ability itself, mirroring `selfCostReduction`) and `targetCostReduction` ("costs {N} less if it targets …", `count` of them — priced by `Game.activatedAbilityManaCost` from the chosen targets, assumed while only offered, the offer narrowed by `abilityTargetReductionOptions`); `mana` may contain `{X}`). `TriggeredAbility` (`trigger: TriggerSpec` — `enters-battlefield`/`dies` (with `filter?`/`otherOnly?`), `leaves-battlefield`, `gains-life`, `loses-life {who, firstDuringTheirTurn?}`, `becomes-target {who, filter?, byOpponentOnly?, spellOnly?}` (ward's trigger), `attacks {who, filter?, otherOnly?, attackingYou?, youDefend?, defender?, aloneAgainstDefender?}` (once per attacker, which is the trigger object; every attacker is attacking before the first fires; `attackingYou` is "you or a planeswalker you control" and `youDefend` "you're the defending player", a battle you protect included — `Game.attacksYouOrYourPlaneswalker`, rule 508.5), `attacks-player {who, defender, filter?}` (once per player attacked — a planeswalker's attackers attack it, not its controller), `blocks`/`becomes-blocked` (once per blocker / once per blocked attacker, each the trigger object), `sacrifice {who, filter?, otherOnly?}`, `deals-combat-damage-to-player {who, filter?, otherOnly?, toPlayerControlsMore?}` (the damaging creature is the trigger object; `toPlayerControlsMore` — "to a player who controls more lands than you" — is part of the event, not an intervening-if), `deals-damage-batch {who, filter?, to?, combat?, once?}` ("one or more … deal combat damage to a player" — once per player per simultaneous damage event, settled with it through `withDamageBatch`; `once: "per-event"` for once per event), `dealt-damage {who, filter?, combat?}` (the receiving end, once per permanent per damage batch), `deals-damage {who, filter?, otherOnly?, to?, toFilter?, combat?, exactly?, toItsTarget?}` (the dealing end — `who`/`filter` are about the *source*, a spell, permanent or ability's source; once per recipient per damage event, for the amount dealt after prevention; the source is the trigger object), `step-begins {step}`, `surveils {who}` / `scries {who, orSurveil?}` (once per scry or surveil of one or more — into an empty library too, rule 701.22d — its trigger value the cards looked at), `cast-spell {who, noncreatureOnly?, firstEachTurn?, from?, notFrom?, modal?, sharesNoCreatureType?, countCastBefore?}` (the zone it was cast from, recorded on the `spell-cast` event; `modal` — a modal spell, its modes chosen as the trigger value; `countCastBefore` — the trigger value is how many matching spells the caster cast this turn before this one, fixed as it triggers — Thousand-Year Storm; a `filter`'s `targets` clause asks what the spell targets), `plays-card {who, from?, filter?}` (a land played or a spell cast — "whenever you play a card from exile"; `filter` asks about the card played, the land on the battlefield or the spell on the stack — Rendmaw's "a card with two or more card types"), `this-cast`, `activates-ability {who: "attached" | "you" | "opponent", ninjutsu?, loyalty?, source?}` (an ability activated onto the stack — never a mana ability; `"attached"` is the host's, still attached once costs are paid — Illusionist's Bracers; `"you"`/`"opponent"` any player's of that side, narrowed to ninjutsu, loyalty, or a battlefield source matching a filter; its trigger object is the ability and `"trigger-player"` the activator), `transforms {who, intoFront?, filter?}`, `leaves-graveyard {who, filter?, perCard?}` (batched — once per `cards-left-graveyard`, i.e. per simultaneous move; `perCard` once per card), `put-into-graveyard {who, filter?, from?, notFrom?, batched?}` (off `cards-put-into-graveyard`, which groups what one simultaneous move put into graveyards — `withGraveyardEnterBatch`, joined by every leave batch, mill, discard and surveil), a `predicate` escape hatch; optional `condition?: StaticCondition` — an intervening-if, rule 603.4, whose `x` kind reads the X a permanent's own enters trigger or a cast trigger snapshots (`Game.triggerCastX`, rule 107.3m — ravenous), and whose `trigger-spell-first` kind asks whether a cast trigger's spell is its caster's first matching spell this turn (`ConditionOptions.triggerSpellStint` picks its cast record, Alania); optional `whileCondition?: StaticCondition` — part of the trigger condition ("whenever you cast a spell while Fire Lord Azula is attacking", rule 603.1), asked as the event happens and never again on resolution. `StaticAbility` also carries `crewsAs` (`{ plus?, toughness?, saddles? }`, `scope: "self"` — what the creature pays a crew, or with `saddles` a saddle, cost with; read by `Game.tapCostPower`), `restrictions: CombatRule[]`, `combatDamageByToughness` + `canAttackAsThoughNoDefender` (Arcades, the Strategist — the latter checked in `whyCannotAttack`), `cantBeSacrificed` (rule 701.21a — folded into `Characteristics.cantBeSacrificed` with the effect-granted form, and asked by every sacrifice the engine makes or offers through `characteristics.ts`'s `cantBeSacrificed`: edict and cost choices, "Sacrifice ~" costs and mana abilities, `sacrifice-source`/`sacrifice-target`, a completed Saga, an end-step sacrifice), `legendRuleOff` (rule 704.5j — "the 'legend rule' doesn't apply to" whatever its `affects` reaches, read live by `characteristics.ts`'s `exemptFromLegendRule`; a copy exception can carry it too — Sakashima of a Thousand Faces), `notLegendaryIfToken` (Aeve, Progenitor Ooze — read by `filter.ts`'s `supertypesOf`), `costModification {applies, reduceGeneric?, increaseGeneric?}`, `protection {colors?, types?}`, `grantsActivated: ActivatedAbility[]`, `grantsToGraveyard` (flashback or escape for matching cards in your graveyard — Iroh, Grand Lotus, The Master of Keys — offered like a printed one; `keywords` for unearth, encore, embalm, harmonize, retrace or sneak with their costs — Sedris, Graywater's Fixer, Six), `castWithSneakFromGraveyard` (Ninja Teen), `grantsCyclingInHand` (Rhet-Tomb Mystic, Homing Sliver), `alternativeCyclingCost` (New Perspectives, Gavi, Nest Warden: a second cycling ability at {0}, listed by `handGrantedActivated`), `grantsForetellInHand` (Dream Devourer), `outsideBattlefield` (layer 4 off the battlefield — Arcane Adaptation, Ashes of the Fallen), `alternativeCostForSpells` (Jodah's "{W}{U}{B}{R}{G} rather than the mana cost", offered as the `altCost` variant (a variant per alternative cost and per opponent to gain the life — `Game.alternativeCostsOf`, `altCostIndex` naming one past the first, rules 118.9a, 601.2b); its `payLifeManaValue` is life equal to the spell's mana value, with `oncePerTurn`, `yourTurnOnly` and `fromHand`; its `energy` paid with it and offered only while the caster has it — Nissa, Worldsoul Speaker — and its `flash`, a cast for it begun as though it had flash, rule 601.3c — Primal Prayers), `grantsToSpells` (keywords and triggered abilities the controller's spells have on the stack — Abaddon's cascade as a granted `this-cast` trigger, found by `effectiveTriggeredEntries` with a `"spell-triggered"` `GrantedAbilityRef`, the keywords folded in `computeCharacteristics`), `condition?: StaticCondition` (`controls {filter, atLeast}` / `opponent-controls {filter, atLeast}` / `your-turn` / `threshold` / `metalcraft`). `isManaAbility` is rule 605.1a — no target, not a loyalty ability, an `add-mana` effect (or a `sequence` of them) — whatever the cost: a filtered sacrifice, counters removed, energy and a coloured cost are all mana abilities that never use the stack, and `Game.manaSources` leaves out the ones the auto-payer can't pay by itself, so they're activated by hand. One activated by hand (its player holding priority) is followed by `afterPlayerAction` like any action — state-based actions and triggers before that player gets priority back (117.3c, 117.5) — while the auto-payer's, inside a cast or activation, give no priority until it's done (601.2g-h). An `exhaust` ability is activatable once while the object exists (`GameObject.exhaustedAbilities`, cleared by any zone change), and so is a `powerUp` one (rule 702.193), which also costs less by its permanent's mana cost the turn that permanent entered (`Game.poweredUp`, rule 118.7). A `becomes-target` trigger's `targeterNotTarget` keeps the targeting player out of its first target slot (Venerated Rotpriest's "target opponent"). `AbilityCost.mill` ("Mill a card" — rule 701.17b; it makes an ability that adds mana not a mana ability, 605.1a). `ActivatedAbility.divided` / `TriggeredAbility.divided` split an amount among a group's targets (602.2b, 603.3d), `total` any `EffectAmount` read as it's activated or put on the stack (Lukka, Ureni, Orca's last-known power). `ActivatedAbility.crew` marks a crew ability (702.122a — `crew()` writes it), as `saddle` does saddle's: what a `crewsAs` static reads.
