Trigger kinds `becomes-monstrous`, `becomes-renowned`, `becomes-saddled` (`firstTimeEachTurn` — only when it wasn't saddled already), `exerted` (`asItAttacks` — rule 607.2h), `enlists` (rule 702.154c — `who` the enlisting attacker, the trigger object the creature enlisted), `crews-or-saddles` (rules 702.122b, 702.171c — `as` the keywords, `who` the creature tapped, the trigger object the Vehicle or Mount) and `blocked-by` (once per blocker, `blocker` filtering it — flanking). `AbilityCost.reveal: { count, filter, text }` is "reveal N [kind of] cards from your hand" as an activation cost (the Coldsnap Martyrs; `count: "x"` announces X, which `costAnnouncesX` reports); `AbilityCost.unattach` (rule 701.3d) pays by unattaching: `"self"` (Sunforger), `"granter"` (Leonin Bola, whoever controls it) or a chosen `{ filter }` attached to the source (Captain America, named on the action's `sacrifice`, offered with `sacrifice.unattach`, read back as `"sacrificed"`, one offer per total when a `divided` total reads it, `dividedNow`'s `sacrificed`), unpayable with nothing attached (`unattachCandidates`, `unattachNow`); `exilesSacrifice` pays a chosen `sacrifice` by exiling it (no sacrifice event, read back as `"sacrificed"`; `additionalCost` too), `exileFromHand` asks with a `choose-from-zone` (a mana ability's mana comes with the answer, `thenManaAbility`), `exileSpell` takes a spell or copy of yours off the stack; `exileFromGraveyard: { count, filter? }` is "exile N [kind of] cards from your graveyard" as an activation cost (`singleGraveyard`: any player's, all from one, a `share` rule) (Varina, Moorland Haunt), chosen as the ability goes on the stack; `TriggerSpec`'s `becomes-untapped` (rule 701.26b — Key to the City, Mesmeric Orb). `AbilityCost.addCounter: { kind, count }` puts counters on the source as a cost (Wall of Roots' -0/-1, Devoted Druid's -1/-1). `TriggerSpec` `this-cycled` is "when you cycle this card" (rule 702.29c — Dismantling Wave, Decree of Pain). `this-discarded` is "when you discard this card" (rule 113.6k — from wherever the discard put it; `byOpponent`: the `cards-discarded` event's `cause`, the controller of the spell or ability that caused it, from `DecisionSource.controller`, is an opponent — Psychic Purge), and `discards` takes `firstTimeEachTurn` (the turn's first matching discard, off the turn's history — Rielle). `AbilityCost.returnToHand: { count, filter }` returns permanents you control as a cost (Quirion Ranger, Multani); `attack-with`'s `thisOrYourCommander` counts only this permanent and commanders you own (Ainok Strike Leader). `attack-with`'s `notAttackingYou` is "if they aren't attacking you" (Mirkwood Trapper), the player alone, asked again on resolution by `attackConditionsStillHold`. The `attacks` trigger's `firstTimeEachTurn`, and `DefenderLife`'s `"most-still-attacking"` (an intervening-if — Scourge of the Throne). `AbilityCost.exileFromGraveyard`'s `count: "x"` (Necropolis Fiend's "exile X cards from your graveyard", capping X) and `returnSelfToHand` (Shigeki). A `discard` cost's `count` may be `"x"` (`costAnnouncesX`: X announced without `{X}` in the mana — Gix); `deals-combat-damage-to-player`'s `toOpponent`. `TriggerWho` `"attached"` about a *player* is the player a Curse is attached to (`attacks-player`'s `defender: "attached"` — "whenever enchanted player is attacked"), and `"enchanted-player-controls"` is "a creature enchanted player controls" (Trespasser's Curse); a Curse's trigger whose event names nobody names the enchanted player in `LastKnownRefs.player` (`refsAtTrigger`). `AbilityCost.blight: N` is "Blight N" as a cost (rule 701.68a — Gristle Glutton): the creature chosen once the ability is on the stack (`Game.beginBlight`, the `choose-permanents` decision), activatable only while its controller has a creature (701.68b). A `becomes-target` trigger's `otherOnly` leaves out its own source (Monk Gyatso), and its trigger object is the permanent targeted. `AbilityCost.waterbend: true` makes the cost's `mana` a waterbend cost (rule 701.67a — Geyser Leaper's "Waterbend {4}", Katara, Water Tribe's Hope's "Waterbend {X}"): its generic may be paid by tapping artifacts and creatures, the source too unless `{T}` taps it (`Game.waterbendTaps`). A `CostReductionAmount` may be `{ speed: "you" }` — its controller's speed (Samut, the Driving Force). `ActivatedAbility.anyPlayer` is "any player may activate this ability" (rule 602.2): `Game.pushActivations` offers it on another player's permanent (a printed one only — the cheap pre-check), `whyCannotActivateAbility` waives the controller check for it and reads its `condition` with the activator as "you"; the activator controls the ability and pays its costs (602.2a). `TriggeredAbility.targetsChosenBy` has another player answer its `choose-targets` decision (`Game.triggerTargetChooser`; the awaiting's `targetsFor` is its controller, whose view judges the targets — rules 603.3d, 700.2e) (2026-10-29). `cost.putFromExile` (2026-10-31): a processor's "put N cards an opponent owns from exile into that player's graveyard", chosen once on the stack (`Game.payPutFromExileCost`, shared with `additionalCost.putFromExile`). `removeCounter.count: "all"` (2026-10-31): every counter of its kind off the source, none a legal payment, the number removed the ability's X (`Game.activateAbility` sets `chosenX` as it pays — rule 601.2h via 602.2b; Catti-brie of Mithral Hall, Molten Hydra); never with a chosen removal. `targetsAtRandom`, on triggered and activated abilities, is "target … chosen at random" (Witch Hunt, Goblin Test Pilot): `Game.randomTargets` picks each slot from its legal targets as the ability goes on the stack or before an activation's costs, weighing a token stack by its tokens, and an activated one is offered with no targets and refuses any named.
- **`game.ts`** — a card's several cycling abilities (`CardDefinition.moreCycling`, each `name`d): `cyclesByName` says whether the plain `cycle` action can name a card's cycling, and otherwise `handGrantedActivated` lists each as an activated ability from the hand, chosen as it's activated. A flashback's other costs: `FlashbackOption.tapCreatures` through `spellTapCost` and the cast offer's `tapCost`, `FlashbackOption.discard` as a number or `"x"`, announced for the cast (`discardX`, `hasX`). The `ability-activated` event's `sacrificedToActivate`: the ability's own cost, read by the `activates-ability` trigger's narrowing of that name.
- **`combat/rules.ts`** (2026-11-08, need-key themes step 7, theme 11, B284) — one combat rule, read in one place: `CombatRule` is a `CombatWord` (no parameters, today's strings) or a `CombatRecord` (`cant-attack`, `must-attack`, `cant-block`, `must-block`, `cant-be-blocked`, `must-be-blocked`, `must-be-blocked-if-able`, and `attack-only-nearest` — a direction every player attacks in, made only by a `restrict` over the game and read by `nearestOpponentRule` beside Pramikon's static), kept in `StaticAbility.restrictions`, `PtModifier.restrictions` (the set fixed as it resolved) or `PlayerEffect.combat` (a rule over the game, 611.2c). `bindCombatRule` reads a record's references as a `restrict` resolves (ids and stints, 400.7, and its `you`); `recordsOn` is every record binding an object now (a per-region index of the statics carrying one, its modifiers, the player effects), and `recordsOfKind` a view of it; `aimNames` says whether a defender is what `at` names; `playerTestHolds` (moved here from `eligibility.ts`) reads a `PlayerTest`. `eligibility.ts` and `decisions/blockers.ts` read through these. A word stays in `characteristics.restrictions` / `restrictionsOf` as cheap as it was. `combatRuleHelps` and `combatRuleSlots` are the bots' read of a rule. `blocksAsThoughShadow` is a permission beside it, not a rule; `enter-blocking` (rule 509.4) puts a just-made permanent onto the battlefield blocking.
- **`combat/tax.ts`** — what particular creatures cost to attack and block, reads only: `StaticAbility.combatTax` and the `blockTax` player effect. `creatureAttackTax`/`creatureAttackTaxes` give a creature's mana, life and `permanents` (`CombatPermanentTax`: `{ do, count, noun, among }`, what can pay each read now by `attackCostAmong`); `creatureAttackPermanentTaxes` the taxes themselves, which `Game.attackCostParts` adds up by kind. `Game` raises and pays them: `pendingAttackCost` holds a declaration past its mana and life (rule 508.1h-j), `continueAttackCosts` asks each kind with the existing `sacrifice` or `choose-permanents` (`attackCost`) decision unless there's no choice, one permanent never paying two (`sacrificePartsFillable`), and `payAttackCosts` pays it all, leaving out a creature that paid with itself (508.1k), then enlists and announces. `combat/attacking.ts`'s `withinAttackTax` keeps a driver that doesn't weigh the cost from sacrificing the last three of what's asked (`SACRIFICE_KEEPS`).
- **`combat/limits.ts`** — (2026-11-09) Past a full capped defender a requirement can't narrow per creature: `decisions/attackers.ts` widens `defendersFor` for creatures aimed at capped defenders with too few seats and records `company.aimed`/`aimedSeats`, which `attackCompanyViolations` checks and `withAimedSeated` restores for a driver. how many creatures may attack or block (rules 508.1c, 509.1b), reads only: `attackCaps(state, registry)` (`StaticAbility.attackLimit`, granted ones too: a `most` over everyone, a `perDefender` on a player or planeswalker) and `blockersLeft(state, registry, player)` (`blockLimit` less those already blocking this step, and the `limit-blockers` effect's `GameState.combatBlockLimits` for this combat). The offers carry them (`company.caps`, `blockMost`); `combat/attacking.ts`'s `attackCompanyViolations` and `seatAttackers` (a matching, so requirements count the most that fit under per-defender caps, rule 508.1d), `combat/blocking.ts`'s `tooManyBlockers` and `withinBlockMost` (a requirement yields when the limit's worth of creatures each obey one, 509.1c) check and cut.
- **`game.ts`** — `drawCard`: `cantDrawMore` (`prohibits`'s `drawsPerTurn`, from the journal's draws) stops a draw before anything could replace it, again in `drawCardRaw` for a draw a multiplier or a Notion Thief made, and `mayBeDone` doesn't offer "you may draw" past it; `nextDrawReplaced` applies a `player-effect`'s `nextDrawInstead` before a Thief's redirect, asking through `choose-modes`'s `nextDrawFor` when different ones (or a printed effect replacement) could apply. It stamps `GameObject.drawnOnTurn` on what it draws (cleared by any move, so a replaced draw or a card put back isn't one), which `CardFilter.drawnThisTurn` reads; a `may`'s `aboutTarget` goes on as `aboutCard` (the awaiting and the offer), an id so a card in a hand stays unnamed to other seats, and a `look-and-choose`'s `prompt` and `givesUp` as the offer's (Sylvan Library: `givesUp` has both bots pick their worst).
- **`replacements.ts`** (`instead`) — One vocabulary of what a replacement does (need-key themes, theme 9): `AmountChange` (`times`, `plus`, `half`, `atLeast`, `atMost`, `set`, an `EffectAmount` read as the event would happen), `ReplacedByEffect` (`effect`, the event never happens, rule 614.6), `ModifiedEvent` (`modify`, `also`), and damage's `DamagePrevention` / `DamageRedirection`. `insteadEffect` and `insteadModify` read them. `Game.runReplacedBy` runs an effect `instead`, binding `"x"`, `"trigger-object"`, `"trigger-recipient"`, `"trigger-player"`. New events: `would-lose-game` (`Game.loseGameReplaced`, in the SBA check and `loseGame`; conceding isn't replaced, 104.3a) and `would-scry` (`beginScry`, which also adds a static's `surveilAdditional` to its controller's surveils — rule 701.25b, not a replacement). `would-draw` adds `instruction`, `whileHandAtMost`, `any-player`; `would-create-token`'s `becomes` is asked through `Game.tokenEventReplaced` (Moonlit Meditation) at the effects that make tokens as a resolution step: `create-token`, `create-token-copy`, `incubate`, `populate`, `encore` and an `amass` with no Army, which goes on after with whatever Army there is (`tokenReplaced`, 701.47b); a copy of the host is made as the replaced effect, its `tokenRiders` still applying (Divine Visitation's ruling), with the host in the target slot after its own (`replacedFor.copyHost`); `would-be-put-into-graveyard` takes `"library-bottom"` and `"hand"` (`Game.ownDestinationInstead`).
- **`state.ts`** (`ActiveReplacement`) — A resolved replacement or prevention effect: the `ReplacementSpec`, `owner`, `source`, an `Expiry` (ended by `Game.mapPlayerExpiries` with player effects), `next` / `left` (a shield: 615.8, 615.7), and what its references fixed as it resolved (`target`, `onlyPlayers`, `from`, `byFilter`, `recipients`, `redirect`, `then`, its `targets`, stints and X). `GameState.activeReplacements` is the one list; `Game.shieldCovers` is its one covers check. `replacement-effect` makes them, as do `prevent-damage`, `damage-rule`, `prevent-damage-from-chosen-source` and Fog.
- **`replacements.ts`** (proliferate) — `ProliferateReplacement` (`would-proliferate`, `instead: { modify: { times } }`), read by `Game.beginProliferate`, which raises the `proliferate` decision once per time, `awaiting.more` counting those left and `then` after the last. The offer's `copies` gives each token stack's size and an answer names a stack once per token it reaches; `Game.applyProliferate` splits those off together (`splitCountFromStack`).
- **`replacements.ts`** (copying a spell) — `SpellCopyReplacement` (`would-copy-spell`, `instead: { modify: { plus } }`), read by `Game.withSpellCopyEvent`, which wraps one instruction to copy (the `copy-spell` effect through `ctx.spellCopyEvent`, storm, Zada's ordered answers) and adds its copies after, with new targets; `GameState.spellCopyPlacements` then asks where each goes among copies that couldn't take new targets, once its targets differ from theirs (`choose-number`'s `placeCopy`).
- **`Game.offersFor`** — `legalActions` plus what life can pay only by going to 0 (rule 119.4), each with `phyrexianLifeAtLeast`: what a person is offered (`Room.actionsFor`, auto-pass, `isDeadForMana`). Bots and the auto-payer read `legalActions` and never go below 1 on their own; `phyrexianLife` named on an action lets it (`canDispatch` judges under it).
- **`characteristics.ts`** (layer 4's end, 2026-11-11) — once every layer-4 effect has applied, a subtype that goes with no card type the object now has is dropped (`subtypesFitting`, rule 205.3d): in `ownLayerFour` when a modifier changed anything and in `foldLayerFourOnce`'s result (not in what the pass has `seen`). An effect that made a creature an Angel for good keeps applying while the permanent isn't a creature and shows again when it is (Archangel Elspeth's ruling); a copy exception's subtypes are still fixed as they're applied (rule 707.9b). `test/creature-subtype-needs-type.test.ts` also holds every pool card's printed subtypes to `subtypeFitsTypes`, which found `Communicator` missing from the artifact types.
- **`effects.ts`** — `separate-into-piles`' `each: PlayerScope` (2026-11-11): piles for every player of a scope in three shared steps (rule 608.2e — Make an Example): all separate their own permanents in turn order, a pile of each is chosen, then one `finishPiles` over every chosen pile (one simultaneous sacrifice) and every other. All in the effect's resolver, its place kept in `progress.each` (`toSeparate`, `made`, `chosen`, `asked`) across the same `separate-piles` / `choose-pile` decisions; no new decision or `Game` method.
- **`effects.ts`** — `exile-spell-as-it-resolves`'s `anyOwner` and `linked` (2026-11-11): the mark (`GameObject.exileAsItResolves`) names the spell's owner rather than the effect's controller, and carries a `link` that `Game.exileAsItResolved` hands to `linkExiledToSource` (rule 607.2a — River Song's Diary). `modify-pt-all`'s `perMatchOf` (2026-11-11): the amount once for each listed filter a permanent matches, every count read in `Game.modifyPtAll` before any modifier goes on (rule 611.2c — Akroma, Vision of Ixidor); with it `CardFilter.keywordVariant` (`"hexproof"` / `"protection"` in any variant, off the computed characteristics, a keyword-reading clause for the layer fold) and the `hasKeyword: "partner"` row in `filter.ts`. The `thisWay` amount's `byOwner` counts each object as its owner's, for `who: "each"` and `perPlayer` (`ctx.ownerOf`; Kefka, Dancing Mad's "each player who owns a spell you cast this way"). `search-library`'s `playable` (and `SearchMore`'s) is `"while-exiled"` or any `ExilePermission`, which `Game.beginLibrarySearch` grants on every exiled find through `exilePermissionGrant`, as `exile`'s `mayCast` (Ugin, Eye of the Storms' free cast until end of turn). `flicker`'s `returnOnOwnersTurn` (`FlickerOptions`): `Game.flickerByEffect` mints a link and a delayed return per owner of the cards exiled, each with `turnOf` that owner (The Eternal Wanderer's "that player's next end step"). `roll-dice`'s table rows can say `mayRollAgain` (rule 706.3c): a `may` of the same roll after the row, the number of dice held. `readsCreatedTokens`, a memoized walk of a spec: `applyEffectSpec` runs one that reads the tokens it made as a set under `mintCreatedApart`, so they don't fold into an older stack (`Game.mintTokenBatch`) and the read is theirs alone. `put-arrived-onto-battlefield`'s `count: "all"` (every card a batched trigger fired on, no choice), the `playerCounters` amount's `forTarget` (the player in a target slot), `grant-protection`'s `asHexproof` and `grant-player-hexproof`'s `from` (hexproof from a quality for a while: `PtModifier.hexproofFrom`, `GameState.hexproofFromPlayers`, read by `playerHexproofFromBlocks`);`choose-one-of-each`'s `chosenBy: "you"` (the effect's controller answering for each player, Mythos of Snapdax).`mayBeDone` is asked of a plain `may` and, as that player (`ctx.asPlayer`), of each `each-player-may`'s `effect`, and knows tap and untap (`ctx.tappedNow`, rule 701.26); `counter`'s `into: "library-top" | "library-bottom"` and `withTimeCounters` (`Game.suspendExiledCard`); `DelayedWhenever.whileAnyExiled` and `look-and-choose`'s `exiledThatCanEnter` and `player: "active-player"` (Dimensional Breach, `Game.couldEnterBattlefield`);`cast-now`'s`grant-graveyard-keyword`'s`PermanentDuration`'s `"until-source-cast"` (`PtModifier.untilCast`, ended by `Game.endUntilCastEffects` once the card in that exile stint has been cast, its mana payable from the grant first); `grant-activated-of`; `grant-graveyard-keyword`'s `"escape"` (`GameObject.grantedGraveyardKeywords`' `exileCount`, read by `escapesOf`), `grant-graveyard-cast`'s `free`, `exileAfter`, `filter` (`graveyardCastPermission`, a permission in `graveyardGrantsFor`), `cast-now` `from: "milled-this-way"`; `CardDefinition.escape`'s `counterChoice` (asked in `nextEnterChoice`) and `exileLinked` (`GameObject.escapeExiled`, linked as the permanent enters); `cast-now`'s cost terms (`byPaying`, `byPayingLife`, `alsoPaying`, `spendAs`, each carried on `CastNowOptions`, the decision and `CastNowAsk`, read by `castCostString`, `whyCannotCastSpell`, `castSpell` and `spendAsFor` through `castNowOffer`), `mustCast` (`castNowForced`: no decline only with a whole, legal cast found as it's raised, `fillCast`, payable from what's floating, rules 118.3c, 118.8c; that cast is the decision's `forcedCast`, what a controller that declines answers with, and `decisions/cast-now.ts` refuses a null answer only then), `from: { graveyardOf | zones | exiledOwnedWithCounter }`, `thisWay: "cast"`; `become-foretold` (rule 702.143d; `"exiled-this-way"` or a `then`'s target 0, `costReducedBy` a given cost), `look-at-face-down` (a look they close, by slot, player or filter), `look-and-choose`'s `faceDownLooks` (who may look at a face-down pick).`player-effect`'s `graveyardExile` (a would-go-to-a-graveyard replacement for the turn, asked live from its owner's side in `Game.graveyardExileReplacements`; `returnAtEndStep` gives each permanent its own delayed `return-flickered`, `Game.returnAtNextEndStep`), `playLandsFromGraveyard`, the library-top permissions `playFromLibraryTop` / `castFromLibraryTop` / `looksAtOwnLibraryTop` (read beside the statics' by `mayPlayFromLibraryTop`, `libraryTopCastPermissions` and `viewFor`), and `tokensComeToOwner` (`Game.tokenCreatorAfterRedirects`, first in `createTokens` and `createTokenCopy`, each effect once, a would-be attacker only tapped under another player). `choose-value`'s `"roll-result"`, asked through the `choose-creature-type` menu of the last roll's differing results (`Game.chooseValue`'s `xByOption`), `then` reading it as `"x"` and the amount `{ roll: "other" }` the rest.`modal`'s `random` with `announced`, and `chooseIf`. `bindDynamicCompares` leaves a `reflexive-trigger` with `about` alone: `"trigger-object"` in it means what `about` names, which only the reflexive ability knows.`reselect-attack` (`target` or `filter`, `onlyPlayers`, through `Game.reselectAttack`): the `enter-attacking` decision with `EnterAttackingChoice.reselect`, emitting `attack-reselected` (rule 508.7). `become-blocked` (`target` or `filter`, through `Game.becomeBlocked`): an attacker blocked by the effect, no creature blocking it (rule 509.1h), which emits `attacker-blocked` only for one that was unblocked (509.3c). `choose-permanents`' `keepTargets` hands `then` the resolution's targets from slot 1 (the awaiting's `thenTargets`). `unless`'s sacrifice option takes `totalPowerAtLeast`, `choose-permanents`' `together` the same (`ChoosePermanentsTogether`), a `look-and-choose`'s `totalPowerAtMost`, and a `vote` for a permanent its own `prompt`. `filter.ts`'s `{ chosenThisWay: "power" }` operand is the greatest power chosen so far in this resolution. `{ manaSymbolsOf, color }` and an aggregate `of: { manaSymbols }` over a this-way set are chroma; `{ devoured: "source", filter? }` what the source devoured, as each last was; `choose-value`'s `"commander-mana-value"` asks on the existing menu only when the mana values differ, its `then` reading `"x"` (`value.xByOption`); `countOf`'s `excludeTriggerObject`. `{ amongPlayers: "greatest" | "least", of, who?, includingLeft? }` (2026-11-08, the player aggregate) is any amount read once per player with that player as its `"each"`, the greatest kept: inside `difference`, "the player with the most" (Tales of the Ancestors, Scholarship Sponsor); a scoped `draw` reads every player's number before anyone draws (608.2h). `choose-one-of-each` is one for each of several categories, asked through `ResolutionContext.askOneOfEach` on the `choose-permanents` or `choose-from-zone` decision, one player at a time, with `chosen` and `rest` once all have answered. `cast-now`'s `suspendCost` (The Face of Boe). `manifest-dread`'s `count` is an amount read once (`MAX_COPY_COUNT` caps it); `put-counters-of`'s `target: "created"` puts the set on each token the resolution created, the kinds read before any goes on. `allow-cast-linked-exile`'s `spendAs` rides on the grant (`linkedCastGrants`), which `Game.spendAsFor` reads. `search-library`'s `upTo` is a count printed "up to": fewer, none included, may be found in a public zone too, where `min: 0` alone is only 701.23b's leave to miss in a hidden one (The Five Doctors). `choose-creature-type`'s `sentinel` (`FIRST_CHOSEN_TYPE`) lets a second choice nest inside the first (Vision Charm). `DelayedLeaves.leaves: "created"` watches each token the resolution made on its own (`createdTokensApart`), and a `leaves` watch takes `about` like the step timings. `skip-step`'s `"combat"` is a whole combat phase (rules 500.11, 614.10): `Game.enterStep` goes straight to what follows a combat phase, reading `PlayerState.stepsToSkip.combat`, `TurnState.skipsThisTurn` and `TurnState.noCombat` (`PlayerState.noCombatNextTurn` until that turn begins) through `skipsStepNow`, and the phase doesn't count in `combatPhases` (`TurnState.skipsEachThisTurn`, 2026-11-11: `skip-step`'s `eachThisTurn`, Fatespinner — every draw or upkeep step or main phase left this turn, read first in `skipsStepNow` and never used up; `enterStep` passes a skipped main phase as it does a step, uncounted in `mainPhases`; every combat phase is `noCombat`). `may.costTimes` (a cost paid a chosen number of times, one question on the `choose-modes` offer's `xCost.times`, the number the X it applies with), `delayed-trigger`'s `DelayedWhenever` is the one general watch: `watching` names what `"self"` in its trigger reads as (fixed as it resolves, `delayTrigger` keeping only what's on the battlefield, each for this stint), `until: "game"`, `once` and `orLosesControl` (Coffin Queen); `DelayedWheneverAt` is it with those read. `CopyForEach`, `copy-spell`'s `forEachItCouldTarget` with `controller`, judged from the caster (`Game.copySpellForEachItCouldTarget`), `other: "targeted"` and `chooseAndPayEach`. `unless`'s `chooser` is any `PlayerRef` (resolved here to a player) or `{ anyOf }` (a chain of one-player `unless`es, turn order); `UnlessOption`'s `doing` (any effect by the chooser, gated by `mayBeDone`, exported for `Game.optionModes`) and `payManaCostOf` (`ctx.manaCostToPay`, rule 107.3h). a `restrict` over a filter with `sideOf`, `duration: "until-end-of-combat"` (`expires: end-of-combat`, read by `combat/eligibility.ts`). `counter`'s `thenSource` runs an effect on the countered ability's source while it's still that permanent (`counterSpellByEffect` hands it back), and `for-each-on-stack` is a combinator over the stack as `for-each-permanent` is over the battlefield (`ctx.onTheStack`: never what's resolving, rule 608.2n; an ability object once per copy). `choose-permanents`'s `together` is the set rule of a `choose-from-zone` on the battlefield. `PointDuration`, values of `PermanentDuration` and `gain-control`'s `until`. `choose-permanents`'s `random` picks one match, nobody asked, and runs `then` with it at slot 0. `player-effect`'s `noMaxHandSize` and `duration: "rest-of-game"`, read by `Game.maxHandSizeOf` at the effect's `timestamp` (rule 613.11). `playerCounters` reads energy too (`HeldPlayerCounter`, `playerCountersHeld`). `search-library`'s `zones` takes every set of library, graveyard and hand (`SearchZones`, read through `SEARCHED_ZONES`; "and/or" is the `searchZonesAndOr` helper's `modal`), and `exileRest` / `inAnyOrder` (Doomsday); `exile-graveyard`'s `fromHand`. `end-the-turn` and `end-the-combat-phase` (rule 724, through `ctx.endTurnOrCombat`); `limit-blockers`. a A land with an Adventure (rule 715, 2026-11-03) is a `play-land` from exile while `onAdventure` (`landPlayableReason`), not a cast. `paradigm` (rule 702.192a, 2026-11-03) is an emblem as `epic`'s is, its `cast-now` with `copyOfCardNamed` (a copy made in exile by name), set up once per name by `PlayerState.paradigmResolved`. `vote` with `for: { options, about }` (2026-11-03) is one question per player, "for each player, choose friend or foe", each answer a `VoteChoice` with `about`, read by the scopes `"each-friend"` and `"each-foe"` over every answer the resolution holds. `vote` for a permanent (2026-11-01): `count` and `upTo` (one vote a permanent, each token of a stack singled out as `castVote` does), `keep` (what's chosen is spared: asked as "to keep", the strongest first, unasked when all there is must be chosen; a default answer keeps its own), `startingWith: "you-then-active-player"`. A number a player chooses (2026-11-01, the one `choose-number` decision throughout): a `vote` of `for: { number }`, open or secret, its answer cast by `cast-vote`'s `"number"` from the resumed `x` and read by the amount `{ votedNumber }` and the condition `voted-number` (`Game`'s `conditionMet`), with `VoteChoice`'s `{ number }` logged by `vote-cast`/`votes-finished` rather than `number-chosen`; `choose-value`'s `who` (another chooser) and `max` as an amount (read before the answer replaces X; `{ leastOf }` is new for it) and `remember` (`AwaitingDecision`'s `remember`, written to `chosenOnEnter` if it's still that stint, rule 400.7); `sacrifice-all-but`'s `keep` as an amount and `who` naming one player through a target. `additionalCost.chooseNumber` (`define.ts`) is an X announced as it's cast, the offer's `xCost.chosenNumber` for the client's words. `PlayerScope`'s `{ controllerOf: "attached" }` (a resolution reads it, through `attachedKnown`) and `"opponents-of-trigger-controller"`; `for-each-player`'s `before`, which finds `whoIf`'s players, does the instruction, then runs `effect` for those found (engine-built `found`), so "each opponent who can't" isn't asked after the discard; `amass`'s `who`, `modify-pt-all`'s `controlledByPlayer`, `become`'s `controlledByTarget`. `skip-turn` (`PlayerState.turnsToSkip`, used up in `Game`'s turn advance by `skipOwedTurn`, rule 614.10) and `take-extra-turn`'s `thatTurn` (`GameState.extraTurnRiders`, by the queued turn's index; taken up as that turn begins, gone with a skipped one; `atEndStep` is a delayed ability at `"this-turn-end-step"`). `reveal-until` and `reveal-until-count` share `RevealedCardsGo` (where the revealed cards go, `Game.placeRevealed`) and `revealingPlayer`; `reveal-until`'s `thenKeepsTargets` applies `then` under `withFoundBeforeTargets`, the card found at slot 0 and the resolution's own targets a slot up, which `target-polarity.ts` reads through `childAfterFound` (2026-11-01). The `EffectSpec` vocabulary (a `target` is an `ObjectRef`: a slot, `"source"`, `"trigger-object"`, or `"attached"` — "enchanted/equipped creature", what the source is attached to as the effect resolves, else what it was last attached to while that's still the same object, `ResolutionContext.attachedHost` over `LastKnownInfo.attachedTo`/`attachedToStint`; or `"chosen"`, the source's chosen permanent (`ResolutionContext.chosenPermanent`); or `"trigger-recipient"`, what the triggering event was aimed at (`ResolutionContext.triggerRecipient`, `LastKnownRefs.recipient`; `DelayedTrigger.recipient` carries it into a delayed trigger, 603.7c); a `delayed-trigger`'s `about: "attached"` carries it as the delayed ability's target — Wings of Hubris): `sequence` (`simultaneous?` — its steps are one instruction over several targets, so what they move leaves together), `damage`/`draw`/`modify-pt`/`modify-pt-all`/`create-token`/`create-token-copy` `count` fields accept an `EffectAmount = number | "x" | {countOf: CardFilter} | {triggerValue: true} | …` (a scoped effect reads a per-player amount — `{lifeTotal: "each"}`, "half their life" with `{half, round}` — once per player, via `amountValue`'s third argument); `damage` also takes `target: "source"` ("deals 3 damage to itself", untargeted, nothing once it has left), `toTriggerRecipient` ("that permanent or player") and `from: "trigger-object"` ("it deals damage" — the triggering object is the source); `mill`/`discard-chosen` (a target player's hand revealed — or only looked at — the controller chooses, that player discards; a `choose-from-zone` with destination `"discard"`, `Game.beginDiscardChosen`; `upTo` is "up to X cards"; `reveals`/`picks`, "reveals three cards from their hand and you choose one of them", ask the hand's owner which to show by a `choose-from-zone` of destination `"reveal"` carrying `thenChooseFromRevealed`, then the chooser over those alone: `Game.beginRevealFromHand`, `beginRevealedChoice`) and `look-at-hand` ("look at target player's hand" alone: the same decision with nothing to pick, `max: 0`); `discard` `target` is a slot or a `PlayerScope`; a `PlayerScope` includes the event-named `"trigger-player"` and `"each-other-opponent"` (from `LastKnownRefs.player`, resolved by `Game.scopedPlayers`); `add-mana` (`+ painToController?`; `target`, a player slot, is "target player adds" — on the stack, its colour the controller's pick (The Warring Triad); `mana: { oneOf, same: true }` is all of one listed type — "X {G} or X {W}"; a `BoardManaList` is read off the board as the mana is made — `producedBy` ("any type a land you control could produce", rule 106.7), `colorAmong` ("any color among …", on the battlefield, in your graveyard or linked to the source) and `eachColorAmong` ("for each color among …"), resolved by `EffectApi.manaTypesOf`; `amount: { devotionTo: "that-color" }` is read for the colour an `"any-color"` is making, `ResolutionContext.manaColor`; `{ greatestCommanderManaValue: true }` is the commanders' greatest mana value wherever they are (Cactus Preserve); `who` is "each player adds …" (Yurlok); outside a mana ability — `ResolutionContext.manaAbility` unset — a choice in it is asked as it resolves (rule 608.2d), `addManaChoice` turning it into `modal`s over concrete `add-mana`s: one colour for all, one choice of split up to 35, else one `split-mana` decision through `ResolutionContext.splitMana` — each unit in turn before 2026-10-08, and still on a bare context without it), `gain-life`/`lose-life`, `tap`/`untap`/`untap-all`, `destroy`/`destroy-all` (`destroy-all`'s `exceptSource` is "all other creatures", one token of a stack spared by `destroyAllByEffect`; `destroy`'s `target: "trigger-recipient"` is what a damage trigger's damage hit — Stinkweed Imp; `tap` and `exile` take it too — Kashi-Tribe Warriors, Pit Spawn), `damage-all`, `fight {a, b, oneSided?}`, `counter {target, into?: "hand"}` (`into` is Remand's "put it into its owner's hand instead" — still a counter, unlike `return-to-hand`'s `from: "stack"`), `gain-control {target, untilEndOfTurn, who?}` (`who` an `PlayerRef` — a player slot, "that player" the event names; Zedruu's "target opponent gains control of target permanent you control", Alexios's `"active-player"`) / `gain-control-all {filter, untilEndOfTurn, who?}` (one timestamp for every match, `who: "owner"` for "each player … they own") / `rotate-control {direction, filter}` (Aminatou's −6: left is onward in turn order), `loyalty-activations {target, times: 2, duration}` (a resolved "twice this turn rather than only once" for one permanent, rule 606.3 — `PtModifier.loyaltyActivations`, read by `Game.loyaltyActivationsAllowed`; Kaito, Dancing Shadow), `cant-be-sacrificed {target|filter, duration, by?}` (`by`: that player can't sacrifice them — `PtModifier.cantBeSacrificedBy`, a rule and not an ability, read with `cantBeSacrificed` in `Game.canBeSacrificed`; Call for Aid), `return-to-hand`/`return-to-hand-all`/`exile`/`exile-graveyard`/`mill`/`return-from-graveyard`/`discard`, `grant-keyword`/`grant-keyword-all`, `restrict {target|filter, restrictions}` (combat restrictions until end of turn — a modifier, or with `filter` a turn-wide rule, read from a target player's or a target's controller's side by `controlledByTarget`, leaving out `exceptTarget` — the rule's stint-held `except`, matched by `characteristics.ts`'s `playerEffectCovers` — or with `asItResolves` modifiers on the permanents matching it then, rule 611.2c), `prevent-damage-divided {from}` (a `prevent-damage` shield per target of an `any-number` group, its share of the `divided` total — rules 601.2d, 615.7), `add-counter`/`remove-counter`/`proliferate` (`remove-counter`'s `amount: "all"` takes every counter of its kind, with `counter: "any"` every kind — Vampire Hexmage; `remove-counter-all {filter, counter, amount}` does that to every permanent matching, read before any comes off — Oblivion Stone, Aether Snap, Pestilent Haze — a token stack losing them together in one `counter-removed` with `tokens`; both through `Game.removeCountersFrom`), `remove-counters`'s `anyController` (its `from` over everyone's permanents — Eventide's Shadow) and a player target (a slot's `orPlayer`: their counters, energy included, asked kind by kind on `choose-modes` through the engine-set `playerProgress`, `applyPlayerCounterRemoval`, and taken off together by the context's `removePlayerCounters`), `move-counters {from, to, counter?, amount?, ifMoved?}` (rule 122.5: a `counter-removed` and then an `addCounter` put by the controller, neither when either can't happen — the same object, none of the kind, either end gone; `amount` 1 asks the kind as a `choose-modes` among those there when unnamed, `"all"`, `"each-kind-missing"`, and `"any"`/`"one-or-more"` go on across their decisions — `applyMoveCounters`, a `choose-modes` per kind for how many, and with an end `{ choose, prompt }` a `choose-permanents` for it before each pair, the copy each answer applies carrying `MoveCountersProgress` (ids, which can't change zones mid-resolution)) and `put-counters-of {of, target}` (rule 122.8: the same number of each kind `of` had, read as it last existed — not a move), `shuffle-into-library` (a permanent into its owner's library, shuffled — Chaos Warp, whose `reveal-top` takes `of: { ownerOf }` for the owner's library), `another-counter-of-each-kind` (proliferate's step on one permanent or player, `Game.anotherCounterOfEachKind`, shared with the `proliferate` answer; `orRemove` asks a `choose-modes` per kind), `double-counters-all`/`double-pt-all` (a one-shot doubling effect reading each matching permanent's own current count/P-T, distinct from Doubling Season's replacement-based multiplier; `double-pt-all`'s `powerOnly` and `exceptSource` — God-Eternal Rhonas), `animate {target, power, toughness, addTypes, addSubtypes, setSubtypes?, setColors?, loseAbilities?, keywords?, duration}` (`power`/`toughness` may be live amounts, fixed as it applies) / `animate-all {filter, power, toughness, addTypes?, addSubtypes?, keywords?, duration}` (every match at once, a token stack whole), `lose-abilities {target, loseLandTypes?, activated?, duration}` (Ultima's blight — what it "has" granted by the same effect, rule 613.7); a `duration` (`PermanentDuration`) is `"end-of-turn"`, `"permanent"`, `"until-your-next-turn"` (the effect's controller's, kept on the modifier as `untilTurnOf` and ended as `beginTurn` begins that turn — or passes over a departed player's seat, rule 800.4m) or `{whileCounter}` (ended by the `counter-removed` event that takes the last one, rule 611.2b), `grant-flashback`, `take-extra-turn`/`additional-combat`, `storm`/`cascade`/`copy-spell` (`target` is a target slot, `"source"` — the spell now resolving copying itself — or `"trigger-spell"`, the spell whose casting fired the trigger; `newTargets`; `ifTargets: "permanent-or-player"`; `otherwise`, applied when nothing was copied; with `"trigger-spell"`, `retargetTo: "source" | slot` — every target of the copy that one, or no copy if it isn't legal for each, rule 707.10e, Ivy — and `forEachItCouldTarget: {filter, other?}` — one copy per permanent it could target, each aimed at its own, ordered by their controller through a `choose-permanents` whose `then` is an engine-built `copy-spell` carrying the captured `spell`, rule 707.10d, Zada; `count` — "copy it for each …", an amount read as it applies, each copy asked about in turn — Thousand-Year Storm, Thunderclap Drake; `except` — a `CopyExceptions` as one copiable modifier on the copy, 707.9b, which `moveObject` keeps as the copy enters a token, Double Major, Tawnos, Donal; `gains` — keywords and triggered abilities on a `castRider` modifier the permanent keeps, 400.7a, `ifPermanent` read on the copy, Nalfeshnee — both handed to `Game.copySpellFrom` as a `SpellCopyChange`), `copy-ability {target: slot | "trigger-ability", newTargets?}` (rule 707.10: a copy of an activated or triggered ability on the stack — the original object with every choice made for it, the same source (707.10b) and one instance of a stacked one, under the effect's controller; `"trigger-ability"` is the ability whose activation fired an `activates-ability` trigger, copied as it last was there from `GameState.departedAbilities` once countered — Lithoform Engine, Weaver of Harmony, Vantress Visions, Illusionist's Bracers), `exile-spell-as-it-resolves {returnAtNextEndStep?}` (Feather: marks the trigger's spell — `GameObject.exileAsItResolves` — to be exiled instead of going to its owner's graveyard as it resolves and, with `returnAtNextEndStep`, returned at the next end step; `applyFirst` is the engine-built answer to the rule 616.1 choice `Game.leaveStackAfterResolving` asks beside another exiling replacement), `create-token {token, count, who?: "target-controller"|PlayerScope, thenCounters?, exileAtEndStep?}` (`exileAtEndStep` — exiled, not sacrificed, at the next end step; `thenCounters` — "then put X +1/+1 counters on it", put on each token it made as separate objects, never a stack, as `separate` makes them for an effect that tells its tokens from older ones; `basePt` — an X/X token's base P/T, part of what it is), `create-token-copy {of: "source"|"trigger-object"|"equipped"|"enchanted"|slot, or a card read as printed under the effect's controller — "exiled-with-source" (`chooseOne` for "a card exiled with this", through a `look-and-choose` of destination "stay"), { paid: "exiled" } (`LastKnownRefs.sets.paid`), { thisWay: "exiled" } — any card set (`ObjectSetRef`, `isCardSet`; not { thisWay: "destroyed" | "died" | "sacrificed" } or { paid: "sacrificed" | "tapped" | "unattached" }, permanents copied as they last existed through the member's `was`, rule 608.2h — Red Sun's Twilight, Cleaver Skaab) —, {eachExiled}, {eachInGraveyard} (Grist, the Plague Swarm) — no token of an instant or sorcery card (rule 111.5), count (an EffectAmount), sacrificeAtEndOfCombat?, atYourNextEndStep?, exceptions.static?, gainsHaste?, gains?: {keywords?, triggered?} (gained on a non-copiable modifier — Jaxis), exileAtEndStep?, notLegendary?, basePt?, exceptions?, attachedTo?}` (`attachedTo`: each created attached to that permanent through the entering path, `EnteringTokenCopies.attachTo` — an Aura that can't enchant it isn't created, an Equipment enters unattached, Arna Kennerüd; copy exceptions — a name, types, subtypes, supertypes added or "isn't legendary", colours, base P/T, keywords, activated and triggered abilities, the legend-rule static — are the copy's copiable values, the same `CopyExceptions` a `copyOnEnter` takes; a creature subtype one adds doesn't stick to a copy that isn't a creature, rule 205.3d; rule 707.9b: `copiable` modifiers under every other effect, which a token copy or a Clone of it copies along; a copy of something that has left — a creature that died — copies its last-known copiable values, `LastKnownInfo.copiable`), `conditional {condition, then, else?}`, `impulse-exile`'s `whoseIf` (only the players of `whose` a condition holds for, each asked about as `"that-player"` — the `player-counters` condition's `who: "that-player"`, Ixhel's "each opponent who has three or more poison counters"; they still exile at once), `attach` (onto a target, the created token, the trigger's object or the source; one attachment, or every match of `attachments`), `transform`/`day-night`, `become-monarch`/`get-energy`/`create-emblem`, `increase-speed {amount}` (the effect's controller's speed, rule 702.179c — what the inherent speed ability does; the `{ speed: "you" }` `EffectAmount` reads it, 0 for none; a `damage`'s `who` narrows by `whoseIf`, a condition asked of each player as `"that-player"`, the rest dealt 0, which is nothing), `prevent-all-combat-damage`/`prevent-damage`, `modal {minModes, maxModes, modes}`/`may {effect, prompt}` (non-targeted only — a targeted modal spell uses `CardDefinition.castModal` instead; a modal triggered ability's `announced` modes are chosen as it goes on the stack — `GameState.pendingModalTrigger`, a `choose-modes` marked `announcing` — and ride on the ability as `chosenModes`, rules 603.3c, 700.2b), `unless {chooser, options, otherwise}` (a punisher: someone else — or `"you"` — chooses whether to take one of the `options`; `chooser: "any-player"` is the rhystic "unless any player pays", which `Game.beginUnless` unrolls into a chain of one-player `unless`es — `{ player }` choosers — from the active player in turn order, each the one before's `otherwise`; the `takeDamage` option is Browbeat's "have ~ deal N damage to them"), `each-player-may {who, effect|options|choices, ifDid?, ifDidnt?, resultsFor?}` (each player in a scope — or, with `who: {controllerOf}`, the one who controls a target, as it last existed — asked in turn, APNAP, each question parked until the one before is answered and done — an option's `payLifeAmount` read for each player as they're asked, "half their life" being their own (Temporal Extortion) — then per-player follow-ups as the controller's effect with `"that-player"` naming the player and, when they sacrificed a permanent in answer, `"sacrificed"` naming it — Braids, Arisen Nightmare; `choices` is a villainous choice), `sacrifice {who, filter, count, exceptSource?}`, `sacrifice-source {then?}`, `search-library` (whose `destination` includes `"library-top"` — the tutor-to-top family, placed *after* the search's own shuffle), `put-on-library {target, position}`, `put-on-libraries {what: {targetsFrom} | {all} | {graveyardOf}, position: top | bottom | owner-chooses | shuffle | bottom-random, withSource?, progress?}` (`bottom-random`: Endurance's "in a random order", one card after another at a random place among those already down there, through `CommanderLibraryPlacement`'s `randomlyAmong`, which a commander's deferred 903.9b move keeps; nothing asked; `shuffle`: each library a card went into shuffled, logged as `cards-shuffled-into-library` — the `shuffled-into-library` this-way kind, which `shuffle-graveyard-into-library`'s new `filter` path logs too; several cards into their owners' libraries as one instruction, then each owner's order on each end, APNAP — rule 401.4, through `beginLibraryOrder`'s `choose-from-zone`; `owner-chooses` first asks each owner which of their permanents go on the bottom, a `choose-permanents` `keepChoice` — `ctx.askLibraryEnds`, `putOnLibrariesTogether`, `askLibraryOrder`), `flicker {target, underYourControl?, returnAt?, thenCounters?}` (a blink of a slot, `"source"` or several slots at once, returned together — at once, or by a delayed trigger linked to the exile; `thenCounters.entering` has them come back *with* the counters, on before their enter events go out), `delayed-trigger {at, effect, text, controller?}` (rule 603.7 — "at the beginning of the next end step, …", or `at: {leaves, to, thisTurn?}` for "when it dies"; carrying the creating effect's targets forward since a delayed ability chooses none of its own), `earthbend {target, amount}`, `reflexive-trigger {targets, effect, text}` (rule 603.12 — "when you do, …": a trigger queued as a `PendingTrigger` carrying its own `ReflexiveTrigger` record, placed at the next priority check and choosing its targets then), `scry`/`surveil`, `look-and-choose` (`secondPick` — a second choice over what the first left, before the rest go, or with `ifNoneChosen` only if the first took nothing; `destination: "exile"` is imprint's face-up linked exile (Chrome Mox — `GameObject.exiledWith`, as hideaway's `"exile-face-down"`); `reveal: "chosen"` shows only the cards taken, the second choice's too; `player: "that-player"` in a `for-each-player` is that player's own zone and choice — Explore the Vastlands). An amount that reads an object (`powerOf`/`toughnessOf`/`manaValueOf`/`manaSpentOf`) takes an `AmountRef` — a slot, `"source"`, `"trigger-object"`, or `"sacrificed"` (the permanent sacrificed to pay the cost — a mana ability's too, handed to it as it resolves at once — or by a `sacrifice-source` step — `ResolutionContext.sacrificed`, set by `EffectApi.withSacrificed`), each read as it last existed on the battlefield if it has left. `EffectApi`/`ResolutionContext` (`ctx.x`, `amountValue`), `applyEffectSpec` — which does nothing once `ctx.gameOver()` (a `win-game` or a Laboratory Maniac draw ended the game mid-resolution). `win-game` (its controller wins) and `lose-game { who?, target? }` end or shrink the game through `ctx.winGame`/`ctx.loseGame`; `player-effect` takes the game-outcome locks (`cantLoseGame`, `cantWinGame`, `cantLoseLife`, `damageLifeFloor`), each a `PlayerScope` fixed as it resolves; `put-on-library`'s `position` may be `{ fromTop: n }`; `gain-control-all`'s `controlledBy: "trigger-player"` keeps only that player's permanents. `additional-upkeep-steps { amount }` adds that many beginning phases, each only an upkeep step, after the combat phase under way (`ctx.additionalUpkeeps`; not on another player's turn, rule 500.10a). `monstrosity { amount }` (rule 701.37a — its own source, the same object only), `become-saddled { target }` (rule 702.171b; `EffectApi.becomeSaddled`), `grant-flashback`'s fixed `cost` (Archmage's Newt's {0}), `bindDynamicCompares` binding a filter's `saddledSource` to `saddledBy: ctx.source`, `exert { target, asItAttacks? }` (701.43), `untap-all`'s `exceptSource`, `look-and-choose`'s `zone: "graveyards"` (every graveyard; the battlefield-bound go under the chooser's control) and `enterAs` (`EnterTypes`, now with `addColors`; since 2026-10-31 also `addTypes`, `setColors`, `basePt`, `keywords` and `ifCreature`, and on `put-onto-battlefield` as its own `enterAs`), and `{ cardsInHand: "each" }` for a per-player amount. `look-and-choose`'s `min` takes a live amount (Teferi's Puzzle Box's whole hand), `destination: "library-bottom"` puts the chosen cards on the bottom in pick order (Valakut Awakening), and `leftover: "bottom-any-order"` is "the rest on the bottom in any order" — ordered by the chooser next (Stock Up, Dig Through Time), and `"top-any-order"` "the rest on top in any order" (Cruel Fate); `from: PlayerRef` is another player's library, the effect's controller looking and choosing (Architects of Will — that player as the zone's owner, the controller as Tasigur's picker); `discard`'s `filter` limits a discard to matching cards and `discard-hand`'s `filter`/`reveal` is "reveals their hand and discards all [filter] cards"; `ThisWayKind` `"put-on-bottom"` counts cards put from a hand on the bottom; `flicker`'s `tapped` (Nezahal, Primal Tide). A `castModal` mode's `spreeCost` is spree (rule 702.172a). `gift { gift: GiftKind }` gives a promised gift (rule 702.174 — `card`, `tapped-fish`, `food`, `treasure`, `octopus`, `extra-turn`) to the opponent the spell (or the permanent's spell) promised it to, via the `giveGift` hook. `lose-abilities-all { filter, duration }` is the mass `become` (Final Showdown), one timestamp over what matches as it applies. `add-counter-all.controlledByTarget` reads its filter from a targeted player's side, as `modify-pt-all`'s does, and puts none when that player is an illegal target. Also: `set-life-total` (`who`, `amount`: rule 119.5, a real gain or loss of the difference, every player's amount read before any life moves; `EffectAmount`'s `startingLifeTotal` and the `amongPlayers` "least" of `lifeTotal`); `exchange-life-totals` (`first?`, `second`: rule 701.12c, all or nothing under rules 119.7–8, 2026-11-01; `{ lifeDifference }` and `player-compare`'s `within` read beside it — Mirror Universe, Soul Conduit, Mister Negative, Psychic Transfer, Profane Transfusion); `exchange-life-stat` (`stat`, `target`, `player?`, which was `exchange-life-toughness` until `stat: "power"` — Evra, Halcyon Witness — Tree of Redemption, Tree of Perdition); `add-counter-all`'s `amount: "own-toughness"` (Canopy Gargantuan); `reflexive-trigger`'s `value` (an amount read as it triggers, its `triggerValue` — Tip the Scales' X), its `divided` (an amount divided among its group, announced as it's put on the stack — Death to Our Enemies), and its `about` (the token or card the creating resolution made, or one of its targets, read as the reflexive ability's trigger object from that stint — Dáin Ironfoot, Back for More, Curse of the Werefox); an aggregate's `"toughness"` over a this-way set; `exile`'s `linked` and `return-exiled-by-source`'s `linked: "battlefield"` (Colfenor's Urn; `under: "you"` for a "put", 110.2a — Underworld Sentinel; `others` and `filter` for "all other permanent cards exiled with" it — Mirror of Life Trapping), `look-and-choose`'s `zone: "exiled-with-source"` (the linked cards only, resolved to `{ exiled }` for `beginZoneChoice`, whose exile branch now also carries `withCounters`, `enterAs` and `attacking` — Nautiloid Ship, Chaos Shrine's Black Crystal) and `exile-graveyard`'s `linked` (2026-10-31, `put-exiled-with-source.test.ts`); `ManaSpendOnly.castFrom`, `notCastFrom`. Also: the `{ attackingPlayer: "each" }` amount (creatures attacking the player a per-player effect acts on — Within Range); `countOf`'s `forTarget` (the filter read from a target player's side — Will of the Mardu) and `forPlayer` (from one `PlayerRef`'s side — the chosen player, a target's controller, the trigger's player); `countInGraveyard`'s `forEachPlayer`; `{ controllerOf }` (any `ChosenPlayerRef`) as `draw`'s `who`, `discard`'s `target` and `add-player-counters`' `who`; `add-mana`'s `{ colorsAmong }`; `player-effect`'s `tokenMultiplier`. Also `put-in-command-zone`, and `look-and-choose`'s `zone: "command"`. Also `become-copy` (an existing permanent — a `target`, or every one a `filter` matches, `exceptOf` leaving out what's copied — becomes a copy of a permanent, spell or card (`ofCard`), for good or `until` end of turn, the next end step or your next turn, with copy exceptions — Sarkhan, Soul Aflame, Mirrorform, Niko; `CopyExceptions.thisAbility` is "except it has this ability"), `modal`'s `notChosenBefore` ("choose one that hasn't been chosen" — Silent Hallcreeper), `create-token`'s `attacksThisCombat`, `create-token-copy`'s `of: { each }`, `reveal-until`'s `whose: "that-player"` and the `"tapped"` this-way kind. Also `attack-random-opponent`, `tap` of `"source"`, `exile-graveyard`'s `filter`, `sacrifice-all`, `put-exiled-this-way-onto-battlefield` (Living Death), `move-exiled-this-way` (2026-10-31 — every card the resolution exiled, a permanent too, still in exile, `to` its owner's hand — Worlds Within Worlds — or the bottom of its owner's library in a random order — Herald of Amity), `look-and-choose`'s `playable` (the exiled picks get an impulse permission, this turn or while exiled, through the `choose-from-zone` decision's `impulseGrant`) and `leftover: "exile"` and `for-each-convoker` (Lethal Scheme; `connive` reads a departed target through `readTargets`). Also `choose-exiled-to-play`, `put-arrived-onto-battlefield`, `choose-opponent` (with the engine-built `about-player`) and `look-and-choose`'s `chooser`. Also the `{ distinctTokenNames }` amount (tokens counted by their rule 111.4 names). Also `cast-now`'s `from: "exiled-this-way"` and `repeat` (play any number of them as it resolves — Gix; each re-offer is the cards first offered, so a card exiled later in the resolution, as a cost of casting one, isn't among them). Also voting (rule 701.38): `each-player-may`'s `startingWithYou` with `choices`, read back by the `{ votesFor }` amount; and `reveal-until-count` (Selvala's Stampede). `PermanentDuration`'s `"while-source"`, `gain-control`'s `whileSource` and `restrict`'s `duration` (Opportunistic Dragon). Also `DamageFrom` (`damage`/`damage-all` dealt by a target), `prevent-all-combat-damage`'s `by`, `tap`'s `doesntUntapNext`, `return-from-graveyard`'s `from: "all-graveyards"` and `enterAs`, `look-and-choose`'s `zone: "targets"`, `cast-now`'s `from: "targets"`, and an aggregate's `"mana-value"` over a this-way set (`"max"` the greatest — Heretic's Punishment, 2026-10-31), and the `thisWay` amount's `sharingManaValue` counts only those sharing a mana value with another of them (Vault 21: House Gambit, 2026-11-01). `look-and-choose`'s `zone: "exiled-with-source"` takes `destination: "stay"` (then `then` acts on it — Lazav) and `under: "owners"` (Purgatory), `choose-exiled-to-play` takes `linked` (Muse Vessel) and `discard-chosen` takes `mayCast` (Psychic Intrusion), 2026-11-01; `grantPtPerCount.aggregate` is a count-scaled bonus over any `AggregateSpec` (Carrion Grub, Emissary Escort), and `grantPtPerCount.turnHistory` one over a this-turn journal list through `journalCount` (Kinbinding, 2026-11-11). The `PlayerScope` `"you-and-opponents-attacking-trigger-player"` (the controller, then each opponent controlling a creature attacking the trigger player as it resolves — the Commander 2017 Curses' "each opponent attacking that player does the same"), and `untap-all`'s `who` (each player in a scope untaps what matches from their own side — Curse of Bounty). `PlayerRef`'s `{ controllerOf }` (the controller of a target slot's permanent, as it last existed), which `cast-now`'s `by` (who is offered the cast — Transforming Flourish's "they may cast that card") and `reveal-until`'s `whose` take; and `copy-spell`'s `controller` (the copy goes on the stack under that player, who chooses its new targets — demonstrate, `demonstrate()` in `cards/helpers.ts`). `exileByEffect` takes a card a `reveal-until` has revealed in a library (Creative Technique). The 2026-10-09 precon stand-ins added: `add-mana`'s `spellGains` (the spell the unit pays for gains keywords until end of turn, a `castRider` — rule 106.6), `damage`'s `excessToController` (beyond lethal to the creature's controller, both at once — rule 120.4a; `lethalDamageTo` and `damageSourceHas` on the context; `equippedCreature` is the host of `of: "equipped"`, the departed source's last host once it has left), `for-each-player`'s `who: "owners-of-exiled-with-source"` and the aggregate `in: "exiled-with-source"` (the cards linked to the source, rule 607.2a — Skyclave Apparition). Random choices (2026-10-11): `choose-random-card` (a card at random from a graveyard, bound as `then`'s target 0 — Exalted Flamer of Tzeentch, Deadbridge Chant; from a hand, `show`n as a reveal to everyone or a look by the controller alone through `ResolutionContext.showCardFromHand` — Hired Torturer, Urza's Bauble; from the cards exiled with the source, `zone: "exiled-with-source"` — Skyship Weatherlight, whose `search-library { linked }` exiles them; `slot` binds the card beside the ability's own targets through `withFoundAt` — Cursed Scroll; `random-card-from-zone.test.ts`), `choose-opponent`'s `random`/`anyPlayer`, and a resolution-time `modal`'s `random` with `ModeOption.onlyIf` (Crystalline Giant); each picks through `ResolutionContext.pickAtRandom` — `Game.pickAtRandom`, on the seeded `rng`, logged as `chosen-at-random` — and `graveyardMatching` lists the candidates. `blight {amount, who?, then?}` (rule 701.68): each player in `who`, one at a time from the active player, puts the counters on a creature of theirs (`ResolutionContext.blight`); `mayBeDone` keeps a "you may blight" from being offered to a player with no creature a -1/-1 counter can go on (`ResolutionContext.canBlight`, 701.68b), and `then` reads the blighted creature as target 0 (701.68c). `WardCost.blight` is paid with it; `WardCost.discardAtRandom` makes a ward's discard random (Alpharael, Stonechosen). `put-onto-battlefield`'s `attacking` (an `EnterAttacking`, passed through `EffectApi.putOntoBattlefield` to `Game.putOntoBattlefieldByEffect`'s `putIntoAttack`) is "…tapped and attacking" (Chorale of the Void). A `blight`'s `who` may be `{ target: n }` ("target opponent blights 2"). `BeholdSpec {filter, text, count?, exile?}` is behold (rule 701.4a) everywhere it appears; the `behold {filter, text, count?, then?}` effect beholds as it resolves (`ResolutionContext.behold`), `mayBeDone` asking `canBehold` first, and its `then` reads the beheld object as target 0. `exile`'s `airbend` and `exile-all`'s `airbend` (with `exceptTarget`, "all other creatures") are airbend (rule 701.65a); the `player-effect`'s `castOnlyFromHand` is Avatar's Wrath's "your opponents can't cast spells from anywhere other than their hands". `phase-out` (rule 702.26b): `target` / `attachedToSource` ("equipped creature") / `filter` + `controlledByTarget`, `untilSourceLeaves` + `tappedOnReturn` (610.4 — Oubliette), through `ResolutionContext.phaseOut`. **Voting** (rule 701.38) is the `vote` effect: each question a `choose-modes` (options, players) or `choose-permanents` (permanents, read from the controller's side) whose answer's effect is the internal `cast-vote`; the votes are kept in `GameState.voting` (out of every view, so a secret vote stays secret) until `finishVoting` emits `votes-finished`, which the later steps (`{ votesFor }`, `{ votesReceived }`, `vote-won`, `for-each-vote`, `for-each-voted`) and the `players-finish-voting` trigger (`LastKnownRefs.votes`) read. `prevent-damage-from-chosen-source` (rule 609.7) asks for the source as it resolves and makes one shield: a recipient (`to`: you, a slot, your side, `"your-side-creatures"`, anything), an amount (next time, a number, all), a redirection, life gained (`gainLifePrevented`, only from sources matching `gainLifeFrom`) or a `then` (`chosen-source-recipients.test.ts`). `grant-protection` (rule 702.16) grants protection for a duration to a target and/or a filter's matches (one timestamp, `PtModifier.protection`); a `ProtectionChoice` `from` (`choose: "color" | "card-type"`, `or`, `optional`) is turned into one `choose-modes` mode per quality as it resolves (`protectionChoiceOptions`), and `fixProtectionSide` rewrites a filter's `controlledBy` to `controllerIn`. `protectionLabel` words a grant for the log and the view. **Searching another player's zones** (2026-10-28, B248): `search-library`'s `whose` (an `PlayerRef`, which gains `{ ownerOf }`), `zones: "graveyard-hand-library"`, `all` (`choose-from-zone`'s `alsoTaken`: the graveyard's matches, taken with whatever is picked — rule 701.23b), the `"exile"` / `"exile-face-down"` / `"stay"` destinations, `playable` (the decision's `impulseGrant`) and `then` (the finds as targets, `thenPlayer` as "that player"), passed to `Game.beginLibrarySearch` as a `SearchMore`; the decision's `forPlayer` is the library's owner, who shuffles, and battlefield finds enter under the searcher (`enterUnder`). Rule 101.4 holds: a scoped `discard` holds its players' chosen cards (`ctx.holdChoices`, released by an engine-built `release` copy once all have chosen) and an `each-player-may`'s chosen hand or graveyard cards bound for the battlefield wait in `heldChoices.entries` (`Game.enterHeldEntries`). A `vote` with `choose: true` is "each player chooses" (no extra votes, a `choice` flag on its events so voting triggers skip it); `with-choices` and `for-each-vote` substitute `CHOSEN_THIS_WAY` (`substituteChosenThisWay`). `repeat` (`applyRepeat`) parks itself between passes with its `progress` and goes through `may` for `mayAgain`. B253: `grant-static` (a static ability on a permanent's modifier, `target` or a locked `filter`), `restrict`'s `cant-be-blocked` records, `exchange-control` (701.12a/b all or nothing, `then`/`otherwise`), `exchange-control-all`, `gain-control-all`'s `controlledByTarget`, `choose-creature-type`'s fixed `options`/`prompt`. `ChosenPlayerRef` (a target slot's player, or its object's controller or owner) may be a `for-each-player`'s or a `vote`'s chooser (`effectPlayer`); `discard-chosen` takes a `destination` (exile linked to the permanent by default, the library's top or bottom, the battlefield under the chooser), an `alsoFrom` (the graveyard by its own filter, or the library's revealed top card, offered in the same choice — Agonizing Remorse, Psychotic Episode) and a `target` the event names; `switch-pt` (2026-10-29). `search-library`'s `outside` (`true` beside `zones`, `"only"` alone — rule 701.23j's wishes) and `faceUpExile` ("or choose a face-up … card you own in exile") ride through `SearchMore`; `learn()` (cards/helpers.ts) is rule 701.48a whole, its Lesson half that search (2026-10-30). `for-each-player`'s `whoIf` filters its players by a condition read with each as "that player", all of them found before any acts ("each player who …", 2026-10-30); its `whoseAmount` keeps only the players for whom an amount read for them (`thisWay` of `who: "each"`) is 1 or more, and a `search-library`'s `min`/`max` are read for its searcher ("for each creature exiled this way, its controller searches", Winds of Abandon, 2026-11-06). `choose-value` chooses a colour, a card type (`CARD_TYPE_CHOICES`, rule 205.2a), a kind of counter on a target or a matching permanent, or a number as the effect resolves, and runs `then` with it: the word through the `choose-creature-type` decision's menu (its awaiting's `value` carrying the placeholder and the whole `ResolutionResume`), substituted for `CHOSEN_COLOR` / `CHOSEN_CARD_TYPE` / `CHOSEN_COUNTER` by `substituteChosen`; the number through `choose-number`, read by `then` as `"x"`. `reveal-hand` shows a hand to all, its cards `{ thisWay: "revealed" }`; `discard-hand`'s filter reads the resolution's X (2026-10-30). `prohibit`'s other forbidden actions (2026-10-31): `regeneration` (rule 701.19c — on a target, or with `dealtDamageThisWay` every creature the resolution has dealt damage to, `EffectApi.prohibitRegeneration` setting `GameObject.cantBeRegeneratedTurn`, which `Game.regenerateInstead` reads) and `monarch` (`prohibitMonarch`, `PlayerState.cantBecomeMonarchTurn`, read by `Game.canBecomeMonarch` in `setMonarch` and rule 725.4's hand-off); a target's abilities prohibition with `duration: "until-your-next-turn"` is a detain-like modifier without the combat half; `become-monarch`'s `who` may be a target slot (`becomeMonarchPlayer`). `choose-among-targets`: a player picks `count` of the resolving spell's still-legal targets (`askChooseAmong` — `choose-permanents` or `choose-from-zone` with `keepChoice`), and the chosen and the rest take `PileFate`s through `finishPiles`. `meld` (2026-10-31, rule 701.42): `partner` (a `CardFilter`, `{ name, type }`) and `into`; `chosen` is the `choose-permanents` answer's `then`, never authored. `turn-face-up` (rule 708.7 — 2026-11-01) turns a face-down permanent up by an effect, nothing paid, through `Game.becomeFaceUp` as the special action does (`cant-turn-face-up` stops it; an instant or sorcery card is revealed and stays down, 701.40g); `turn-face-down` (rule 708.2a — 2026-10-31) turns a permanent face down in place, with `as`'s listed characteristics; `EnterTypes.faceDown` (`put-onto-battlefield`'s `faceDown`, look-and-choose's `enterAs`) enters a card face down (708.3) as the `"effect"` kind, its "as this enters" choices skipped; `manifest`'s `from` manifests from another player's library under the manifesting player (110.2a), and a look-and-choose from another's library (`from`) puts its battlefield picks under the effect's controller. `each-deals-damage` (2026-10-31) has every permanent matching a filter deal damage — `"its-power"` or a fixed amount — to `"itself"`, a target slot, `"trigger-object"` or `{ who }`, each its own source (rule 120.1), read once (608.2h) and dealt as one damage event (`Game.eachDealsDamageByEffect`, `withDamageBatch`; a token stack deals once per token); `controlledByTarget`, `exceptSource`, `exceptTarget` (Alpha Brawl's "each other creature that player controls"). `look-and-choose`'s `zone: "exile"` and `needsMin` (2026-10-31): exiled cards a filter takes (or, with `from`, that player owns) to a graveyard or onto the battlefield under the chooser's control, a face-down one only by an owner-only filter and by group at random (`Game.exileChoiceCandidates`); `needsMin` is all of `min` or nothing. `search-library`'s `instead` (`SearchInstead`, 2026-10-31): each find to a destination of its own — by a `filter` on the card (`pick: "all"`), a `condition` read as the search resolves, the searcher's choice (`"any"`) or a split of exactly one (`"one"`, rule 609.3 for a single find), `under: "you"` entering those picked under the effect's controller — resolved into `SearchMore.instead` (`ResolvedSearchInstead`); its `withCounters` (rule 122.6, a live amount) and `who: "that-player"` (an `each-player-may`'s `ifDid`, Rootweaver Druid). `player-effect`'s `castFromGraveyard` takes `whose`, `once`, `spendAs` and `exileAfterwards`, and `cast-now`'s `from` takes `"any-graveyard"` (2026-10-31: Whispersteel Dagger, The Great Work, Spectral Arcanist). `allow-cast-from-exile` takes `whileExiled` and `undaunted` (Durnan of the Yawning Portal). `AttachHostRef` (`ObjectRef` or `{ among }`) is the `attachedTo` of `put-onto-battlefield`, `return-from-graveyard`, `search-library` and `look-and-choose`; `AttachRef` adds `"created"` and `"put-this-way"` for `attach`'s `target`/`attachment`, and `attach`'s `target: { among }` attaches to a host chosen as it resolves (`attaching` carries the attachment past the choice). `counters-distributed` (2026-10-31, rule 608.2d): counters distributed among permanents chosen as it resolves — `distributeCounters` reads the total, asks `ctx.askChoosePermanentsKept` (a `choose-permanents` with `keepChoice`, resumed through `resumeAfterDecisions`), then a `choose-value` number per chosen one but the last whose `then` is the effect again with `progress` (the share arriving as `"x"`), and puts the counters on, a stack's tokens singled out per pick; `choose-value` takes a `prompt`. `grant-spells-this-turn` takes `filter`, `next`, `cantBeCountered`, `convoke`, `improvise` (2026-11-05) and `entersWithCounters` beside `triggered` (now optional); `enters-with-counters`' `counter: "sunburst"` is a granted sunburst (2026-10-31). A player named as an owner: `{ ownerOf }` on `gain-life`, `lose-life`, `damage`'s `who`, `mill` and `exile-graveyard` (through `effectPlayer`), the `{ ownerOf: "trigger-object" }` scope (`Game.scopedPlayers`, as the object last existed) and `damage`'s `who: "attached-owner"` with `DamageFrom`'s `"attached"` (`attachedHostNow`) (2026-11-01). `players-pay-any-amount` and `bid-life`, each a question at a time on a copy of the effect carrying its progress, the answer arriving as `"x"` (`applyPlayersPayAnyAmount`, `applyBidLife`), through `ctx.askPlayerAboutEffect` (a `choose-modes` asked of another player whose answer stays the effect's controller's), `ctx.mostPayable` and `ctx.withX` (2026-11-01). `exile-graveyard`'s `faceDown` for whole hands, `return-exiled-by-source`'s `filter` and `others` with `linked: "hand"`, `delayed-trigger`'s `orNone` and `turnOf` (2026-11-02). `return-from-graveyard`'s `from: "all-graveyards"` with `destination: "hand"` is each card to its owner's hand; `sacrifice-all`'s filter reads `isSource` ("all other creatures") (2026-11-02). `return-to-hand`'s `from: "library"` puts a `reveal-top`'s card into its owner's hand; `mayBeDone` refuses a `put-into-graveyard` with nothing in its slot and a `move-exiled-this-way` with nothing exiled, so an `unless { anyOf }` over them asks no one (2026-11-03). `PlayerScope`/`PlayerRef`'s `player-to-your-left`/`-right` are seats computed as it resolves (`scopedPlayers`) and `source-controller` is the source's controller or last controller (only a resolution knows, `scoped`); `flicker.under` returns under a named player (`return-flickered.underPlayer` carries it across an as-enters choice); `reveal-until-count`'s `thenEach` and `thenToDifferentOpponents` act on what entered, the second through the engine-built `distribute-control` (one `choose-modes` a permanent, then `giveControlTogether`, one timestamp); `choose-permanents.controlledByPlayer` narrows to one seat through its `except` list; `for-each-player` takes `source-owner`. A `search-library` the searcher can't make isn't a choice: `mayBeDone`, `applyEachPlayerMay` and `searchableModes` (an and/or `modal`'s library-alone mode) read it through `cantSearchThisLibrary`; a mandatory one goes on and finds nothing. `roll-dice`'s `modifier` is signed (The Deck of Many Things subtracts). `cast-now`'s `from: "opponents-graveyards"` with `repeat` and `onePerGraveyard`: each opponent's graveyard, one spell from each, in the caster's order (Jetsam). `vote`'s `for.permanent.about`: "for each player, you choose", one chooser, one question a player, among what that player controls, `for-each-voted` acting on them (Winnowing). `sacrifice-all`'s `who` may name one player. `choose-for-each-slot` (Call the Spirit Dragons): one permanent chosen for each slot in turn through `ResolutionContext.askForSlot` (a `choose-permanents` keep choice, `slotWords` the slots' words), a stack's token singled out as it's chosen, then `each` over every one chosen at once with `"x"` the slots it was chosen for (rule 608.2f).
- **`characteristics.ts`** (`staticTimestamp`, 2026-11-11) — the timestamp a source's own static applies by (rule 613.7a): the source's, or for a static of a Room's door unlocked on the battlefield the time of the unlock (`GameObject.doorUnlockedAt`, set by `Game.unlockDoor`, dropped by `lockDoor` and on any zone change; a door the Room entered with has none). Steaming Sauna's ruling. Read by `scanContributingStaticSources` (P/T, keywords, restrictions, protection) and `Game.maxHandSizeOf`; with both doors unlocked the left half's statics are the first of the Room's list (`roomCard`). The layer-4 type grants and the other rule-changing statics still read the source's timestamp. `door-unlock-timestamp.test.ts`.
- **`characteristics.ts`** — a static's `hexproofFrom.ownColors` (2026-11-11, Tam, Mindful First-Year): the layer-6 fold adds the permanent's own layer-5 colours to its `hexproofFrom.colors`; `isQualifiedHexproof` doesn't take it for the quality-less "can't be the target of spells".
- **`characteristics.ts`** — a static's `sharesKeywords` (2026-11-11, Wretched Bonemass): `sharedKeywordsOf` reads the cards exiled with the static's source (`liveReferenceObjects`' `exiled-with-source`) for the listed keywords each has, and their own unconditional `hexproofFrom` and `protection` statics; `collectStaticEffects` folds the keywords into the static's applied effect and pushes one more per protection or hexproof-from ability, at the same timestamp. `AbilityCost.collectEvidence`'s record (`abilities.ts`' `evidenceCostOf`, The Capitoline Triad) is collect evidence's payment over matching cards only.
- **`effects.ts`** — `spell-cant-be-countered` (2026-11-11): "target spell can't be countered" (Vexing Shusher) sets `GameObject.uncounterable` on a spell on the stack through `ctx.spellCantBeCountered`; `target-polarity.ts` reads it as help.
- **`effects.ts`** — `cast-now`'s `marksAbilityDone` records the ability done this turn (the journal's `done` entries, through the context's `markAbilityDone`) when a spell is cast off the offer — Planetarium of Wan Shi Tong's "Do this only once each turn", spent only by a cast; its triggered ability's `not this-ability-did-this-turn` condition stops it triggering again. `copy-ability`'s `count` copies the ability that many times ("X times" — Gogo, Master of Mimicry), as `copy-spell`'s does.
- **`effects.ts`** — `impulse-exile`'s `grantIf` (2026-11-13): a `CardFilter` the exiled cards must match to get the permission, judged once all are exiled from the controller's side, an `{ amount }` in it read in this resolution (so `bindDynamicCompares` leaves it unbound, and the context's `cardMatches` answers amounts) — Triple Triad's "the card you own exiled this way and each other card exiled this way with lesser mana value than it". `Game.impulseExile` gives the permission (or the `choose` offer) over those alone.
- **`abilities.ts` / `cards/define.ts`** — "This ability can't be copied" is `ActivatedAbility.cantBeCopied` (Gogo), "This spell can't be copied" `CardDefinition.cantBeCopied` (See Double, a rare field): `Game.copyStackAbility` and `copySpellFrom`, where every copy of an ability or spell is made (rule 707.10), make nothing for one and return `null`. Still a legal target.
- **`filter.ts` / `object-sets.ts`** — `TypeShareReference` (2026-11-13): the four `shares…TypeWith` clauses take a bare `ShareReference` (its objects always in, Killer's and the Crowns' "it and each other …") or `{ typesOf, each? }`, the plain words: a reference object is in only by a type it has (rule 205.3m — a typeless one shares none, even with itself), and `each` needs a type in common with every one, holding with none (Cryptic Gateway's "each creature tapped this way"). `typeShareParts` unwraps it for the live read in `matchesFilter` and for `bindDynamicCompares`, which binds `each` as one `SharedTypes` entry per object. The `"convokers"` `ObjectSetRef` is the creatures that convoked the source's spell (`convokedBy`, or `enteredConvokedBy`), resolved in `Game`'s `objectSet` as each last existed in the stint that tapped (608.2h) and by `staticSetMembers` for a static — Everything Comes to Dust.
- **`characteristics.ts` / `combat/rules.ts`** — `sourceAmount` moved here from `goad.ts` (2026-11-13; `goad.ts` re-exports it), with `{ cardsInHand: "you" }` (its controller's hand) beside the source's power, toughness, mana value and a plain `countOf`. `restrictionsOf` reads the P/T-scoped combat words' scope with it, and `recordsOn` the records', as a static goad's already was — all as they are while attackers or blockers are declared: Ensnaring Bridge's `"cant-attack"` over creatures with power greater than your hand size. Anything else still fails closed.
- **`effects.ts`** — `bindDynamicCompares` leaves a reflexive ability's target compares with its source's own power, toughness or mana value unbound (2026-11-13, `readsSourceItself`): the reflexive ability has the creating one's source, so its targeting reads them as the targets are chosen and rechecked (rules 603.12, 608.2b) — they had been frozen as the creating ability resolved, so a recheck read a stale number (Auron, Venerated Guardian). Every other amount in its targets is still bound in the creating resolution.
- **`effects.ts`** — `choose-permanents`' `thenTogether` (2026-11-13) is one instruction over the chosen set, run even when it's empty: with nothing to choose, the effect case applies `then` with no targets (`decisionPending` before and after), and `Game`'s answer applies it however few were picked, first emitting `cards-chosen-from-zone` (`chosenOnly`) so the set is `thisWay: "chosen"` for `then` and the steps after (Nahiri's Resolve, Spry and Mighty). `flicker`'s slot list may name `"source"` (`fromSource` then guards it, rule 400.7 — Fortune, Loyal Steed's `["source", 0]`).
- **`card-names.ts`** — choosing a card name (rule 201.4), all pure. `nameableCardNames(registry, filter)`: every name the registry holds that a choice may name — not a token's (201.4), not a split card's whole (709.4a: one half or the other), each face of a double-faced card and each half its own — alphabetical, built once per registry and `CardNameFilter` (types, notTypes, nonbasic, notBasicLand; judged on the name's own definition, 201.4b–d). `namesOf`/`lastKnownNames`/`hasCardName`: the names an object has (both halves of a split card off the stack, none face down). `suggestedCardNames(state, registry, player, aim, filter, seen)`: the 8 names offered first by what the choice is for (`CardNameAim`: an opponent's commanders and shown cards, their permanents with non-mana activated abilities, the chooser's hand or library by count), the cards just looked at or revealed leading — computed in `legalActions`, since it reads the chooser's library. Raised by `chooseCardNameOnEnter` (`Game.askCardNameOnEnter`, with its look or reveal first) and the `choose-card-name` effect; the name lands on `GameObject.chosenNames` (cleared on a zone change, kept in `lastKnown`), which `CardFilter.chosenName` reads off a filter's `source`; inside a resolving `then`, `CHOSEN_CARD_NAME` is substituted (`CardFilter.hasName`). The catalogue is the registry's ~25,800 names, so it is never stored on `state.awaiting` (every seat's view would carry it): only the offer holds it.
- **`creature-types.ts`** — `CREATURE_TYPES`, every creature type (rule 205.3m) alphabetically — **generated**, from Scryfall's `catalog/creature-types` via `npm run gen:creature-types -w engine` (checked in; rerun when a set adds types).
